# 感知与感觉

> 本章收看、听、闻、尝、触的通用感知动词与感官状态词。**不**收已在 `01-04 认知与思维` 立条的基础动词（see, hear, listen, watch, observe, notice, perceive, sense, feel）；**不**收五感器官与生理名物（eye, ear, vision, touch, smell, taste, blind, deaf → `01-01`）；**不**收纯情绪感受（happy, anxious → `01-02`）；**不**收食材风味形容词（sweet, spicy → `04-01`）。

**look**
英 /lʊk/ 美 /lʊk/
v. 看；显得
n. 看；样子；外观
[义] To direct your eyes in a particular direction in order to see something; to seem or appear. 把视线转向某处以看见；或表「看起来」（look tired 看起来累，look like 看起来像）。与 see 相对：look 强调「看」的动作与方向，see 强调「看见」的结果。须接 at/into/through 等介词接宾语（look at the photo 看照片）。
[例] ① Look at the screen—the answer is highlighted in yellow. 看屏幕，答案用黄色标出来了。 ② She looked worried when she read the email. 她读邮件时显得很担心。
[搭] look at 看, look for 寻找, look like 看起来像, look after 照顾, look out 当心, take a look 看一看
[联] see 看见（偏结果）：watch 观看（偏持续）, glance 瞥一眼, stare 盯着看, ignore 不理睬, glimpse 瞥见, appear 显得
[记] look 作「看」时为不及物动词，须加 at 等；look + adj. 表「显得」。过去式 looked。look like + 名词/-ing 表「像……」。不可说 × look the photo，须 look at the photo

**glance**
英 /ɡlɑːns/ 美 /ɡlæns/
n. 一瞥
v. 瞥一眼；扫视
[义] To look quickly at someone or something; a quick look. 快速、短暂地看一眼（glance at the clock 瞥一眼表，glance through a report 浏览报告）。比 stare 短得多，常无意或顺带；比 look 更强调「快、短」。
[例] ① She glanced at her watch and realized she was late. 她瞥了一眼表，发现自己迟到了。 ② He glanced through the headlines before breakfast. 早饭前他扫了一眼标题。
[搭] glance at 瞥一眼, glance through 浏览, at a glance 一眼看出, steal a glance 偷瞄, exchange glances 交换眼色
[联] glimpse 瞥见（常带偶然）, peek 偷看, stare 盯视, scan 扫描式看, look 看, ignore 忽视
[记] glance at + 名词；glance through 后接文件、报告。at a glance 为固定短语表「一目了然」。名词 a glance / glances

**glimpse**
英 /ɡlɪmps/ 美 /ɡlɪmps/
n. 一瞥；短暂的看见
v. 瞥见；开始了解
[义] To see someone or something for a very short time or only partly; a brief sight. 极短暂、不完整地看见（glimpse a figure in the fog 雾中瞥见人影）；亦可喻「略知一二」（catch a glimpse of the truth 略窥真相）。比 glance 更强调「看见的内容」而非动作本身，常带偶然、不完整色彩。
[例] ① Through the window I caught a glimpse of the garden in bloom. 透过窗户我瞥见花园正开着花。 ② The documentary offers only a glimpse of life behind the scenes. 这部纪录片只让人略窥幕后生活的一角。
[搭] catch a glimpse of 瞥见, a brief glimpse 短暂一瞥, glimpse of hope 一线希望, glimpse through 透过……瞥见
[联] glance 瞥（偏动作）, peek 偷看, spot 发现, miss 没看见, flash 一闪, shadow 影子, fog 雾
[记] glimpse 作动词常接宾语（glimpse sb. through the door）；catch/get a glimpse of 极常用。与 glance 勿混：glance at 主动瞥；glimpse 常指「瞥见到了什么」

**stare**
英 /steə/ 美 /ster/
v. 盯着看；凝视
n. 凝视
[义] To look at someone or something for a long time with eyes wide open, often rudely or because of surprise. 睁大眼长时间看——常因惊讶，或显得无礼（stare at sb. 盯着某人看，stare in disbelief 难以置信地瞪着）。比 gaze 更常带「失礼、惊讶、空白」色彩；比 look 强调「久、直、不动」。
[例] ① It's rude to stare at people in public. 在公共场合盯着别人看是不礼貌的。 ② She stared at the bill in disbelief. 她难以置信地盯着账单。
[搭] stare at 盯着, stare in disbelief 难以置信地瞪着, blank stare 茫然的凝视, stare into space 发呆地盯着前方
[联] gaze 凝视（较中性或欣赏）, glance 瞥, peek 偷看, ignore 无视, glare 怒视, blink 眨眼
[记] stare at + 宾语；staring 进行时表「正盯着」。固定搭配 blank stare（茫然凝视）。stare 与 glare（带怒意）近义但 glare 更含敌意

**gaze**
英 /ɡeɪz/ 美 /ɡeɪz/
v. 凝视；注视
n. 凝视
[义] To look at someone or something for a long time, especially in admiration, thought, or wonder. 长时间注视——常带欣赏、沉思或惊奇（gaze at the stars 凝望星空，gaze out of the window 望向窗外）。比 stare 更中性或偏诗意；少含「无礼瞪视」意味。
[例] ① We stood on the cliff, gazing at the sunset over the ocean. 我们站在崖上，凝望海上日落。 ② He gazed out of the train window without saying a word. 他一声不吭地望向车窗外。
[搭] gaze at 凝视, gaze out of 望向窗外, steady gaze 坚定的目光, meet someone's gaze 与某人对视, lose oneself in a gaze 陷入凝视
[联] stare 盯着看（常带无礼或惊讶）, glance 瞥, admire 欣赏, contemplate 沉思, horizon 地平线, sunset 日落
[记] gaze at/out 为常见搭配；名词 a gaze。文学与描写中 gaze 极频。与 stare 相比，gaze 可用于浪漫或沉思场景而不显 rude

**peek**
英 /piːk/ 美 /piːk/
v. 偷看；窥视
n. 偷看；一瞥
[义] To look quickly and secretly at something, often through a narrow opening. 快速、偷偷地看——常从门缝、指缝、帘后（peek through the curtain 从帘后偷看，peek at a gift 偷看礼物）。比 peep 略口语；均含「不该看却看了」或「好奇偷看」。
[例] ① The children peeked through the keyhole before opening the door. 孩子们开门前从钥匙孔偷看。 ② Don't peek—I'm wrapping your present. 别偷看，我在包你的礼物呢。
[搭] peek at 偷看, peek through 透过……窥视, sneak a peek 偷偷看一眼, take a peek 瞧一眼（口语）
[联] peep 窥视（略正式）, glance 瞥, spy 窥探, hide 躲藏, curtain 窗帘, secret 秘密
[记] peek at/through 常见；美式口语 take a sneak peek 极频。与 peak（山峰、顶点）同音异义，拼写勿混

**peep**
英 /piːp/ 美 /piːp/
v. 窥视；微露
n. 窥视；唧唧声
[义] To look quickly and secretly, especially through a small space; to appear slightly. 从缝隙偷看（peep through the blinds 从百叶窗窥视）；亦指小鸟唧唧叫、或阳光微露（peep out 探出头）。与 peek 近义，peep 略偏书面或英式，亦用于「刚露出一点」。
[例] ① She peeped through the letterbox but saw no one. 她从信箱口窥视，却没看见人。 ② Crocuses were peeping up through the snow. 番红花从雪中探出头来。
[搭] peep at 偷看, peep through 透过……窥视, peep out 探出, not a peep 一声不吭（口语）
[联] peek 偷看, glance 瞥, chirp 唧唧叫, blind 百叶窗, crack 缝隙, emerge 露出
[记] peep at/through/out 为常见搭配。口语 not a peep 表「一声不出」。peep 与 peek 可互换场合多，peep 略旧式

**squint**
英 /skwɪnt/ 美 /skwɪnt/
v. 眯眼看；斜眼看
n. 眯眼；斜视
[义] To look with eyes partly closed, often because of bright light or poor sight; to look sideways. 因强光或视力问题半闭眼睛看（squint at the sun 眯眼看太阳）；亦指斜视（医学/名词义）。比 stare 多一层「眼睛挤成缝」的动作。
[例] ① She squinted at the tiny print on the medicine bottle. 她眯眼读药瓶上的小字。 ② Without my glasses I have to squint to read road signs. 不戴眼镜我得眯着眼才能读路牌。
[搭] squint at 眯眼看, squint in the sunlight 在阳光下眯眼, squint through 透过……眯眼看
[联] blink 眨眼, stare 盯视, glare 强光, sunlight 阳光, glasses 眼镜, tiny 微小的
[记] squint at + 宾语；名词 a squint 可指斜视（医学）。squint 与 blink 不同：blink 是眼睑开合，squint 是持续半闭眼看

**visible**
英 /ˈvɪzəbl/ 美 /ˈvɪzəbl/
adj. 可见的；明显的
[义] Able to be seen; obvious or noticeable. 能被眼睛看见的（visible from the hill 从山上看得见）；引申「明显的、可察觉的」（visible effort 看得见的努力，visible signs of progress 明显的进展迹象）。反义 invisible。
[例] ① The lighthouse is visible from miles away on a clear day. 晴天时几英里外都能看见那座灯塔。 ② There are visible signs that the economy is recovering. 有明显迹象表明经济正在复苏。
[源] 拉丁语 *visibilis*：vis-（看，同 vision）+ -ible（可……的）→ 可见的。
[族] visibility 能见度, visibly 明显地, invisible 看不见的
[搭] clearly visible 清晰可见, visible from 从……看得见, visible signs 明显迹象, visible to the naked eye 肉眼可见, remain visible 仍然可见
[联] invisible 看不见的, apparent 明显的, noticeable 值得注意的, hidden 隐藏的, obscure 模糊的, transparent 透明的
[记] visible to sb. / from somewhere；与 visual（视觉的）不同：visible 强调「能被看见」，visual 强调「与视觉有关」

**invisible**
英 /ɪnˈvɪzəbl/ 美 /ɪnˈvɪzəbl/
adj. 看不见的；无形的
[义] Unable to be seen; hidden or not noticed. 无法被看见的（invisible to the naked eye 肉眼看不见）；引申「无形的、未被注意的」（invisible workload 看不见的家务负担，feel invisible 觉得自己被忽视）。in-（不）+ visible。
[例] ① Air is invisible, but we feel it when the wind blows. 空气看不见，但刮风时我们能感觉到。 ② Much of her daily work remains invisible to her colleagues. 她每天大量工作同事根本看不到。
[源] in-（不）+ visible（可见的）→ 看不见的。
[族] invisibility 不可见性, invisibly 看不见地, visible 可见的
[搭] invisible to 对……不可见, invisible ink 隐形墨水, invisible hand 无形之手（经济）, feel invisible 觉得被忽视, invisible barrier 无形障碍
[联] visible 可见的, hidden 隐藏的, unnoticed 未被注意的, transparent 透明的, obscure 难辨的, ghost 鬼（喻 invisible presence）
[记] invisible to the naked eye 为固定搭配。比喻义 invisible 在性别、劳动研究中常见（invisible labor 隐形劳动）

**visibility**
英 /ˌvɪzəˈbɪləti/ 美 /ˌvɪzəˈbɪləti/
n. 能见度；可见性；知名度
[义] The distance you can see, especially in fog or rain; the state of being able to be seen; how well known something is. 气象上指能见度（poor visibility 能见度差）；亦指「被看见的状态」或比喻「知名度」（increase visibility 提高曝光度）。不可数居多。
[例] ① Fog reduced visibility to less than fifty metres. 大雾使能见度降到不足五十米。 ② The campaign helped improve the brand's visibility online. 这次活动提高了品牌在网络上的知名度。
[源] 源自 visible + -ity → 可见性。
[族] visible 可见的, visibly 明显地, invisible 看不见的
[搭] poor/good visibility 能见度差/好, low visibility 低能见度, visibility conditions 能见度条件, increase visibility 提高可见度/知名度, zero visibility 零能见度
[联] fog 雾, mist 薄雾, clear 晴朗, obscure 遮挡, prominent 突出的, exposure 曝光
[记] 不可数；航空与驾驶语境极常见。比喻义 visibility 在营销、职场（gain visibility 获得关注）中高频

**audible**
英 /ˈɔːdəbl/ 美 /ˈɔːdəbl/
adj. 听得见的
[义] Able to be heard. 声音大到能被耳朵接收到（barely audible 几乎听不见, audible sigh 听得见的叹息）。与 visible 成对——一个管看，一个管听。反义 inaudible。
[例] ① Her voice was barely audible over the noise of the traffic. 在车流噪音中她的声音几乎听不见。 ② There was an audible gasp when the winner was announced. 宣布获胜者时有人明显倒吸一口气。
[源] 拉丁语 *audire*（听）+ -ible（可……的）→ 可听见的。
[族] audio 音频, audience 听众, inaudible 听不见的, audibly 可听见地
[搭] barely audible 几乎听不见, clearly audible 清晰可闻, audible alarm 有声警报, audible breath/sigh 听得见的呼吸/叹息
[联] inaudible 听不见的, loud 大声的, silent 无声的, muffled 闷声的, hear 听见, sound 声音
[记] audible 多作表语或定语；audible gasp/sigh 为新闻与叙事常用搭配。与 audio（名词/形容词「音频的」）相关但 audible 强调「能被听到」

**inaudible**
英 /ɪnˈɔːdəbl/ 美 /ɪnˈɔːdəbl/
adj. 听不见的
[义] Unable to be heard. 声音太小或距离太远而听不到（inaudible whisper 听不见的低语）；或设备/录音问题导致（inaudible recording 听不清的录音）。in-（不）+ audible。
[例] ① He muttered something inaudible and walked away. 他咕哝了句听不清的话就走开了。 ② Parts of the old recording are almost inaudible. 旧录音的部分几乎听不清楚。
[搭] almost inaudible 几乎听不见, inaudible to 对……不可闻, inaudible whisper 听不见的低语
[联] audible 听得见的, silent 无声的, muffled 闷声的, whisper 低语, mutter 咕哝, deaf 聋的
[记] 与 invisible 结构对称。inaudible 常修饰 whisper, mutter, comment 等「本就很轻的发声」

**silent**
英 /ˈsaɪlənt/ 美 /ˈsaɪlənt/
adj. 沉默的；无声的；静悄悄的
[义] Making no sound; not speaking. 没有声音的（silent film 无声电影）；或人不说话（remain silent 保持沉默）；或地方静悄悄（the silent forest 寂静的森林）。比 quiet 更强调「完全无发声」；quiet 可仍有背景低响。
[例] ① The house was silent except for the ticking of the clock. 除了钟声，屋里一片寂静。 ② She remained silent during the entire meeting. 整个会议她一言不发。
[搭] remain silent 保持沉默, silent film 无声电影, silent treatment 冷暴力（不说话）, silent mode 静音模式, fall silent 静下来
[联] silence 寂静（名词）, quiet 安静的, loud 大声的, mute 哑的, still 静止的, noise 噪音
[记] silent 与 silence 成对；silent treatment 指「用沉默惩罚对方」。手机 silent mode 即静音

**silence**
英 /ˈsaɪləns/ 美 /ˈsaɪləns/
n. 寂静；沉默
v. 使安静；压制
[义] A complete lack of noise or speech; to make someone or something stop speaking or making sound. 完全没有声音或言语（a moment of silence 默哀片刻）；动词表「使安静、压制」（silence critics 压制批评）。可数/不可数均可。
[例] ① A long silence followed his question. 他的问题之后是长时间的沉默。 ② The announcement silenced the room instantly. 宣布一出，全场顿时鸦雀无声。
[搭] break the silence 打破沉默, moment of silence 默哀, in silence 安静地, silence sb. 使某人住嘴, deafening silence 震耳欲聋的寂静（喻）
[联] silent 沉默的, quiet 安静的, stillness 静止, pause 停顿, speech 言语, noise 噪音
[记] break the silence 极常用。deafening silence 为修辞：寂静到让人不适。动词 silence 较正式（silence the alarm 关掉警报）

**deafening**
英 /ˈdefnɪŋ/ 美 /ˈdefnɪŋ/
adj. 震耳欲聋的
[义] Extremely loud; so loud that you cannot hear anything else. 响到盖过一切、几乎让人听不见别的（deafening roar 震耳欲聋的轰鸣，deafening applause 掌声雷动）。现分形容词化，强调「响度极端」；与 deaf（聋的）同词根。
[例] ① The deafening roar of the waterfall made conversation impossible. 瀑布轰鸣震耳，根本没法说话。 ② There was deafening applause when she finished her speech. 她结束演讲时掌声雷动。
[源] deaf（聋）+ -en（使……）+ -ing → 使（人）聋的 → 震耳欲聋的。
[族] deaf 聋的, deafen 使聋, deafness 耳聋
[搭] deafening roar 震耳欲聋的轰鸣, deafening silence 令人窒息的寂静, deafening applause 掌声雷动, deafening noise 震耳噪音
[联] loud 大声的, silent 无声的, muffled 闷声的, roar 轰鸣, applause 掌声, ear 耳朵
[记] deafening silence 为反语修辞：静到反常。deafening 只作定语/表语，不说 × very deafening 时多用 deafening 本身已含「极响」

**muffled**
英 /ˈmʌfld/ 美 /ˈmʌfld/
adj. （声音）闷声的；压低的
[义] Not clear because something is in the way; quiet and hard to hear. 因隔阻而听不清、发闷（muffled voice 闷声闷气，muffled thud 沉闷的撞击声，muffled by a blanket 被毯子捂住而变闷）。常形容隔着门、墙、布料传来的声音。
[例] ① We could hear muffled voices from the next room. 我们能听见隔壁闷闷的说话声。 ② Her cough sounded muffled behind the mask. 口罩后面的咳嗽声听起来发闷。
[搭] muffled voice 闷声, muffled sound 闷响, muffled thud 沉闷撞击, muffled by 被……闷住
[联] muted 减弱的, faint 微弱的, loud 响亮的, clear 清晰的, whisper 低语, blanket 毯子
[记] muffled 为 muffle 的过去分词形容词化。与 muted（音量被调低）近义：muffled 偏「被物理阻隔」，muted 偏「被调弱色/音量」

**murmur**
英 /ˈmɜːmə/ 美 /ˈmɜːrmər/
n. 低语；咕哝；（心脏）杂音
v. 低语；发出轻柔持续的声音
[义] To say something in a quiet, soft voice; a low continuous sound. 轻声、持续地说（murmur approval 低声表示赞同）；或轻柔持续的自然声（murmur of the stream 溪流的潺潺声）。医学义 heart murmur 指心脏杂音。比 whisper 更柔、更连续。
[例] ① The audience murmured in agreement during the speech. 演讲过程中听众低声表示赞同。 ② We fell asleep to the murmur of the river outside. 我们在门外河水的潺潺声中入睡。
[搭] murmur of ……的低语/潺潺声, murmur approval 低声赞同, heart murmur 心脏杂音, murmur to oneself 自言自语
[联] whisper 耳语, mutter 咕哝（偏不满）, mumble 含糊说, hum 哼唱, stream 溪流, approval 赞同
[记] murmur 可及物/不及物；murmur of the crowd/stream 为常见搭配。heart murmur 体检常用，属医学通用词

**mutter**
英 /ˈmʌtə/ 美 /ˈmʌtər/
v. 咕哝；抱怨地说
n. 咕哝
[义] To speak quietly and unclearly, often in dissatisfaction or to yourself. 小声、含混地说——常带不满或自言自语（mutter under one's breath 低声抱怨，mutter about the price 抱怨价格）。比 murmur 更含「牢骚、含糊」色彩。
[例] ① He muttered something about being too busy and left. 他咕哝了句太忙之类的话就走了。 ② Stop muttering and speak up if you have a problem. 别嘟嘟囔囔的，有问题就大声说。
[搭] mutter under one's breath 低声咕哝, mutter about 抱怨, mutter to oneself 自言自语, indistinct mutter 含混的咕哝
[联] murmur 低语, mumble 含糊说, complain 抱怨, whisper 耳语, grumble 发牢骚, inaudible 听不见的
[记] mutter about/under 常见；mutter 常含轻微负面。与 mumble 近义：mumble 更强调「吐字不清」，mutter 更强调「低声抱怨」

**sniff**
英 /snɪf/ 美 /snɪf/
v. 嗅；吸鼻子；嗤之以鼻
n. 嗅；吸气声
[义] To breathe air in through your nose, especially to smell something or when you have a cold. 用鼻子吸气——为了闻（sniff the milk 闻闻牛奶坏没坏），或感冒抽鼻子（sniff loudly），或喻「不屑」（sniff at the offer 对提议嗤之以鼻）。
[例] ① She sniffed the sauce and added a little more garlic. 她闻了闻酱汁，又加了点蒜。 ② The dog sniffed around the base of the tree. 狗在树根处嗅来嗅去。
[搭] sniff at 嗅；对……嗤之以鼻, sniff out 嗅出；查明, sniff the air 嗅空气, have a sniff 闻一闻
[联] smell 闻/气味, scent 气味, whiff 一股气味, inhale 吸入, stench 恶臭, dog 狗
[记] sniff at 可表「嗤之以鼻」（sniff at danger 对危险满不在乎）。sniff out 喻「侦查出」（sniff out the truth）。规则变化 sniffed

**whiff**
英 /wɪf/ 美 /wɪf/
n. 一股（气、味）；一点点
v. 飘出微弱气味；挥击（空气）
[义] A smell that is only smelt for a brief time; a slight trace. 短暂闻到的一股气味（a whiff of perfume 一阵香水味，catch a whiff of smoke 闻到一点烟味）；亦喻「一丝迹象」（a whiff of scandal 一点丑闻风声）。
[例] ① As we entered the bakery, we caught a whiff of fresh bread. 进面包店时我们闻到一股新鲜面包香。 ② There was a whiff of panic in the markets last week. 上周市场有一丝恐慌气息。
[搭] a whiff of 一股……, catch a whiff 闻到一点, whiff of perfume/smoke 香水/烟味, get a whiff 闻到
[联] scent 气味, aroma 芳香, stench 恶臭, sniff 嗅, breeze 微风, trace 一丝
[记] a whiff of 为固定搭配；可数名词。比喻 a whiff of danger/scandal 指「略有一点苗头」。与 sniff 不同：whiff 是「飘来的那一阵」

**aroma**
英 /əˈrəʊmə/ 美 /əˈroʊmə/
n. 芳香；香气
[义] A pleasant, distinctive smell, especially from food, coffee, or spices. 宜人的、有特征的气味——多来自食物、咖啡、香料（aroma of coffee 咖啡香，rich aroma 浓郁香气）。比 smell 更正面、更讲究；比 scent 更偏「食物/饮品」的热气香。
[例] ① The aroma of fresh coffee filled the kitchen every morning. 每天早晨新鲜咖啡的香气弥漫厨房。 ② Spices give the dish a warm, rich aroma. 香料给这道菜带来温暖浓郁的香气。
[搭] aroma of coffee/cooking 咖啡/烹饪的香气, rich aroma 浓郁芳香, pleasant aroma 怡人的香气, aromatic 芳香的（形容词）
[联] scent 气味, fragrance 芬芳, smell 气味, odor 气味（中性/偏负）, pungent 刺鼻的, coffee 咖啡
[记] 可数/不可数均可；aroma 几乎总是褒义或中性 pleasant。形容词 aromatic（aromatic herbs 芳香草药）

**scent**
英 /sent/ 美 /sent/
n. 气味；香味；线索
v. 闻到；察觉
[义] A pleasant smell, especially a delicate one; a trail followed by smell; to detect by smell. 淡雅宜人的气味（scent of flowers 花香）；亦指动物追踪的「气味踪迹」或喻「蛛丝马迹」（pick up the scent 找到线索）；动词表嗅到、察觉（scent danger 觉察危险）。
[例] ① The scent of roses drifted through the open window. 玫瑰香从敞开的窗飘进来。 ② The dogs picked up the scent and ran into the woods. 狗嗅到踪迹跑进了树林。
[搭] scent of ……的气味, pick up the scent 嗅到踪迹/找到线索, lose the scent 失去线索, scented candle 香薰蜡烛
[联] aroma 芳香, fragrance 芬芳, odor 气味, whiff 一股气, perfume 香水, trail 踪迹
[记] scent 与 cent（分币）、sent（send 过去式）同音异义。pick up / lose the scent 为侦探/狩猎常用比喻。形容词 scented, scentless

**fragrance**
英 /ˈfreɪɡrəns/ 美 /ˈfreɪɡrəns/
n. 芬芳；香气；香水
[义] A sweet or pleasant smell; a perfume. 甜美、宜人的香气（fragrance of lilacs 丁香芬芳）；商品语境指「香水、香氛产品」（a new fragrance 新款香水）。比 scent 更强调「芬芳、精致」；比 aroma 更偏花卉、香水、护肤。
[例] ① The fragrance of jasmine hung in the evening air. 晚风中弥漫着茉莉的芬芳。 ② She launched her own fragrance line last year. 她去年推出了自己的香水系列。
[搭] fragrance of flowers 花香, light fragrance 淡香, fragrance industry 香氛业, floral fragrance 花香调
[联] scent 气味, aroma 芳香, perfume 香水, fragrant 芬芳的, odor 气味, flower 花
[记] 可数/不可数均可；fragrance 几乎总褒义。形容词 fragrant（fragrant garden 芬芳的花园）。commercial fragrance 指「香水产品」

**odor**
英 /ˈəʊdə/ 美 /ˈoʊdər/
n. 气味（尤指强烈或令人不快的）
> 英式拼写 odour /ˈəʊdə/
[义] A smell, especially an unpleasant or strong one. 气味——常偏中性到负面（chemical odor 化学味，body odor 体味，odor of decay 腐烂气息）。美式用 odor；英式 odour 同义。比 smell 更正式；比 stench 程度轻。
[例] ① There was a strange odor coming from the refrigerator. 冰箱里飘来一股怪味。 ② The odor of paint lingered in the room for days. 油漆味在房间里残留了好几天。
[搭] body odor 体味, chemical odor 化学味, unpleasant odor 难闻气味, odor of decay 腐烂气息, strong odor 强烈气味
[联] smell 气味（最通用）, scent 香味, stench 恶臭, reek 臭气, aroma 芳香, fragrance 芬芳
[记] 可数/不可数均可；odor 在美式英语更常见。body odor 固定搭配。与 aroma/fragrance 不同：odor 常需语境判断正负，默认略偏「有味/可能不好」

**pungent**
英 /ˈpʌndʒənt/ 美 /ˈpʌndʒənt/
adj. 刺鼻的；尖刻的
[义] Having a strong, sharp smell or taste; sharply expressive. 气味或味道强烈刺激（pungent smell of onions 洋葱的刺鼻味，pungent cheese 味道冲的奶酪）；引申文字「尖刻」（pungent comment 一针见血的评论）。
[例] ① A pungent smell of garlic filled the whole apartment. 大蒜的刺鼻味弥漫整间公寓。 ② The reviewer is known for her pungent wit. 这位评论家以尖刻机智著称。
[搭] pungent smell/odor 刺鼻气味, pungent taste 冲味, pungent comment/remark 尖刻评论, pungent aroma 强烈香气
[联] fragrant 芬芳的, musty 发霉的, spicy 辣的, sharp 尖锐的, acrid 辛辣呛人的, stench 恶臭
[记] pungent 可形容气味、味道、文字；比 strong 更强调「冲、刺」。pungency 名词

**fragrant**
英 /ˈfreɪɡrənt/ 美 /ˈfreɪɡrənt/
adj. 芬芳的；香的
[义] Having a pleasant, sweet smell. 有好闻、甜美气味的（fragrant flowers 芳香的花，fragrant rice 香米）。与 pungent 相对：fragrant 为pleasant，pungent 为 sharp/strong。
[例] ① Fragrant herbs were drying in the sun by the door. 门口阳光下晾着芳香的草药。 ② The garden is most fragrant in early summer. 这座花园初夏最为芬芳。
[搭] fragrant flowers 芳香的花, fragrant aroma 芬芳, fragrant rice/tea 香米/香茶
[联] fragrance 芬芳, aroma 芳香, scent 气味, pungent 刺鼻的, musty 霉味的, sweet 甜的
[记] fragrant 几乎总褒义；名词 fragrance。与 flagrant（臭名昭著的）拼写相近勿混

**musty**
英 /ˈmʌsti/ 美 /ˈmʌsti/
adj. 发霉的；陈腐的
[义] Smelling stale, damp, and old, as in a room that has been closed for a long time. 像久未通风的潮旧房间那样——霉味、陈腐（musty smell 霉味，musty basement 发潮发霉的地下室）。与 fragrant 相反。
[例] ① The attic had a musty smell after years without air. 阁楼多年不通风，一股霉味。 ② Old books often smell slightly musty. 旧书常略带霉味。
[搭] musty smell/odor 霉味, musty room 霉味的房间, musty air 陈腐的空气, musty basement 潮湿发霉的地下室
[联] stale 不新鲜的, damp 潮湿的, moldy 发霉的, fragrant 芬芳的, fresh 新鲜的, attic 阁楼
[记] musty 只作定语/表语；mustiness 名词。描述旧书、地下室、衣柜极常用

**reek**
英 /riːk/ 美 /riːk/
v. 散发恶臭；强烈带有（负面特征）
n. 臭气；烟雾（古/方）
[义] To smell very unpleasant; to be strongly suggestive of something bad. 散发恶臭（reek of smoke 满是烟臭）；喻「充满（坏事）」（reek of corruption 充满腐败气息）。比 smell bad 更强烈、更书面；与 stench 近义但 reek 多作动词。
[例] ① The kitchen reeked of burnt oil after the accident. 出事之后厨房满是糊油的臭气。 ② The whole scheme reeks of dishonesty. 整个方案透着一股不诚实。
[搭] reek of 充满……（气味或坏意味）, reek of smoke/sweat 烟/汗臭熏天, reek of corruption 充满腐败气息
[联] stench 恶臭, odor 气味, smell 闻, pungent 刺鼻的, corruption 腐败, smoke 烟
[记] reek of 为固定搭配——字面（气味）与比喻（腐败、谎言）均常见。名词 reek 较旧，现代多用动词

**stench**
英 /stentʃ/ 美 /stentʃ/
n. 恶臭
v. 发恶臭（罕）
[义] A strong, unpleasant smell. 极强烈、令人作呕的臭气（stench of rotting garbage 腐烂垃圾的恶臭）。比 odor 更重；比 reek 更偏名词、更强调「臭不可闻」。
[例] ① The stench from the river was unbearable in summer. 夏天河里的恶臭让人受不了。 ② A stench of decay hung over the abandoned building. 废弃大楼上弥漫着腐烂的臭气。
[搭] stench of decay/garbage 腐烂/垃圾的恶臭, unbearable stench 难以忍受的臭气, stench bomb 臭弹（新闻）
[联] reek 发臭, odor 气味, smell 气味, foul 恶臭的, rot 腐烂, garbage 垃圾
[记] 可数/不可数均可；stench 几乎总指「极臭」。the stench of 为常见框架

**detect**
英 /dɪˈtekt/ 美 /dɪˈtekt/
v. 察觉；探测；发现
[义] To notice or discover something that is not easy to see, hear, etc. 察觉、发现不易感知的存在（detect a leak 发现泄漏，detect a change 察觉变化）。比 notice 更偏「通过仪器、仔细辨别或推理」；科技/医学/安全语境极常见。
[例] ① The sensor can detect movement in the dark. 传感器能在黑暗中探测移动。 ② I detected a note of sarcasm in his voice. 我听出他话里带点讽刺。
[源] 拉丁语 *detegere*：de-（去掉）+ *tegere*（覆盖）→ 揭开覆盖 → 发现。
[族] detection 探测, detective 侦探, detector 探测器, undetected 未被发现
[搭] detect a change/signal 察觉变化/信号, detect fraud 发现欺诈, hard to detect 难以察觉, detect early 早期发现
[联] notice 注意到, discern 辨明, discover 发现, sense 感觉到, overlook 忽视, sensor 传感器
[记] detect 较正式；日常小事 notice/see 更自然。detective 为「侦探」；detector 为「探测器」（smoke detector 烟感）

**discern**
英 /dɪˈsɜːn/ 美 /dɪˈsɜːrn/
v. 辨明；识别；看出
[义] To recognize or understand something by thinking carefully; to see or hear something with difficulty. 经仔细辨别后认出、理解（discern a pattern 辨明规律，discern the truth 辨明真相）；或费力看清/听清（discern a figure in the mist 雾中辨出人影）。比 detect 更强调「分辨、判断力」；偏正式。
[例] ① It can be hard to discern fact from opinion online. 网上很难把事实和观点分辨清楚。 ② Through the rain she could barely discern the outline of the house. 雨中她几乎看不清房子的轮廓。
[源] 拉丁语 *discernere*：dis-（分开）+ *cernere*（筛、辨）→ 分开筛辨 → 辨明。
[族] discernible 可辨的, discerning 有眼光的, indiscernible 难以分辨的
[搭] discern between 区分, discern a difference/pattern 辨明差异/规律, hard to discern 难以辨明, discerning eye 有辨识力的眼光
[联] detect 察觉, distinguish 区分, perceive 感知, recognize 认出, vague 模糊的, clarity 清晰
[记] discern 后接宾语或 between A and B；discerning 作形容词表「有鉴赏力的」（discerning reader 有眼光的读者）

**apparent**
英 /əˈpærənt/ 美 /əˈpærənt/
adj. 明显的；表面上的
[义] Easy to see or understand; seeming real or true but perhaps not so. 显而易见（for no apparent reason 无缘无故，apparent success 明显的成功）；或「表面上如此、未必属实」（the apparent calm 表面的平静，heir apparent 法定继承人——表面/法律上的）。两义并存，依语境区分。
[例] ① For no apparent reason, the computer shut down. 电脑无缘无故关机了。 ② There was an apparent contradiction between the two reports. 两份报告表面上有矛盾。
[源] 拉丁语 *apparere*：ad-（向）+ *parere*（出现）→ 出现在眼前 → 明显的。
[族] apparently 显然, appear 出现, appearance 外表, transparent 透明的
[搭] for no apparent reason 无缘无故, apparent contradiction 表面矛盾, become apparent 变得明显, heir apparent 法定继承人
[联] obvious 明显的, evident 显然的, seeming 表面的, visible 可见的, hidden 隐藏的, clear 清楚的
[记] apparently 副词表「显然、看来」；apparent 第二义「表面上」读上下文（apparent friend 未必是真朋友）。heir apparent 为固定法律/新闻用语

**sensation**
英 /senˈseɪʃn/ 美 /senˈseɪʃn/
n. 感觉；轰动
[义] A physical feeling; an event that causes great public excitement. 身体感官上的感觉（a tingling sensation 刺痛感，loss of sensation 失去知觉）；或新闻/口语「轰动」（cause a sensation 引起轰动，overnight sensation 一夜成名）。与 sense 同源但 sensation 更偏「具体一次感受」或「爆炸性事件」。
[例] ① He felt a burning sensation in his chest after running. 跑完后他感到胸口烧灼感。 ② The young singer became an overnight sensation. 这位年轻歌手一夜爆红。
[源] 拉丁语 *sensatio*，来自 *sentire*（感觉）→ 感官触发的感受。
[族] sense 感觉, sensory 感官的, sensational 轰动的, sensationalism 煽情
[搭] tingling/burning sensation 刺痛/烧灼感, loss of sensation 失去知觉, cause a sensation 引起轰动, overnight sensation 一夜成名
[联] feeling 感觉, sense 感觉到, perception 感知, numbness 麻木, excitement 轰动, tingle 刺痛
[记] 可数/不可数均可。医学/日常 sensation 指「体感」；媒体 sensation 指「轰动人物/事件」。sensational 别义「耸人听闻的」

**perceptible**
英 /pəˈseptəbl/ 美 /pərˈseptəbl/
adj. 可察觉的；看得出的
[义] Able to be noticed or felt, though often only just. 能被感官或注意力察觉——常指「勉强可感」（a perceptible change 可察觉的变化，perceptible tremor 轻微可见的颤抖）。与 perceive 同源；反义 imperceptible。
[例] ① There was a perceptible improvement in her mood after the call. 那通电话后她的情绪有明显（可察觉的）好转。 ② The temperature drop was barely perceptible. 气温下降几乎难以察觉。
[源] perceive（感知）+ -ible（可……的）→ 可感知的。
[族] perceive 感知, perception 感知, imperceptible 难以察觉的, perceptibly 可察觉地
[搭] perceptible change/difference 可察觉的变化/差异, barely perceptible 几乎难以察觉, perceptible to 对……可感知
[联] imperceptible 难以察觉的, noticeable 明显的, subtle 细微的, visible 可见的, detect 察觉, slight 轻微的
[记] perceptible 多修饰 change, difference, shift, tremor。与 noticeable 比：perceptible 可指「很轻微但仍可感」

**imperceptible**
英 /ˌɪmpəˈseptəbl/ 美 /ˌɪmpərˈseptəbl/
adj. 难以察觉的；极细微的
[义] Impossible or very hard to notice or feel. 几乎感觉不到（imperceptible movement 难以察觉的移动，imperceptible shift 细微转变）。im-（不）+ perceptible。
[例] ① The changes were imperceptible to anyone who saw her every day. 对每天见她的人来说，这些变化几乎看不出来。 ② He nodded with an almost imperceptible smile. 他点了点头，嘴角掠过一丝几乎看不出的笑。
[搭] almost imperceptible 几乎难以察觉, imperceptible change/shift 细微变化, imperceptible to 对……不可感知
[联] perceptible 可察觉的, subtle 细微的, invisible 看不见的, inaudible 听不见的, slight 轻微的, gradual 逐渐的
[记] imperceptible 常修饰 smile, nod, shift, change——强调「细到几乎注意不到」。与 invisible 不同：imperceptible 可指「有变化但太难察觉」

**tingle**
英 /ˈtɪŋɡl/ 美 /ˈtɪŋɡl/
v. 感到刺痛/发麻
n. 刺痛感；兴奋感
[义] To feel a slight stinging or prickling, often in the skin; a thrill of excitement. 皮肤轻微刺、麻、痒（My fingers tingle with cold 手指冻麻了）；亦可喻兴奋（a tingle of excitement 一阵兴奋）。比 numb 轻——仍有感觉但是刺/麻。
[例] ① My toes were tingling after sitting on them for an hour. 坐压了一小时脚趾，开始发麻。 ② She felt a tingle of excitement before opening the letter. 拆信前她感到一阵兴奋。
[搭] tingle with 因……而刺/麻/兴奋, a tingle of excitement 一阵兴奋, fingers tingle 手指发麻
[联] numb 麻木的, itch 痒, prickle 刺痒, sting 刺痛, chill 寒意, excitement 兴奋
[记] tingle with cold/excitement 常见；tingling 形容词/现在分词。与 tickle（挠痒）不同：tingle 是神经性的刺麻

**itch**
英 /ɪtʃ/ 美 /ɪtʃ/
n. 痒；渴望
v. 发痒；渴望
[义] An uncomfortable feeling on the skin that makes you want to scratch; to have this feeling; to want to do something very much. 皮肤发痒（scratch an itch 挠痒）；喻强烈想做（itch to tell 憋不住想说，have an itch to travel 很想去旅行）。
[例] ① Mosquito bites itch for days if you keep scratching them. 蚊子块要是老挠，能痒好几天。 ② He was itching to ask what was in the box. 他憋不住想问盒子里是什么。
[搭] scratch an itch 挠痒, itch to do 渴望做, have an itch for 渴望, itchy skin 发痒的皮肤
[联] scratch 挠, tingle 刺麻, rash 皮疹, sting 刺痛, urge 冲动, mosquito 蚊子
[记] itch to do / for sth. 为口语常用。形容词 itchy（itchy sweater 扎人的毛衣）见属性章；本章取动词/名词「痒」义

**tangible**
英 /ˈtændʒəbl/ 美 /ˈtændʒəbl/
adj. 可触摸的；切实的
[义] Able to be touched or physically felt; clear and real enough to be recognized. 可触知的（tangible object 实物）；引申「切实的、可衡量的」（tangible benefits 实在的好处，tangible results 看得见的成果）。反义 intangible。
[例] ① The policy produced tangible improvements in public transport. 这项政策让公共交通有了实实在在的改善。 ② There was no tangible evidence linking him to the crime. 没有实物证据把他和犯罪联系起来。
[源] 拉丁语 *tangere*（触摸）+ -ible → 可触摸的。
[族] intangible 无形的, tangibly 可触知地, touch 触摸
[搭] tangible benefits/results 实在的好处/成果, tangible evidence 实物证据, tangible asset 有形资产
[联] intangible 无形的, concrete 具体的, abstract 抽象的, visible 可见的, touch 触摸, evidence 证据
[记] tangible 在商业/政策语境极频（tangible outcomes）。与 touch 同 *tang-* 词根

**intangible**
英 /ɪnˈtændʒəbl/ 美 /ɪnˈtændʒəbl/
adj. 无形的；难以捉摸的
n. 无形资产（常复数）
[义] Unable to be touched; not having physical presence. 无法触摸、无实体（intangible assets 无形资产，intangible quality 难以名状的品质）。in-（不）+ tangible。
[例] ① Trust is an intangible but essential part of teamwork. 信任无形，却是团队合作必不可少的一部分。 ② The company invests heavily in intangibles such as brand and reputation. 公司在品牌和声誉等无形资产上投入很大。
[搭] intangible assets 无形资产, intangible quality/benefit 无形的品质/好处, intangible cultural heritage 非物质文化遗产
[联] tangible 有形的, abstract 抽象的, invisible 看不见的, trust 信任, brand 品牌, heritage 遗产
[记] 可数复数 intangibles 指「无形资产」。UNESCO intangible cultural heritage 为固定术语

**visual**
英 /ˈvɪʒuəl/ 美 /ˈvɪʒuəl/
adj. 视觉的；看得见的
n. 视觉资料（常复数）
[义] Relating to seeing or sight; used to help people understand through images. 与「看」有关的（visual arts 视觉艺术，visual memory 图像记忆）；名词 visuals 指图表、视频等「视觉材料」。与 visible 不同：visual 强调「感官通道/媒介」，visible 强调「能被看见」。
[例] ① The app offers visual reminders as well as text alerts. 这款应用除了文字提醒还有可视化提醒。 ② The presentation relied heavily on visuals rather than dense slides of text. 演示主要靠视觉材料，而不是密密麻麻的文字幻灯片。
[源] 拉丁语 *visualis*：vis-（看，同 vision）→ 视觉的。
[族] vision 视力, visible 可见的, visualize 想象, visually 视觉上
[搭] visual aid 视觉辅助, visual memory 图像记忆, visual arts 视觉艺术, visual effect 视觉效果, rely on visuals 依靠视觉材料
[联] visible 可见的, auditory 听觉的, tactile 触觉的, image 图像, sight 视力, presentation 演示
[记] visual vs. visible：visual learner 靠「看」学；visible from here 从这儿「看得见」。visuals 在演讲、视频制作中指「画面素材」

**auditory**
英 /ˈɔːdətri/ 美 /ˈɔːdətɔːri/
adj. 听觉的
[义] Relating to the sense of hearing. 与听觉通道有关的（auditory nerve 听神经，auditory learning 听学习型）。与 visual 成对。较 ear 更正式、偏科学/教育语境。
[例] ① Some children respond better to auditory instructions than written ones. 有些孩子听口头指令比读文字反应更好。 ② The auditory system processes sound from both ears. 听觉系统处理来自双耳的声音。
[源] 拉丁语 *auditorius*：audire（听）→ 听觉的。
[族] audio 音频, audience 听众, audible 听得见的, audition 试听
[搭] auditory nerve 听神经, auditory learning 听觉学习, auditory processing 听觉处理, auditory signal 听觉信号
[联] visual 视觉的, acoustic 声学的, hearing 听力, sound 声音, ear 耳朵, listen 听
[记] auditory 在心理学/教育学常见（auditory learner 听觉型学习者）。与 acoustic 近义：acoustic 偏「声波/声学」，auditory 偏「感官/神经」

**olfactory**
英 /ɒlˈfæktri/ 美 /ɑːlˈfæktəri/
adj. 嗅觉的
[义] Relating to the sense of smell. 与嗅觉有关的（olfactory bulb 嗅球，olfactory receptors 嗅觉受体）。正式、科学/医学用语；日常说 smell 相关即可，olfactory 见于科普、论文、品酒/香水专业文本。
[例] ① Dogs have a highly developed olfactory system. 狗的嗅觉系统非常发达。 ② The wine's olfactory profile includes notes of berry and oak. 这款酒的嗅觉特征包括浆果与橡木调。
[源] 拉丁语 *olfacere*：*olfact-*（闻）→ 嗅觉的。
[族] olfaction 嗅觉（正式）
[搭] olfactory bulb/receptors 嗅球/嗅觉受体, olfactory system 嗅觉系统, olfactory sense 嗅觉, olfactory nerve 嗅神经
[联] smell 闻, scent 气味, aroma 芳香, nasal 鼻的, gustatory 味觉的, sensory 感官的
[记] olfactory 几乎只作定语。与 smell 日常词并存：科普/医学用 olfactory，日常用 sense of smell

**gustatory**
英 /ˈɡʌstətri/ 美 /ˈɡʌstətɔːri/
adj. 味觉的
[义] Relating to the sense of taste. 与味觉有关的（gustatory cells 味觉细胞，gustatory pleasure 味觉享受）。正式用语；日常多直接说 taste。
[例] ① Gustatory receptors on the tongue detect sweet and salty flavors. 舌上的味觉受体分辨甜咸。 ② The dish offers little gustatory interest. 这道菜没什么味觉上的亮点。
[源] 拉丁语 *gustare*（品尝）→ 味觉的。
[族] gustation 味觉（正式）
[搭] gustatory cells/receptors 味觉细胞/受体, gustatory pleasure 味觉享受, gustatory system 味觉系统
[联] taste 尝, flavor 风味, palate 味觉, olfactory 嗅觉的, sensory 感官的, tongue 舌
[记] gustatory 与 gustation 多见于生理/美食评论正式文本。日常写作 prefer taste-related 简单表达

**tactile**
英 /ˈtæktaɪl/ 美 /ˈtætl/
adj. 触觉的；有质感的
[义] Relating to the sense of touch; perceptible by touch. 与触觉有关的（tactile sensation 触觉，tactile learner 动手学习型）；亦指材质「摸得着、有手感」（tactile keyboard 有按键反馈的键盘）。
[例] ① Blind readers rely on tactile dots on the page. 盲读者依靠页上的凸点触觉阅读。 ② The phone's tactile buttons feel more satisfying than a flat screen. 手机实体键的手感比纯平屏幕更踏实。
[源] 拉丁语 *tangere*（触摸，同 tangible）→ 触觉的。
[族] tangible 可触摸的, touch 触摸, tactility 触感
[搭] tactile sensation 触觉, tactile feedback 触觉反馈, tactile learner 触觉/动手型学习者, tactile dots 凸点（盲文）
[联] touch 触摸, texture 质地, tangible 切实的, sensory 感官的, visual 视觉的, blind 盲的
[记] tactile 与 tangible 同 *tang-* 根：tactile 偏「感官/学习风格」，tangible 偏「可摸/可衡量」。tactile feedback 在游戏、键盘中常见

**seem**
英/美 /siːm/
v. 似乎，好像
[义] To give the impression of being or doing something; to appear. 给人某种印象、看起来如何（seem happy 好像很高兴，seem to be 似乎是，it seems that 似乎……）。与 appear 近义；seem 更偏「主观印象、判断」，常接形容词、不定式或 that 从句。不用于进行时表「此刻显得」的日常结构。
[例] ① The problem seems more complicated than we first thought. 这问题似乎比我们最初想的更复杂。 ② It seems that they have already made a decision. 看来他们已经做出决定了。
[搭] seem to be/do 似乎是, it seems that 似乎, seem like 好像, seem happy/tired 显得高兴/累, seem unlikely 似乎不太可能
[联] appear 显得, look 看起来, sound 听起来, feel 感觉, prove 证明是, turn out 结果是
[记] seem + adj. / to do / that 从句；不可说 × is seeming happy（进行时极少）。seem 与 look 比：look 更视觉，seem 更泛「整体印象」

**appear**
英/美 /əˈpɪə/
v. 出现；显得
[义] To come into sight; to give a particular impression. 进入视野「出现」（appear on stage 登台）；或「显得、似乎」（appear confident 显得自信，appear to be 似乎是）。比 seem 更可用于「物理出现」；表「显得」时二者常可互换。
[例] ① A rainbow appeared after the storm passed. 暴风雨过后出现了一道彩虹。 ② She appears to have changed her mind overnight. 她似乎一夜之间改变了主意。
[搭] appear to be/do 似乎是, appear on 出现在, suddenly appear 突然出现, appear confident 显得自信, it appears that 看来
[联] seem 似乎, emerge 浮现, look 看起来, vanish 消失, visible 可见的, disappear 消失
[记] appear 可表「露面/出版/出庭」（appear in court 出庭）。appearance 名词：外表或出现。appear 与 disappear 成对

**view**
英 /vjuː/ 美 /vjuː/
n. 视野；看法；景色
v. 观看；认为
[义] What you can see from a place; an opinion; to look at or consider. 从某处望出去所见（a beautiful view 美景）；观点（in my view 依我看）；动词「观看、审视」（view the exhibition 参观展览，view sth. as 把……视为）。比 look 更正式，名词义极常见。
[例] ① The hotel room has a view of the mountains. 酒店房间能看到山景。 ② Experts view the policy as a step in the right direction. 专家把这项政策视为朝正确方向迈出的一步。
[搭] in my view 依我看, point of view 观点, view of 对……的看法/……的景色, view as 把……视为, with a view to 为了
[联] sight 视力/景象, scene 场景, opinion 意见, perspective 视角, watch 观看, landscape 景观
[记] view 可数（a view）；复数 views。with a view to + -ing 表「为了（做某事）」。viewpoint 同根，偏「立场」

**survey**
英 /ˈsɜːveɪ/ 美 /ˈsɜːrveɪ/
n. 调查；概览
v. 审视；测量；调查
[义] To look carefully at the whole of something; a general study or set of questions. 环顾、审视整体（survey the damage 查看损失）；或系统调查（conduct a survey 做调查）。动词重音在第二音节 /veɪ/；名词重音在第一音节 /ˈsɜːveɪ/。
[例] ① He surveyed the room before deciding where to sit. 他扫视房间后才决定坐哪儿。 ② The company surveyed customers about the new design. 公司就新设计向客户做了调查。
[搭] survey the area 查看区域, conduct a survey 做调查, customer survey 客户调查, land survey 土地测量, quick survey 快速环顾
[联] inspect 检查, examine 检查, scan 扫描, review 回顾, study 研究, glance 瞥
[记] survey 动词与名词重音不同。surveyor 测量员；survey 作「问卷调査」极常见。survey the scene 为新闻/警察用语

**inspect**
英 /ɪnˈspekt/ 美 /ɪnˈspekt/
v. 检查；视察
[义] To look at something carefully to check that it is correct, safe, or of good quality. 仔细查看以确认状况、安全或质量（inspect the engine 检查引擎，health inspector 卫生检查员）。比 examine 更偏「按标准验收、官方视察」；比 look at 更系统。
[例] ① The mechanic inspected the brakes before the long trip. 长途出发前机师检查了刹车。 ② Officials inspect restaurants for hygiene violations. 官员检查餐厅是否有卫生违规。
[源] 拉丁语 *inspicere*：in-（向内）+ *specere*（看）→ 向内看 → 检查。
[族] inspection 检查, inspector 检查员, spectacle 景象
[搭] inspect carefully 仔细检查, inspect for 检查是否……, routine inspection 例行检查, inspect the premises 视察场所
[联] examine 检查, check 核对, review 审查, observe 观察, audit 审计, quality 质量
[记] inspect 较正式；日常小事用 check 更自然。inspector 可指「Inspector」警衔（英式）。spect- 词根表「看」

**examine**
英 /ɪɡˈzæmɪn/ 美 /ɪɡˈzæmɪn/
v. 检查；审查；测验
[义] To look at or consider something carefully; to test someone's knowledge. 仔细查看或研究（examine the evidence 审查证据，examine a patient 检查病人）；或考试测验（examine students 考学生）。比 inspect 更宽——可指学术分析、医学触诊。
[例] ① The doctor examined her knee and ordered an X-ray. 医生检查了她的膝盖并开了 X 光。 ② Researchers examined data from the last ten years. 研究人员分析了过去十年的数据。
[源] 拉丁语 *examinare*：*ex-*（出）+ *agmen*（队列）→ 逐出称量 → 检验。
[族] examination 检查/考试, exam 考试, examiner 考官
[搭] examine closely 仔细检查, examine the evidence 审查证据, examine a patient 检查病人, examine whether 审查是否
[联] inspect 视察, investigate 调查, analyze 分析, check 核对, study 研究, test 测试
[记] examine 不可用于「快速看一眼」；须含「仔细、系统」意味。examination 可数/不可数；口语 exam 指「考试」

**skim**
英 /skɪm/ 美 /skɪm/
v. 浏览；略读；掠过
[义] To read quickly for the main points; to move lightly over a surface. 快速阅读抓要点（skim a report 浏览报告）；或轻掠过表面（skim stones on water 打水漂，skim the cream 撇去奶油）。视觉/阅读上的「扫过」，不是逐字细读。
[例] ① I only had time to skim the report before the meeting. 开会前我只来得及浏览报告。 ② Birds skimmed low over the lake at dusk. 黄昏时鸟低低掠过湖面。
[搭] skim through 浏览, skim over 掠过, skim the surface 浮于表面（喻）, skim a report 略读报告
[联] scan 扫描式看, glance 瞥, read 读, skip 跳过, browse 浏览, dive 深入
[记] skim through/over 常见；skim the surface 喻「只触及表面」。skim milk 脱脂奶（撇去奶油）。与 scan 比：skim 更「快、粗」

**glare**
英 /ɡleə/ 美 /ɡler/
n. 刺眼的强光；怒视
v. 发出刺眼光；怒视
[义] A strong, bright, unpleasant light; an angry stare. 刺眼的强光（glare of the sun 日光刺眼，anti-glare screen 防眩屏）；或怒目而视（glare at sb. 瞪某人）。比 shine 更含「令人不适、太亮」。
[例] ① The glare from the snow made it hard to see the path. 雪地反光刺眼，很难看清路。 ② She glared at him when he interrupted her again. 他再次打断她时，她怒视着他。
[搭] glare at 怒视, glare of the sun 日光刺眼, anti-glare 防眩, harsh glare 刺眼的强光
[联] stare 盯视, shine 发光, dazzle 炫目, blink 眨眼, sunlight 阳光, anger 愤怒
[记] glare at sb. 表怒视；glare 作名词表强光。glaring error 为另一义「明显的错误」（ glaring 形容词）

**dazzle**
英 /ˈdæzl/ 美 /ˈdæzl/
v. 使目眩；使倾倒
n. 耀眼的光彩
[义] To blind someone briefly with too much light; to impress greatly. 强光使人暂时睁不开眼（dazzled by headlights 被车灯晃花）；喻「才华/魅力让人倾倒」（dazzle the audience 令观众倾倒）。比 glare 更强调「瞬间闪、令人赞叹」。
[例] ① The stage lights dazzled her for a moment as she walked out. 她出场时舞台灯光晃得她一时睁不开眼。 ② The young pianist dazzled critics with her debut performance. 这位年轻钢琴家首秀令评论家倾倒。
[搭] dazzle sb. with 用……使某人倾倒, dazzled by 被……晃眼/倾倒, dazzle the audience 令观众倾倒
[联] glare 刺眼, shine 闪耀, blind 致盲, impress 打动, sparkle 闪烁, spotlight 聚光灯
[记] dazzled 常作表语；dazzling 形容词（dazzling performance 精彩绝伦的演出）。dazzle 与 daze（发懵）近形

**flicker**
英 /ˈflɪkə/ 美 /ˈflɪkər/
v. 闪烁；摇曳
n. 闪烁
[义] To shine unsteadily; to appear briefly and intermittently. 光不稳定地闪动（candle flickered 烛光摇曳，screen flickering 屏幕闪烁）；亦指情感、希望「一闪而过」（a flicker of hope 一线希望）。
[例] ① The candle flickered in the draft and nearly went out. 烛光在穿堂风里摇曳，差点熄灭。 ② There was a flicker of recognition in her eyes. 她眼里闪过一丝认出的神色。
[搭] flicker of hope 一线希望, lights flicker 灯光闪烁, screen flickering 屏幕闪烁, flicker on and off 忽明忽灭
[联] flash 闪光, glow 微光, blink 眨眼, flame 火焰, unstable 不稳定的, spark 火花
[记] flicker 作名词可数（a flicker）。flickering light 为常见搭配。技术语境 screen flicker 指「屏幕闪烁」

**glow**
英 /ɡləʊ/ 美 /ɡloʊ/
n. 微光；红光；喜悦
v. 发出柔和的光；容光焕发
[义] A soft, steady light without flame; to produce such light; to look warm and healthy. 无火焰的柔和稳定光（glow of the embers 余烬微光）；或皮肤/表情「泛红、容光焕发」（glow with pride 因自豪而满面红光）。比 shine 更柔、更暖。
[例] ① The city glowed at night when seen from the hill. 从山上看，城市在夜里泛着柔光。 ② Her face glowed after the long run in the cold air. 在冷空气里长跑后，她双颊通红。
[搭] glow of ……的微光, glow with pride/health 因自豪/健康而容光焕发, warm glow 暖意, embers glow 余烬发光
[联] shine 发光, gleam 微光, flicker 闪烁, blush 脸红, warmth 温暖, light 光
[记] glow 不及物动词；glowing review 喻「热情洋溢的好评」。glow stick 荧光棒。与 blow 不同词

**shine**
英 /ʃaɪn/ 美 /ʃaɪn/
v. 照耀；发光；出色
n. 光泽
[义] To produce or reflect bright light; to do something very well. 发出或反射亮光（sun shines 阳光照耀，shine shoes 擦鞋）；喻「表现出色」（shine in math 数学很出色）。过去式/分词 shone /ʃɒn/（美式亦 shined 表「擦亮」）。
[例] ① The sun shone through the clouds after the rain. 雨后阳光穿过云层照下来。 ② She really shone in the final interview. 她在最后一轮面试中表现特别出色。
[搭] shine through 显露出来, shine at/in 在……方面出色, rise and shine 起床啦（口语）, shine a light 照光
[联] glow 微光, gleam 闪烁, sparkle 闪耀, bright 明亮的, polish 擦亮, excel 出色
[记] 不规则 shone /ʃɒn/；表「擦亮鞋」时美式用 shined。shine through 喻「（品质）显露」。sunshine 名词

**sparkle**
英 /ˈspɑːkl/ 美 /ˈspɑːrkl/
v. 闪烁；闪耀
n. 火花；光彩
[义] To shine brightly with flashes of light; to be lively and animated. 光点不断闪动（sparkling water 波光粼粼，eyes sparkling 眼睛闪闪发亮）；或气氛、性格「活泼生动」（sparkle with wit 妙语连珠）。
[例] ① The diamond ring sparkled under the shop lights. 钻戒在店灯下闪闪发亮。 ② The conversation sparkled with laughter and stories. 谈话里笑声不断、故事精彩。
[搭] sparkle with 闪烁着……, sparkling water 气泡水/波光, eyes sparkle 眼睛发亮, add sparkle 增添光彩
[联] glitter 闪耀, gleam 微光, shine 发光, twinkle 闪烁, lively 活泼的, diamond 钻石
[记] sparkling 形容词；sparkling wine 起泡酒。sparkle 与 spark（火花）同根。eyes sparkling 为描写常用

**twinkle**
英 /ˈtwɪŋkl/ 美 /ˈtwɪŋkl/
v. 闪烁；闪亮
n. 闪烁
[义] To shine with a light that changes quickly from bright to faint, especially stars or eyes. 光在亮暗间快速变化——尤指星星或眼睛（stars twinkle 星星闪烁，twinkling eyes 笑眼）。比 sparkle 更「一明一暗、轻柔」。
[例] ① We lay on the grass watching the stars twinkle above us. 我们躺在草地上看头顶星星闪烁。 ② His eyes twinkled when he told the joke. 讲笑话时他眼睛亮晶晶的。
[搭] twinkle in one's eye 眼里的笑意, stars twinkle 星星闪烁, twinkling lights 闪烁的灯
[联] sparkle 闪耀, flicker 摇曳, gleam 微光, star 星, wink 眨眼, smile 微笑
[记] twinkle in one's eye 固定搭配，表「调皮/会心的一丝笑意」。Twinkle, Twinkle, Little Star 儿歌。twinkle 与 wrinkle（皱纹）勿混

**obvious**
英 /ˈɒbviəs/ 美 /ˈɑːbviəs/
adj. 明显的；显而易见的
[义] Easy to see, understand, or recognize. 一看就明白、无需解释（obvious answer 显而易见的答案，for obvious reasons 原因很明显）。比 apparent 更口语、更直接；比 evident 略不正式。
[例] ① It was obvious that she had not read the email. 很明显她没读那封邮件。 ② For obvious reasons, the event was moved indoors. 原因显而易见，活动改到室内了。
[搭] obvious answer 显而易见的答案, for obvious reasons 原因很明显, obvious difference 明显差异, painfully obvious 明显到令人尴尬
[联] evident 显然的, apparent 明显的, clear 清楚的, noticeable 值得注意的, subtle 细微的, hidden 隐藏的
[记] obvious 后接 that 从句或 to sb.（It was obvious to everyone.）。obviously 副词极口语。与 oblivious（未察觉的）不同词

**evident**
英 /ˈevɪdənt/ 美 /ˈevɪdənt/
adj. 明显的；清楚的
[义] Plain and clear to see or understand; obvious. 清楚易见、证据充分（evident from the data 从数据可明显看出，with evident relief 如释重负显而易见）。比 obvious 略正式，常出现在书面与论证中。
[例] ① It was evident from her tone that she was upset. 从她语气可明显听出她很不高兴。 ② The damage to the roof was evident after the storm. 暴风雨后屋顶的损坏一目了然。
[源] 拉丁语 *evidens*：e-（出）+ *videre*（看）→ 看得出来的 → 明显的。
[族] evidence 证据, evidently 显然, provide evidence 提供证据
[搭] evident from 从……可明显看出, self-evident 不证自明的, with evident relief 显然如释重负, become evident 变得明显
[联] obvious 明显的, apparent 显然的, clear 清楚的, proof 证据, visible 可见的, subtle 细微的
[记] self-evident 为固定词（self-evident truth 不证自明的真理）。evident 常接 from / that / in。evidently = clearly, apparently

**noticeable**
英 /ˈnəʊtɪsəbl/ 美 /ˈnoʊtɪsəbl/
adj. 显而易见的；值得注意的
[义] Easy to see or recognize; worth paying attention to. 容易被注意到（noticeable improvement 明显进步，noticeable difference 可察觉的差异）。强调「变化/差异大到会被感知到」；比 obvious 语气可略弱。
[例] ① There has been a noticeable improvement in air quality this year. 今年空气质量有明显改善。 ② She dyed her hair a noticeable shade of red. 她把头发染成了很显眼的红。
[搭] noticeable difference/improvement 明显差异/进步, barely noticeable 几乎察觉不到, noticeable change 显著变化
[联] obvious 明显的, perceptible 可察觉的, visible 可见的, slight 轻微的, subtle 细微的, marked 显著的
[记] noticeable 由 notice + -able 构成。barely noticeable 与 noticeable improvement 为常见搭配。 noticeably 副词

**obscure**
英 /əbˈskjʊə/ 美 /əbˈskjʊr/
adj. 模糊的；晦涩的；不知名的
v. 使模糊；遮挡
[义] Not discovered or known about; difficult to understand; to hide from view. 鲜为人知（obscure village 无名小村）；难懂（obscure reference 晦涩典故）；动词「遮挡、使看不清」（clouds obscured the sun 云遮住太阳）。
[例] ① The origins of the custom remain obscure. 这一习俗的起源仍不清楚。 ② Heavy smoke obscured the view from the window. 浓烟遮住了窗外的视野。
[搭] obscure origin 起源不明, obscure reference 晦涩典故, obscure the view 遮挡视线, remain obscure 仍不清楚
[联] unclear 不清楚的, vague 模糊的, hidden 隐藏的, famous 著名的, visible 可见的, block 挡住
[记] obscure 作动词较正式；obscure the issue 喻「把问题搅浑」。obscurity 名词（live in obscurity 默默无闻）

**hidden**
英 /ˈhɪdn/ 美 /ˈhɪdn/
adj. 隐藏的；隐秘的
[义] Kept out of sight; not easy to find or notice. 被藏起来、不易发现（hidden door 暗门，hidden costs 隐性成本，hidden camera 隐藏摄像头）。hide 的过去分词作形容词。
[例] ① They discovered a hidden passage behind the bookshelf. 他们在书架后发现一条暗道。 ② Read the contract carefully for hidden fees. 仔细读合同，留意隐性费用。
[搭] hidden costs/fees 隐性成本/费用, hidden camera 隐藏摄像头, hidden treasure 隐藏的宝藏, remain hidden 仍隐藏
[联] invisible 看不见的, secret 秘密的, visible 可见的, reveal 揭示, conceal 隐藏, obscure 模糊的
[记] hidden 作定语极常见；hidden agenda 隐性议程。hide-and-seek 捉迷藏。hidden 与 hiding 不同：hidden door 暗门

**palpable**
英 /ˈpælpəbl/ 美 /ˈpælpəbl/
adj. 可触知的；明显的
[义] So intense or obvious that it seems as if you could touch it; able to be felt. 强烈到仿佛摸得着（palpable tension 明显可感的紧张，palpable relief 如释重负）；字面义「可触知的」较正式、文学化。
[例] ① There was palpable tension in the room before the verdict was read. 宣读判决前，房间里紧张感几乎摸得到。 ② A palpable sense of relief spread through the crowd. 人群中弥漫着明显的如释重负之感。
[源] 拉丁语 *palpare*（轻触）+ -able → 可触摸的 → 明显的。
[族] palpate 触诊（医学）, palpitation 心悸
[搭] palpable tension/relief 可感的紧张/宽慰, palpable sense of 明显的……感, almost palpable 几乎可触
[联] tangible 可触摸的, perceptible 可察觉的, obvious 明显的, tension 紧张, relief 宽慰, atmosphere 气氛
[记] palpable 多修饰 tension, relief, excitement, fear 等抽象名词——强调「在场者都能感觉到」。医学 palpate 为「触诊」

**perception**
英 /pəˈsepʃn/ 美 /pərˈsepʃn/
n. 感知；看法
[义] The ability to see, hear, or become aware of something through the senses; a way of understanding. 感官知觉能力（visual perception 视觉）；或对事物的理解/看法（public perception 公众看法，change one's perception 改变认知）。与 perceive 动词成对。
[例] ① Depth perception helps you judge how far away objects are. 深度知觉帮你判断物体远近。 ② The scandal changed public perception of the company. 丑闻改变了公众对公司的看法。
[源] perceive + -tion → 感知；看法。
[族] perceive 感知, perceptive 敏锐的, misperception 误解
[搭] public perception 公众看法, visual perception 视觉, depth perception 深度知觉, change perception 改变认知, perception of 对……的看法
[联] sense 感觉, impression 印象, awareness 意识, misperception 误解, reality 现实, perspective 视角
[记] 可数/不可数均可。perception vs. reality 为常见对比框架。perception 作「看法」时近 public opinion

**impression**
英 /ɪmˈpreʃn/ 美 /ɪmˈpreʃn/
n. 印象；感想
[义] An idea or feeling about someone or something formed without conscious thought; the mark left by pressure. 对某人某事形成的总体感觉（first impression 第一印象，give the impression 给人……印象）；或压痕（leave an impression 留下压痕）。
[例] ① She made a strong impression at the job interview. 她在面试中给人留下了深刻印象。 ② My general impression is that the team works well together. 我总体印象是这个团队合作不错。
[搭] first impression 第一印象, give the impression 给人……印象, under the impression 以为, general impression 总体印象
[联] perception 感知, image 形象, impact 影响, memory 记忆, feeling 感觉, notion 看法
[记] under the impression that 表「原以为」（I was under the impression that...）。impressive 形容词；impressionist 印象派

**stimulus**
英 /ˈstɪmjələs/ 美 /ˈstɪmjələs/
n. 刺激；激励（复数 stimuli）
[义] Something that causes a response in the body or mind; something that encourages activity. 引起身体或心理反应的刺激物（visual stimulus 视觉刺激，economic stimulus 经济刺激）。复数 stimuli /ˈstɪmjəlaɪ/。偏正式、心理/经济/生理语境。
[例] ① Bright lights can be a strong stimulus for some migraine sufferers. 强光对有些偏头痛患者是强刺激。 ② The government introduced a fiscal stimulus to boost growth. 政府推出财政刺激以提振增长。
[源] 拉丁语 stimulus（赶牛用的刺棒）→ 刺激。
[族] stimulate 刺激, stimulation 刺激, stimulant 兴奋剂
[搭] economic stimulus 经济刺激, visual/auditory stimulus 视觉/听觉刺激, respond to a stimulus 对刺激作出反应, stimulus package 刺激方案
[联] trigger 触发, response 反应, incentive 激励, signal 信号, irritant 刺激物, boost 提振
[记] 复数 stimuli；stimulus 在心理学指「引起反应的刺激」。economic stimulus package 新闻常用

**illusion**
英 /ɪˈluːʒn/ 美 /ɪˈluːʒn/
n. 错觉；幻想
[义] Something that appears to exist but does not, or is not what it seems; a false belief. 看起来像真的但并非如此的「错觉」（optical illusion 视错觉）；或错误的信念（under the illusion that 误以为）。与 delusion 比：illusion 可指感官骗局，delusion 更偏「顽固妄想」。
[例] ① The mirror created an illusion of a much larger room. 镜子造成了房间大得多的错觉。 ② He was under no illusion about how hard the job would be. 他对这份工作有多难并不抱幻想。
[源] 拉丁语 *illudere*：in-（反对）+ *ludere*（玩）→ 捉弄眼睛 → 错觉。
[族] illusory 虚幻的, illusive 幻觉的, disillusion 使幻想破灭
[搭] optical illusion 视错觉, under the illusion 误以为, create an illusion 制造假象, illusion of control 控制错觉
[联] delusion 妄想, mirage 海市蜃楼, reality 现实, perception 感知, trick 把戏, fantasy 幻想
[记] under no illusion 表「清楚现实、不抱幻想」。optical illusion 极常见。illusion 可数；illusive/illusory 形容词

**loud**
英 /laʊd/ 美 /laʊd/
adj. 大声的；响亮的
adv. 大声地
[义] Producing or capable of producing much noise; with a strong sound. 音量大、传得远（loud music 大声音乐，loud voice 大嗓门）。作副词表「大声地」（read aloud 朗读，think aloud 出声想）。与 noisy 比：loud 可指单次音量大，noisy 偏「吵闹、持续嘈杂」。
[例] ① The music next door was so loud that I couldn't sleep. 隔壁音乐太响，我睡不着。 ② Please read the instructions aloud to the group. 请向全组朗读说明。
[搭] loud noise 巨响, loud voice 大嗓门, read aloud 朗读, too loud 太大声, loud and clear 清清楚楚
[联] quiet 安静的, noisy 吵闹的, silent 无声的, deafening 震耳欲聋的, soft 轻柔的, volume 音量
[记] loud 作表语/定语均可；aloud 副词专指「出声地」（非默读）。loudly 副词与 loud 副词用法有细微分工：play loudly 演奏得响

**quiet**
英 /ˈkwaɪət/ 美 /ˈkwaɪət/
adj. 安静的；轻声的
n. 安静
v. （使）安静
[义] Making little or no noise; peaceful. 声音小或无声（quiet street 安静街道，keep quiet 保持安静）；环境平静。比 silent 弱——quiet 可仍有轻微声响；silent 强调「完全无声」。动词 quiet 表「使安静下来」。
[例] ① The library is a quiet place to study. 图书馆是安静的学习场所。 ② Could you quiet the children down before the call? 打电话前能让孩子们静下来吗？
[搭] keep quiet 保持安静, quiet place 安静的地方, quiet voice 轻声, peace and quiet 安宁, quiet down 静下来
[联] silent 无声的, loud 大声的, noisy 吵闹的, calm 平静的, still 静止的, whisper 低语
[记] quiet 与 quite /ˈkwaɪt/（相当）同音异义。peace and quiet 为固定搭配。quietly 副词

**noisy**
英 /ˈnɔɪzi/ 美 /ˈnɔɪzi/
adj. 吵闹的；嘈杂的
[义] Making a lot of noise; full of loud sounds. 持续或反复发出很多噪音（noisy neighbors 吵闹的邻居，noisy classroom 嘈杂教室）。比 loud 更强调「闹、乱、烦」的整体环境，而非单次音量。
[例] ① The restaurant was too noisy for a private conversation. 餐厅太吵，没法私下交谈。 ② Construction work made the street noisy all week. 施工让整条街吵了一周。
[搭] noisy neighbors 吵闹邻居, noisy environment 嘈杂环境, get noisy 变得吵闹, noisy crowd 喧闹人群
[联] loud 大声的, quiet 安静的, silent 无声的, chaotic 混乱的, disturbance 干扰, racket 吵闹
[记] noisy 只作形容词；noise 名词。noisy vs. loud：noisy street 整条街闹；loud bang 一声巨响

**piercing**
英 /ˈpɪəsɪŋ/ 美 /ˈpɪrsɪŋ/
adj. 刺耳的；穿透性的
[义] Very loud, high, and unpleasant to the ears; sharp and penetrating. 又尖又响、刺耳（piercing scream 刺耳尖叫，piercing cold 刺骨寒冷）。本义 pierce 为「刺穿」；形容声音、寒风、目光（piercing look 锐利目光）。
[例] ① A piercing alarm woke everyone in the building. 刺耳的警报把楼里所有人都吵醒了。 ② She felt a piercing pain in her ankle when she landed wrong. 落地不稳时脚踝一阵刺痛。
[搭] piercing scream 刺耳尖叫, piercing cold 刺骨寒冷, piercing look 锐利目光, piercing pain 刺痛
[联] shrill 尖厉的, sharp 尖锐的, deafening 震耳欲聋的, intense 强烈的, cold 寒冷, gaze 目光
[记] piercing 可修饰 cry, scream, pain, cold, eyes。body piercing 为另一义「穿洞饰品」。pierce 动词

**shrill**
英 /ʃrɪl/ 美 /ʃrɪl/
adj. 尖厉的；刺耳的
v. 尖声发出
[义] High-pitched and piercing, especially in an unpleasant way. 音调又高又尖、往往刺耳（shrill voice 尖嗓子，shrill whistle 尖厉哨声）。与 piercing 近义；shrill 更强调「尖、细、厉」。
[例] ① The shrill ring of the phone startled the baby. 电话尖铃把宝宝吓了一跳。 ② Critics dismissed her shrill tone as unhelpful. 批评者认为她尖刻的语气无助于沟通。
[搭] shrill voice 尖嗓子, shrill whistle 尖厉哨声, shrill cry 尖声叫喊, sound shrill 听起来刺耳
[联] piercing 刺耳的, high-pitched 尖声的, loud 大声的, squeak 吱吱声, whistle 哨声, harsh 刺耳的
[记] shrill 常含负面「刺耳、尖刻」；形容政治 rhetoric 时表「声调过高」。动词 shrill 较少见

**hum**
英 /hʌm/ 美 /hʌm/
v. 哼唱；发嗡嗡声
n. 嗡嗡声
[义] To sing with closed lips; to make a low continuous sound. 闭唇哼曲（hum a tune 哼小调）；或机器、蜜蜂等持续低鸣（refrigerator humming 冰箱嗡嗡响，room hummed with conversation 房间人声嗡嗡）。比 buzz 更低沉、更持续。
[例] ① She hummed softly while washing the dishes. 洗碗时她轻轻哼着歌。 ② The office hummed with activity on Monday morning. 周一上午办公室一片忙碌低语。
[搭] hum a tune 哼小调, hum with activity 一片忙碌, electrical hum 电流嗡嗡声, hum to oneself 自顾自哼
[联] buzz 嗡嗡叫, murmur 低语, drone 低沉嗡鸣, sing 唱, vibration 振动, tune 曲调
[记] hum 可及物/不及物；hum and haw（英式）表「支支吾吾」。hum 与 hymn（圣歌）不同词

**hiss**
英 /hɪs/ 美 /hɪs/
v. 发嘶嘶声；发出嘘声
n. 嘶嘶声；嘘声
[义] To make a sound like a long "s"; to show disapproval by making this sound. 像长音 [s] 的嘶声（snake hissed 蛇嘶嘶，hissing sound 嘶嘶声）；或观众「嘘」表示不满（the crowd hissed 观众发出嘘声）。
[例] ① The radiator hissed when the heating came on. 暖气启动时散热器嘶嘶作响。 ② The audience hissed when the villain appeared on stage. 反派登场时观众嘘声一片。
[搭] hiss at 对……嘶嘶/发嘘声, hissing sound 嘶嘶声, steam hiss 蒸汽嘶嘶声, crowd hissed 观众嘘
[联] buzz 嗡嗡, whisper 低语, snake 蛇, steam 蒸汽, boo 嘘, silence 安静
[记] hiss 作名词/动词均可；hiss of disapproval 表不满嘘声。录音 hiss 指「底噪嘶声」

**crackle**
英 /ˈkrækl/ 美 /ˈkrækl/
v. 噼啪作响
n. 噼啪声
[义] To make short sharp cracking sounds repeatedly. 连续短促的噼啪声（fire crackling 炉火噼啪，crackling radio 杂音很大的电台）。比 crack 更「连续、细碎」。
[例] ① We sat by the fire, listening to the logs crackle. 我们坐在火边听木柴噼啪响。 ② Old recordings often crackle when you play them. 老录音播放时常带噼啪杂音。
[搭] crackle with 噼啪作响, fire crackling 炉火噼啪, crackling sound 噼啪声, radio crackle 电台杂音
[联] crack 噼啪, pop 啪, snap 啪嗒声, fire 火, static 静电杂音, crunch 嘎吱声
[记] crackle 作名词可数；Rice Krispies 广告 snap, crackle and pop。crackling 也可指「脆皮」（roast crackling）

**whine**
英 /waɪn/ 美 /waɪn/
v. 发呜咽声；抱怨地发牢骚
n. 呜咽声；尖鸣
[义] To make a long high cry of pain or complaint; to complain in an annoying way. 长而高的哀鸣（dog whining 狗呜咽，engine whine 引擎尖鸣）；或人「抱怨发牢骚」（stop whining 别抱怨了）。与 wine 同音异义。
[例] ① The dog whined at the door to be let out. 狗在门边呜咽着要出去。 ② He whined about the amount of homework every evening. 他每晚都抱怨作业太多。
[搭] whine about 抱怨, stop whining 别抱怨, engine whine 引擎尖鸣, whine at 对……呜咽/抱怨
[联] complain 抱怨, moan 呻吟, cry 哭, buzz 嗡嗡, grumble 发牢骚, wine 葡萄酒（同音）
[记] whine 含负面「烦人的抱怨」；child whining 极常见。whine vs. wine 拼写勿混

**rumble**
英 /ˈrʌmbl/ 美 /ˈrʌmbl/
v. 隆隆作响；辘辘行进
n. 隆隆声
[义] To make a long low deep sound; to move with such a sound. 低沉持久的轰鸣（thunder rumbled 雷声隆隆，stomach rumbling 肚子咕咕叫，truck rumbled past 卡车轰隆隆开过）。
[例] ① Thunder rumbled in the distance as the storm approached. 暴风雨逼近时远处雷声隆隆。 ② Her stomach rumbling embarrassed her during the quiet exam. 安静考试中她肚子咕咕叫，很尴尬。
[搭] thunder rumble 雷声隆隆, stomach rumbling 肚子咕咕叫, rumble past 轰隆经过, deep rumble 低沉轰鸣
[联] roar 轰鸣, boom 轰响, growl 低吼, thunder 雷, vibration 振动, echo 回声
[记] rumble 可指雷、引擎、肚子；stomach rumble 口语极自然。rumble strip（减速带）见交通章

**vibrate**
英 /vaɪˈbreɪt/ 美 /ˈvaɪbreɪt/
v. 振动；颤动
[义] To move or shake rapidly back and forth; to quiver. 快速来回抖动（phone vibrates 手机震动，voice vibrated with emotion 声音因激动而颤抖）。比 shake 更偏「高频小幅」；物理/工程/手机语境常见。
[例] ① My phone vibrated in my pocket during the meeting. 开会时手机在口袋里震。 ② The bridge began to vibrate as the train crossed. 火车经过时桥开始颤动。
[源] 拉丁语 *vibrare*（来回摇）→ 振动。
[族] vibration 振动, vibrant 充满生机的, vibrancy 活力
[搭] vibrate with 因……而颤动, phone vibrates 手机震动, vibrate at a frequency 以某频率振动
[联] shake 摇晃, tremble 颤抖, buzz 嗡嗡, resonance 共鸣, oscillate 振荡, quiver 微颤
[记] vibrate 及物/不及物；on vibrate 指手机「震动模式」。vibrant 形容词义不同：「充满活力的」

**acrid**
英 /ˈækrɪd/ 美 /ˈækrɪd/
adj. 刺鼻的；刻薄的
[义] Having a strong bitter smell or taste that stings the nose or throat; bitter in tone. 刺激鼻喉的辛辣臭（acrid smoke 刺鼻烟，acrid smell 呛人气味）；引申「刻薄的」（acrid comment 尖刻评论）。比 pungent 更偏「呛、辣、烧」。
[例] ① Acrid smoke from the fire stung our eyes and throats. 火灾的刺鼻烟熏得我们眼喉发痛。 ② The review was written in an acrid tone. 这篇评论笔调尖刻。
[搭] acrid smoke/smell 刺鼻烟/气味, acrid taste 呛味, acrid comment 刻薄评论
[联] pungent 刺鼻的, bitter 苦的, foul 恶臭的, smoke 烟, sharp 尖锐的, stench 恶臭
[记] acrid 几乎总修饰 smoke, smell, fumes, tone。与 acid（酸）联想助记「刺激性强」

**odorless**
英 /ˈəʊdələs/ 美 /ˈoʊdərləs/
adj. 无味的；没有气味的
> 英式拼写 odourless /ˈəʊdələs/
[义] Having no smell. 没有任何可闻气味（odorless gas 无味气体，odorless paint 无味涂料）。化学、家装、安全说明中常见——强调「闻不出但可能仍危险」。
[例] ① Carbon monoxide is an odorless, colorless gas that can be deadly. 一氧化碳是无色无味气体，可致命。 ② They switched to odorless paint for the nursery. 他们给婴儿房换成了无味涂料。
[搭] odorless gas 无味气体, odorless paint 无味涂料, colorless and odorless 无色无味
[联] odor 气味, scentless 无香的, smell 闻, fragrance 芬芳, invisible 看不见的, gas 气体
[记] odorless 与 colorless 常并列描述危险气体。英式 odourless。勿以为 odorless = safe

**smelly**
英 /ˈsmeli/ 美 /ˈsmeli/
adj. 有臭味的；发臭的
[义] Having an unpleasant smell (informal). 有不好闻的气味（口语 smelly socks 臭袜子，smelly cheese 气味重的奶酪）。比 stinky 略中性；smelly 可半开玩笑。
[例] ① He threw his smelly gym clothes straight into the wash. 他把发臭的运动服直接扔进洗衣机。 ② The bin was smelly after a week in the heat. 垃圾桶在暑热里放了一周，臭气熏天。
[搭] smelly socks 臭袜子, smelly cheese 气味重的奶酪, a bit smelly 有点臭, smelly bin 发臭的垃圾桶
[联] stinky 臭的, odor 气味, fragrant 芬芳的, musty 霉味的, reek 发臭, fresh 新鲜的
[记] smelly 口语；正式写作可用 malodorous（较书面）。smelly 有时形容 cheese 为褒（风味强）

**miss**
英 /mɪs/ 美 /mɪs/
v. 未看见；未听见；错过
[义] To fail to see, hear, or notice; to fail to hit or reach; to feel sad without. 感官上「没注意到」（miss a turn 错过转弯，miss the point 没领会要点）；亦指「错过」事件或「想念」某人——依语境区分。本章侧重感知义。
[例] ① I missed what you said—could you repeat it? 我刚没听清你说什么，能再说一遍吗？ ② She missed the warning sign because she was looking at her phone. 她看手机，没注意到警示牌。
[搭] miss the point 没领会要点, miss a turn 错过转弯, miss the chance 错过机会, hardly miss 几乎不会忽略, miss sb. 想念某人
[联] overlook 忽视, ignore 不理, notice 注意到, catch 听清/抓住, fail 未能
[记] miss 多义：感知「未察觉」、错过机会、想念某人。miss the boat 喻「错过良机」。不可与 Ms. 混淆

**lick**
英 /lɪk/ 美 /lɪk/
v. 舔
n. 舔；少量
[义] To pass the tongue over something; a small amount (informal). 用舌头接触（lick the spoon 舔勺子，dog licked his hand 狗舔他的手）；口语 a lick of paint 薄薄一层漆。味觉/触觉通道上的动作。
[例] ① The cat licked its paws after eating. 猫吃完后舔了舔爪子。 ② She licked the envelope and sealed it. 她舔了舔信封口再封上。
[搭] lick one's lips 舔嘴唇, lick the bowl 舔碗, a lick of paint 薄薄一层漆, lick clean 舔干净
[联] taste 尝, touch 触摸, tongue 舌头, sip 小口喝, bite 咬, dog 狗
[记] lick 规则变化 licked。lick one's lips 可喻「垂涎」。a lick of 口语表「一点点」

**scratch**
英 /skrætʃ/ 美 /skrætʃ/
v. 抓；挠；刮
n. 抓痕；挠痒
[义] To rub your skin with nails because it itches; to make a shallow mark on a surface. 因痒而挠（scratch an itch 挠痒）；或刮擦表面留痕（scratch the car 刮花车）。触觉上的摩擦/刺激。
[例] ① Mosquito bites are worse if you keep scratching them. 蚊子块越挠越糟。 ② Be careful not to scratch the wooden table. 小心别刮花木桌。
[搭] scratch an itch 挠痒, scratch the surface 只触及表面（喻）, from scratch 从零开始, scratch one's head 挠头（困惑）
[联] itch 痒, rub 摩擦, scrape 刮, mark 痕迹, rash 皮疹, claw 抓
[记] from scratch 固定短语表「从头做起」。scratch the surface 喻「只了解皮毛」。形容词 scratchy 见属性章

**rub**
英 /rʌb/ 美 /rʌb/
v. 摩擦；揉
n. 摩擦；困难
[义] To press something and move it back and forth against a surface. 来回按压摩擦（rub your eyes 揉眼，rub shoulders with 与……交往）。名词 the rub 为旧式「难点、障碍」。
[例] ① She rubbed her temples to ease the headache. 她揉太阳穴缓解头痛。 ② Rub the stain with a damp cloth before washing. 洗前用湿布反复擦污渍。
[搭] rub against 摩擦, rub one's eyes 揉眼, rub in 反复提及（喻）, the rub is 难点在于
[联] scratch 挠, stroke 抚摸, polish 擦亮, friction 摩擦, massage 按摩, wipe 擦
[记] rub 规则变化 rubbed。rub it in 口语表「往伤口撒盐」。rub off on sb. 表「感染/影响」

**stroke**
英 /strəʊk/ 美 /stroʊk/
v. 抚摸
n. 一下；中风
[义] To move your hand gently over someone or something; a single movement or medical stroke. 用手轻抚（stroke the cat 抚摸猫，stroke his hair 抚他的头发）。名词 stroke 亦指「一笔、一划」或医学「中风」——依语境区分。
[例] ① She stroked the dog's head and it wagged its tail. 她抚摸狗头，狗摇了摇尾巴。 ② He painted the sky in broad strokes of blue and grey. 他用蓝灰大笔触画出天空。
[搭] stroke one's hair 抚头发, broad strokes 大笔触/大框架, at a stroke 一下子, heat stroke 中暑
[联] caress 爱抚, pat 轻拍, touch 触摸, gentle 轻柔的, brushstroke 一笔, pet 抚摸
[记] stroke 作动词规则变化 stroked。stroke of luck 喻「好运」。医学 stroke 与抚摸义同形

**caress**
英 /kəˈres/ 美 /kəˈres/
v. 爱抚；抚爱
n. 爱抚
[义] To touch someone gently in a loving or sexual way. 温柔、带感情地抚摸（caress her cheek 轻抚她的脸颊）。比 stroke 更强调「亲密、柔情、浪漫或 sensual」。
[例] ① He caressed her hand before saying goodbye. 告别前他轻轻握抚她的手。 ② Sunlight caressed the tops of the hills at dawn. 黎明时阳光柔和地抚过山脊（文学写法）。
[搭] caress gently 轻柔爱抚, caress one's cheek 抚脸颊, soft caress 温柔的爱抚
[联] stroke 抚摸, touch 触摸, hug 拥抱, gentle 轻柔的, affection 温情, kiss 吻
[记] caress 较文学/浪漫；日常简单 touch/stroke 更中性。caress 可数名词 a caress

**prickle**
英 /ˈprɪkl/ 美 /ˈprɪkl/
v. 刺痒；使扎痛
n. 刺；刺痒感
[义] To cause a slight stinging feeling on the skin; a small sharp point. 皮肤感到刺、扎（prickle with sweat 汗毛倒竖，prickling sensation 刺痒感）；名词指刺或小刺。比 itch 更「扎」，比 sting 更轻。
[例] ① The wool sweater prickled against my neck. 羊毛衫扎得我脖子刺痒。 ② I felt a prickle of fear when I heard the door open. 听见门开，我感到一阵恐惧（喻「汗毛倒竖」）。
[搭] prickle with 因……而刺痒/发毛, prickling sensation 刺痒感, prickle of fear/excitement 一阵恐惧/兴奋
[联] itch 痒, tingle 刺麻, sting 刺痛, goosebumps 鸡皮疙瘩, sharp 尖锐的, wool 羊毛
[记] prickle 可作名词/动词；prickly 形容词（prickly heat 痱子，prickly person 难相处的人）

**tremble**
英 /ˈtrembl/ 美 /ˈtrembl/
v. 颤抖；发抖
n. 颤抖
[义] To shake slightly because you are cold, frightened, or emotional. 因冷、怕或激动而轻微发抖（tremble with fear 吓得发抖，voice trembling 声音发颤）。比 vibrate 更偏「身体/情感」、幅度可见。
[例] ① Her hands trembled as she opened the letter. 拆信时她双手发抖。 ② The leaves trembled in the breeze. 叶子在微风中轻颤。
[搭] tremble with fear/cold 因恐惧/寒冷发抖, voice trembling 声音发颤, tremble at 对……发抖
[联] shake 摇晃, shiver 打寒颤, vibrate 振动, fear 恐惧, quiver 微颤, nervous 紧张的
[记] tremble 不及物；trembling 形容词/名词。tremble with 后接 emotion/cold/fear。与 tumble（跌倒）不同词

**throb**
英 /θrɒb/ 美 /θrɑːb/
v. 抽动；搏动
n. 搏动；跳痛
[义] To beat or pulse with a steady, often painful rhythm. 有节奏地搏动——常指痛（head throbbing 头一跳一跳地痛，throbbing pain 跳痛）；或音乐/引擎低鸣（music throbbing 音乐低沉脉动）。
[例] ① My ankle was throbbing after I twisted it. 扭伤后脚踝一跳一跳地疼。 ② Bass music throbbed from the club down the street. 街那头俱乐部传来低音轰鸣。
[搭] throbbing pain 跳痛, head throbbing 头搏动作痛, throb with 随……而搏动, dull throb 隐隐作痛
[联] pulse 脉搏, ache 疼痛, beat 跳动, pain 痛, headache 头痛, vibrate 振动
[记] throb 不及物；throbbing 极常见修饰 pain/headache/music。与 stab（刺痛）不同：throb 为「搏动式」

**gasp**
英 /ɡɑːsp/ 美 /ɡæsp/
v. 喘气；倒吸一口气
n. 喘气声
[义] To breathe in suddenly and loudly, especially in surprise or pain. 突然大口吸气——常因惊讶、疼痛或缺氧（gasp in shock 惊倒吸一口气，gasp for air 喘不上气）。
[例] ① The audience gasped when the magician sawed the box in half. 魔术师把箱子锯开时观众倒吸一口气。 ② She was gasping for air after sprinting up the stairs. 冲上楼梯后她大口喘气。
[搭] gasp for air/breath 喘不上气, gasp in surprise 惊吸一口气, last gasp 最后一口气/最后努力
[联] pant 喘息, breathe 呼吸, shock 震惊, sigh 叹气, choke 窒息, surprise 惊讶
[记] gasp 可数/不可数；last gasp 喻「最后挣扎」。audible gasp 见 audible 条

**hiccup**
英 /ˈhɪkʌp/ 美 /ˈhɪkʌp/
n. 嗝；小问题
v. 打嗝
[义] A sudden involuntary sound when the diaphragm spasms; a minor temporary problem. 膈肌痉挛发出的打嗝（have the hiccups 打嗝）；喻「小波折」（a few hiccups along the way 路上有点小状况）。英式拼写 hiccough 少见。
[例] ① He got the hiccups during his speech and had to pause. 演讲时他打起嗝，只好停了一下。 ② The project went smoothly with only a few hiccups. 项目总体顺利，只有几处小波折。
[搭] have the hiccups 打嗝, hiccup along the way 小波折, cure hiccups 止住打嗝
[联] burp 打嗝（饱嗝）, cough 咳嗽, sneeze 打喷嚏, spasm 痉挛, glitch 小故障, pause 停顿
[记] 常用复数 hiccups 表「打嗝发作」。hiccup 作可数名词喻「小困难」为英式/商业口语

**resonate**
英 /ˈrezəneɪt/ 美 /ˈrezəneɪt/
v. 共鸣；回荡；引起共鸣
[义] To produce or be filled with a deep, clear, continuing sound; to evoke a strong feeling. 声音低沉、持续地回荡（voice resonated 声音回荡）；喻「触动人心、引发共鸣」（resonate with audiences 引起观众共鸣）。比 echo 更强调「浑厚、共鸣腔」。
[例] ① Her voice resonated through the empty hall. 她的声音在空荡的大厅里回荡。 ② The theme of fairness resonated with voters across the country. 公平主题在全国选民中引起共鸣。
[源] 拉丁语 *resonare*：re-（回）+ *sonare*（响）→ 回响。
[族] resonance 共鸣, resonant 洪亮的, resonator 共鸣器
[搭] resonate with 与……共鸣, resonate through 在……中回荡, resonate deeply 深深刻印
[联] echo 回声, reverberate 回荡, vibrate 振动, rumble 隆隆, audience 观众, theme 主题
[记] resonate with 喻义极常见（The story resonated with me.）。物理义指声波在腔体内放大

**reverberate**
英 /rɪˈvɜːbəreɪt/ 美 /rɪˈvɜːrbəreɪt/
v. 回响；回荡
[义] To be repeated several times as an echo; to have continuing serious effects. 声音反复回荡（gunshot reverberated 枪声回响）；喻影响持续扩散（shock reverberated through the industry 冲击在整个行业回荡）。
[例] ① The explosion reverberated across the valley for several seconds. 爆炸声在山谷里回荡了好几秒。 ② The scandal continued to reverberate for months. 丑闻余波持续了好几个月。
[源] re-（回）+ *verberare*（击打）→ 反复弹回 → 回响。
[族] reverberation 回响, reverberant 回响的
[搭] reverberate through/across 在……回荡, reverberate around 在周围回响, continue to reverberate 持续产生影响
[联] echo 回声, resonate 共鸣, rumble 轰鸣, explosion 爆炸, shock 冲击, valley 山谷
[记] reverberate 比 echo 更书面、更强调「反复、久久不散」。reverberations 常喻「连锁后果」

**resound**
英 /rɪˈzaʊnd/ 美 /rɪˈzaʊnd/
v. 回响；响彻
[义] To fill a place with sound; to be mentioned or discussed a lot. 声音充满空间（cheers resounded 欢呼响彻）；喻被大量讨论（resound through history 在历史中回响）。比 echo 更「填满、响彻」。
[例] ① Laughter resounded from the kitchen. 厨房里传来阵阵笑声。 ② His name resounds through the history of science. 他的名字在科学史上回响。
[搭] resound with 充满……声, resound through 响彻, resound across 传遍
[联] echo 回声, resonate 共鸣, reverberate 回荡, cheer 欢呼, fill 充满, history 历史
[记] resound 较文学/正式；日常口语多用 echo / ring out。resound with laughter/applause 为常见搭配

**mumble**
英 /ˈmʌmbl/ 美 /ˈmʌmbl/
v. 含糊地说；咕哝
n. 含糊的话
[义] To speak quietly and unclearly, so that others cannot hear or understand easily. 小声、吐字不清地说（mumble an apology 含糊道歉，stop mumbling 别含混不清）。比 mutter 更强调「听不清内容」；mutter 更偏「抱怨」。
[例] ① He mumbled something and turned away before I could ask again. 他咕哝了句什么，我还没来得及再问就转身走了。 ② Please don't mumble—speak clearly into the microphone. 别含混，对着麦克风说清楚。
[搭] mumble an apology 含糊道歉, mumble to oneself 自言自语, stop mumbling 别含混, mumble under one's breath 低声咕哝
[联] mutter 抱怨地咕哝, murmur 低语, whisper 耳语, slur 含糊, inaudible 听不见的, apologize 道歉
[记] mumble 不含 mutter 的「牢骚」色彩，纯指「听不清」。mumbled 常修饰 apology, reply, thanks

**gleam**
英 /ɡliːm/ 美 /ɡliːm/
v. 闪烁；流露
n. 微光；一丝
[义] To shine with a soft light; a brief show of a quality or emotion. 柔和地闪光（moonlight gleaming 月光闪烁）；或情感/品质「一闪而过」（a gleam of hope 一线希望，gleam in his eye 眼里的精光）。
[例] ① Fresh rain made the leaves gleam in the morning sun. 新雨后叶子在晨光里闪闪发亮。 ② There was a gleam of mischief in her eyes when she suggested the prank. 她提议恶作剧时眼里闪过一丝调皮。
[搭] gleam of hope 一线希望, gleam in one's eye 眼里的光, gleam with 闪烁着, moonlight gleaming 月光闪烁
[联] glow 微光, shine 发光, sparkle 闪耀, glimmer 微光, hope 希望, sunlight 阳光
[记] gleam 比 shine 更「细、柔、短」；a gleam of 常喻「一丝（希望/恶意）」。动词/名词均可

**glitter**
英 /ˈɡlɪtə/ 美 /ˈɡlɪtər/
v. 闪闪发光
n. 闪光；浮华
[义] To shine brightly with flashing points of light; superficial attractiveness. 许多光点闪动（snow glittering 雪光闪闪，glittering jewels 璀璨的珠宝）；名词亦可指「浮华、表面光鲜」（the glitter of fame 名声的光鲜）。
[例] ① The city skyline glittered with thousands of lights at night. 夜晚城市天际线万千灯火闪烁。 ② She was dazzled by the glitter of Hollywood and moved to LA. 她被好莱坞的光鲜迷惑，搬到了洛杉矶。
[搭] glitter with 闪烁着, glittering jewels 璀璨珠宝, the glitter of fame 名声的光鲜, all that glitters 闪光的（谚语）
[联] sparkle 闪耀, shine 发光, gleam 微光, gold 金, fame 名声, dazzle 炫目
[记] All that glitters is not gold 谚语「发光的不都是金子」。glitter 可含「浮华、肤浅」贬义

**discernible**
英 /dɪˈsɜːnəbl/ 美 /dɪˈsɜːrnəbl/
adj. 可辨别的；看得出的
[义] Able to be seen, heard, or understood with difficulty or effort. 经辨别后可察觉（discernible difference 可辨的差异，barely discernible 几乎看不出）。与 discern 动词成对；比 visible 更强调「需仔细看/听才分得清」。
[例] ① There is a discernible shift in public opinion on the issue. 公众在这问题上的看法有明显（可辨的）转变。 ② In the fog, the shore was barely discernible. 雾中海岸几乎看不清楚。
[源] discern（辨明）+ -ible → 可辨别的。
[族] discern 辨明, discerning 有眼光的, indiscernible 难以分辨的
[搭] discernible difference/shift 可辨的差异/转变, barely discernible 几乎看不出, clearly discernible 清晰可辨
[联] perceptible 可察觉的, visible 可见的, noticeable 明显的, vague 模糊的, shift 转变, difference 差异
[记] discernible 较正式；日常 noticeable / visible 更口语。discernible pattern/trend 常见于分析文本

**discerning**
英 /dɪˈsɜːnɪŋ/ 美 /dɪˈsɜːrnɪŋ/
adj. 有辨识力的；眼光独到的
[义] Having or showing good judgment, especially about quality. 能辨优劣、有鉴赏力（discerning customer 懂行的顾客，discerning eye 有辨识力的眼光）。discern 的现在分词形容词化。
[例] ① The restaurant caters to discerning diners who care about ingredients. 这家餐厅面向重视食材的懂行食客。 ② A discerning reader will notice the subtle irony. 有眼光的读者会注意到 subtle 的反讽。
[搭] discerning eye/customer/reader 有眼光的……, highly discerning 极有鉴赏力, discerning taste 独到的品味
[联] perceptive 敏锐的, sharp 尖锐的, taste 品味, quality 质量, judgment 判断力, subtle 细微的
[记] discerning 几乎总褒义。discerning vs. discriminating：后者因 discriminate 另一义「歧视」需依语境区分

**palate**
英 /ˈpælət/ 美 /ˈpælət/
n. 上腭；味觉；鉴赏力
[义] The roof of the mouth; a person's ability to judge food and drink; taste in a broader sense. 口腔上腭；亦指「味觉、品鉴力」（refined palate 精致味觉, suit every palate 适合各种口味）。与 plate 同音异义。
[例] ① The spicy soup burned the back of my palate. 辣汤烫得我上腭发痛。 ② The chef creates dishes for an adventurous palate. 这位主厨为敢于尝鲜的味蕾设计菜式。
[搭] refined palate 精致味觉, suit every palate 适合各种口味, palate cleanser 清口, develop one's palate 培养品鉴力
[联] taste 味觉, flavor 风味, tongue 舌, gustatory 味觉的, appetite 食欲, dish 菜
[记] palate vs. palette（调色板）vs. pallet（货板）三词同音易混。wine palate 指「品酒鉴赏力」

**nauseating**
英 /ˈnɔːzɪeɪtɪŋ/ 美 /ˈnɔːziːeɪtɪŋ/
adj. 令人作呕的；令人厌恶的
[义] Causing nausea; making you feel sick or disgusted. 引发生理恶心（nauseating smell 令人作呕的气味）；或道德/情感上「恶心」（nauseating hypocrisy 令人作呕的虚伪）。动词 nauseate。
[例] ① The nauseating smell of rotting fish filled the alley. 腐烂鱼类的恶臭弥漫小巷，令人作呕。 ② I find his nauseating flattery hard to listen to. 他那令人作呕的奉承我听着难受。
[搭] nauseating smell 恶臭, nauseating hypocrisy 虚伪, find sth. nauseating 觉得……恶心
[联] sickening 令人作呕的, stench 恶臭, disgusting 令人厌恶的, smell 气味, nausea 恶心, rotten 腐烂的
[记] nauseating 可指生理或道德义。nausea 名词；motion sickness 可致 nausea

**scentless**
英 /ˈsentləs/ 美 /ˈsentləs/
adj. 无气味的
[义] Having no smell. 没有任何可闻气味（scentless flowers 无香花，scentless detergent 无香洗涤剂）。与 odorless 近义；scentless 更常指「本可有香味却没有」。
[例] ① Some modern roses are bred to be large but scentless. 有些现代玫瑰 bred 得很大却无香。 ② She prefers scentless soap for sensitive skin. 她敏感皮肤偏好无香皂。
[搭] scentless flowers 无香花, scentless detergent 无香洗涤剂, almost scentless 几乎无味
[联] odorless 无味的, fragrance 芬芳, smell 气味, perfume 香水, flower 花, soap 皂
[记] scentless 与 fragrant 相对。odorless 偏安全/化学语境；scentless 偏花卉、洗护产品

**stink**
英 /stɪŋk/ 美 /stɪŋk/
v. 发恶臭；糟糕透顶
n. 臭气；丑闻
[义] To smell very unpleasant; to be very bad (informal). 散发恶臭（stink of smoke 烟臭熏天）；口语「糟透了」（the plan stinks 计划烂透了）。比 smell bad 更粗、更强；不规则 stank / stunk。
[例] ① The fridge stank because we had forgotten to clean it. 冰箱发臭，因为我们忘了清理。 ② Something stinks about the way the contract was awarded. 合同这样批下来，总觉得有猫腻。
[搭] stink of 充满……臭气/嫌疑, stink to high heaven 臭气熏天, make a stink 大吵大闹（口语）, the plan stinks 计划糟透了
[联] reek 发臭, smell 气味, stench 恶臭, odor 气味, rotten 腐烂的, scandal 丑闻
[记] 不规则过去式 stank，分词 stunk。make a stink about 口语「为……大闹」。stinking 作强调副词（stinking rich 阔得要命）

**pant**
英 /pænt/ 美 /pænt/
v. 喘息；渴望
n. 喘息
[义] To breathe quickly with short breaths, usually after exercise; to want something very much. 急促短喘（pant for breath 喘不上气，panting after the run 跑完后直喘）；亦指「渴望」（pant for success 渴望成功）。狗 pant 为正常散热。
[例] ① She was panting by the time she reached the top of the stairs. 爬到楼梯顶时她已气喘吁吁。 ② The dog panted in the heat, tongue hanging out. 狗热得吐着舌头直喘。
[搭] pant for breath 喘不上气, out of breath and panting 气喘吁吁, pant after 渴望, heavy panting 剧烈喘息
[联] gasp 倒吸气, breathe 呼吸, puff 喘息, run 跑步, exhausted 精疲力竭, dog 狗
[记] pant 与 gasp 不同：pant 为连续短喘；gasp 为突然大口吸。pant 作名词可数

**clatter**
英 /ˈklætə/ 美 /ˈklætər/
v. 哐当响；嗒嗒响
n. 哐当声
[义] To make repeated short loud noises, especially hard objects hitting each other. 硬物反复碰撞的哗啦/嗒嗒声（dishes clattered 盘子哐当，heels clattered 高跟鞋嗒嗒）。比 rattle 更「脆、乱、多物」。
[例] ① Pots and pans clattered as he unpacked the kitchen boxes. 他拆厨房箱子时锅碗瓢盆哐当乱响。 ② Her heels clattered down the corridor. 她的高跟鞋在走廊里嗒嗒作响。
[搭] clatter of dishes 盘碗哐当, clatter down 嗒嗒走下, clatter about 哐当乱响
[联] rattle 嘎嘎响, bang 砰, crash 撞击, heels 高跟鞋, dishes 盘碗, noise 噪音
[记] clatter 作名词不可数居多；a clatter of 常见。clatter of hooves 马蹄嗒嗒

**rattle**
英 /ˈrætl/ 美 /ˈrætl/
v. 嘎嘎响；使紧张
n. 嘎嘎声；拨浪鼓
[义] To make a series of short loud sounds when shaken or moved; to make someone nervous. 连续短促的嘎嘎声（windows rattled 窗户嘎嘎响）；喻「使慌乱」（don't let them rattle you 别被他们搅乱阵脚）。婴儿 rattle 指拨浪鼓。
[例] ① The train rattled over the old bridge. 火车驶过旧桥时嘎嘎作响。 ② Don't let one bad review rattle your confidence. 别因为一条差评就乱了信心。
[搭] rattle around 哐当晃, windows rattle 窗户响, rattle sb.'s nerves 使某人紧张, rattle off 一口气背出
[联] clatter 哐当, shake 摇晃, bang 砰, window 窗户, nerves 神经, list 清单
[记] rattle off 为另一义「一口气说/背出」。rattle 与 rattlesnake（响尾蛇）同根

**jingle**
英 /ˈdʒɪŋɡl/ 美 /ˈdʒɪŋɡl/
v. 叮当作响
n. 叮当声；短广告歌
[义] To make a light ringing sound, especially metal or coins; a short catchy song used in ads. 金属、硬币等轻脆叮当（keys jingled 钥匙叮当）；名词亦指「广告短歌」（Christmas jingle 圣诞广告歌）。
[例] ① Coins jingled in his pocket as he walked. 走路时口袋里硬币叮当作响。 ② That insurance jingle has been stuck in my head all day. 那首保险广告歌在我脑子里响了一整天。
[搭] jingle of coins/keys 硬币/钥匙叮当, advertising jingle 广告歌, jingle bells 铃儿响叮当
[联] chime 钟声, ring 响, coin 硬币, bells 铃, melody 旋律, ad 广告
[记] Jingle Bells 为固定歌名。jingle 作广告歌为美式营销常用词

**chime**
英 /tʃaɪm/ 美 /tʃaɪm/
v. 鸣响；一致
n. 钟声
[义] To ring bells, especially in a harmonious series; to agree. 钟、铃和谐鸣响（clocks chimed 钟鸣）；喻「与……一致」（chime with my views 与我的看法一致）。比 ring 更偏「成套钟声、悦耳」。
[例] ① The church clock chimed midnight. 教堂钟敲了午夜十二点。 ② Her comments chimed with what we had been thinking. 她的说法与我们一直在想的不谋而合。
[搭] chime with 与……一致, clock chimed 钟鸣, chime in 插话（口语）, chime of bells 钟声
[联] ring 响, bell 钟, jingle 叮当, agree 一致, midnight 午夜, harmony 和谐
[记] chime in 口语表「插嘴加入讨论」。chime with 书面/口语均常见

**blare**
英 /bleə/ 美 /bler/
v. 轰鸣；高声播放
n. 刺耳的响
[义] To make a loud unpleasant noise; to play music or announcements very loudly. 刺耳大声（horns blared 喇叭齐鸣，music blaring 音乐震天）。比 loud 更强调「刺、压、不容忽视」。
[例] ① Car horns blared in the traffic jam. 堵车时汽车喇叭一片轰鸣。 ② Music was blaring from the shop speakers. 商店喇叭里音乐震天响。
[搭] blare out 大声播出, horns blared 喇叭齐鸣, music blaring 音乐轰鸣, blare of trumpets 号角齐鸣
[联] deafening 震耳欲聋, loud 大声, horn 喇叭, traffic 交通, speaker 喇叭, intrusive 扰人的
[记] blare 几乎总含负面「太响、太 intrusive」。blaring music/TV 为常见搭配

**wail**
英 /weɪl/ 美 /weɪl/
v. 嚎哭；尖声长鸣
n. 嚎哭；哀号
[义] To cry or make a long high sound in pain or grief; a siren wailing. 长而高的哭号或鸣叫（baby wailing 婴儿嚎哭，sirens wailed 警笛长鸣）。比 cry 更长、更高、更惨。
[例] ① The baby wailed until she was picked up. 宝宝被抱起来之前一直嚎哭。 ② Ambulance sirens wailed in the distance. 远处救护车警笛长鸣。
[搭] wail of sirens 警笛长鸣, wail in pain 痛得哀号, baby wailing 婴儿嚎哭
[联] cry 哭, moan 呻吟, scream 尖叫, siren 警笛, grief 悲痛, howl 嚎
[记] wail 含强烈痛苦或警报义。the wail of a siren 新闻常用

**moan**
英 /məʊn/ 美 /moʊn/
v. 呻吟；抱怨
n. 呻吟
[义] To make a long low sound of pain, pleasure, or complaint; to complain. 因痛或快感的低长声（moan in pain 痛得呻吟）；或口语抱怨（moan about work 抱怨工作）。比 groan 更「拖、低、诉」。
[例] ① He moaned and clutched his stomach after eating the bad seafood. 吃了不新鲜海鲜后他捂着肚子呻吟。 ② Everyone moans about the price of rent, but few move out. 人人都抱怨房租贵，但很少人搬走。
[搭] moan in pain 痛得呻吟, moan about 抱怨, low moan 低声呻吟, moan and groan 呻吟抱怨
[联] groan 深沉呻吟, whine 哀诉, complain 抱怨, pain 痛, sigh 叹息, ache 疼痛
[记] moan and groan 固定搭配。moan about 口语极频

**groan**
英 /ɡrəʊn/ 美 /ɡroʊn/
v. 呻吟；嘎吱作响
n. 呻吟；嘎吱声
[义] To make a deep sound because of pain, despair, or effort; to creak under weight. 因痛、绝望或用力发出的低沉长声（groan with effort 用力时哼出声）；或物体承重时「嘎吱作响」（shelf groaned under books 书架被书压得吱呀响）。
[例] ① The audience groaned when the speaker ran overtime again. 演讲者又拖堂，观众一片呻吟/嘘声。 ② The old floorboards groaned under his weight. 旧地板在他的重量下嘎吱作响。
[搭] groan with pain/effort 痛/用力时呻吟, groan under 在……下嘎吱响, audience groaned 观众一片嘘声
[联] moan 呻吟, creak 嘎吱, sigh 叹息, pain 痛, effort 用力, weight 重量
[记] groan 可指人声或物体声。audience groan 表「不满的呻吟/嘘声」

**snort**
英 /snɔːt/ 美 /snɔːrt/
v. 喷鼻息；哼笑
n. 喷鼻声
[义] To breathe air noisily through the nose, especially to show amusement or contempt; animals snort. 鼻子里出声——可表轻蔑、觉得好笑（snort with laughter 哼笑），或马、猪等喷鼻息。
[例] ① She snorted when he claimed he had never been late. 他说自己从没迟到时，她哼了一声。 ② The horse snorted and tossed its head. 马喷了个鼻息，甩了甩头。
[搭] snort with laughter 哼笑, snort at 对……嗤之以鼻, snort of disbelief 不信的鼻息
[联] sniff 嗅, laugh 笑, scoff 嘲笑, horse 马, contempt 轻蔑, pig 猪
[记] snort 表轻蔑/不信时极口语。snort cocaine 为另一义（俚语毒品），依语境区分

**burp**
英 /bɜːp/ 美 /bɜːrp/
v. 打嗝（饱嗝）
n. 饱嗝
[义] To release gas from the stomach through the mouth (informal). 饭后从胃部返气打出的嗝（burp the baby 给宝宝拍嗝）。比 hiccup 不同：burp 为饱嗝；hiccup 为膈肌痉挛。英式亦 belch，略粗。
[例] ① He burped loudly after finishing the soda. 喝完汽水他大声打了个嗝。 ② She burped the baby gently after feeding. 喂完奶她轻轻给宝宝拍嗝。
[搭] burp loudly 大声打嗝, burp the baby 给宝宝拍嗝, excuse me after a burp 打嗝后说 excuse me
[联] hiccup 膈肌打嗝, belch 饱嗝, stomach 胃, baby 婴儿, feed 喂奶, soda 汽水
[记] burp 美式口语；belch 略不礼貌。bring up wind 英式指婴儿拍嗝

**nauseous**
英 /ˈnɔːzɪəs/ 美 /ˈnɔːʃəs/
adj. 恶心的；令人作呕的
[义] Feeling as if you are going to vomit; causing nausea. 感到想吐（feel nauseous 觉得恶心）；或「令人 nauseous」。口语常混用 nauseated（感到恶心的人）与 nauseous（引起恶心的事物）——正式写作中 feel nauseated 更严。
[例] ① I felt nauseous during the rough ferry crossing. 渡轮颠簸，我觉得恶心。 ② The smell from the bin was absolutely nauseous. 垃圾桶里的气味简直令人作呕。
[搭] feel nauseous/nauseated 感到恶心, nauseous smell 恶臭, slightly nauseous 有点恶心
[联] nauseating 令人作呕的, sick 想吐的, vomit 呕吐, seasick 晕船, smell 气味, stomach 胃
[记] 用法争议：传统语法 nauseous=「引起恶心的」，nauseated=「感到恶心」；美式口语 feel nauseous 极常见

**sickening**
英 /ˈsɪkənɪŋ/ 美 /ˈsɪkənɪŋ/
adj. 令人作呕的；令人震惊的
[义] Making you feel sick or shocked and disgusted. 生理或道德上「让人 sick」（sickening crash 惨烈的撞车，sickening violence 令人发指的暴力）。比 disgusting 更强调「冲击、反胃感」。
[例] ① The sickening crunch of metal could be heard from the street. 街上都能听到金属撞得令人发毛的声音。 ② It is sickening to see food wasted like this. 这样浪费食物令人痛心。
[搭] sickening crash 惨烈撞击, sickening violence 令人发指的暴力, sickening smell 恶臭
[联] nauseating 令人作呕的, disgusting 令人厌恶的, shocking 令人震惊的, crash 撞击, violence 暴力, smell 气味
[记] sickening 可修饰 smell, thud, violence, waste。sicken 动词「使恶心/使震惊」

**overpowering**
英 /ˌəʊvəˈpaʊərɪŋ/ 美 /ˌoʊvərˈpaʊərɪŋ/
adj. 强烈到难以抵挡的
[义] Too strong to fight or resist, especially a smell, taste, or emotion. 强到压倒一切——尤指气味、味道或情感（overpowering smell 浓烈冲鼻，overpowering grief 难以承受的悲痛）。
[例] ① An overpowering smell of perfume hit me as I opened the door. 一开门，浓烈的香水味扑面而来。 ② She was struck by an overpowering sense of déjà vu. 一阵强烈的既视感袭来。
[搭] overpowering smell/perfume 浓烈气味/香水, overpowering urge 难以抗拒的冲动, overpowering emotion 难以承受的情绪
[联] overwhelming 压倒性的, strong 强烈的, pungent 刺鼻, intense 强烈的, smell 气味, grief 悲痛
[记] overpowering 与 overwhelming 近义；overpowering 更常用于 smell/taste/perfume

**insensitive**
英 /ɪnˈsensətɪv/ 美 /ɪnˈsensətɪv/
adj. 不敏感的；感觉迟钝的
[义] Not aware of or able to respond to other people's feelings; not sensitive to physical stimuli. 对他人体谅（insensitive remark 欠考虑的话）；或感官「迟钝、不敏感」（insensitive to pain 对痛不敏感）。in-（不）+ sensitive。
[例] ① His insensitive joke upset several colleagues. 他欠考虑的玩笑让几位同事不高兴。 ② The nerve was damaged and insensitive to touch. 神经受损，对触摸没有感觉。
[搭] insensitive to 对……不敏感, insensitive remark 欠考虑的话, culturally insensitive 文化上不敏感
[联] sensitive 敏感的, numb 麻木的, rude 粗鲁的, touch 触摸, pain 痛, awareness 意识
[记] insensitive 人际义极常见；医学义 insensitive to pain/light。与 insensible（失去知觉）不同词

**receptive**
英 /rɪˈseptɪv/ 美 /rɪˈseptɪv/
adj. 乐于接受的；感受性强的
[义] Willing to listen to or accept new ideas; able to receive sensory input. 对新思想乐于接纳（receptive to feedback 乐于接受反馈）；或感官/身体「有感受性」（receptive field 感受野——生物/心理术语）。较 accept 更强调「处于可接收状态」。
[例] ① Students were more receptive to the idea after the demo. 演示之后，学生更接受这个想法了。 ② The patient remained receptive to light touch in the injured hand. 伤者手对轻触仍有感受。
[搭] receptive to 对……乐于接受, receptive audience 乐于接受的观众, receptive field 感受野
[联] responsive 反应灵敏的, open 开放的, sensitive 敏感的, feedback 反馈, accept 接受, audience 观众
[记] receptive to ideas/feedback/suggestions 为常见框架。receptive 偏「乐于接纳的状态」

**responsive**
英 /rɪˈspɒnsɪv/ 美 /rɪˈspɑːnsɪv/
adj. 反应灵敏的；回应迅速的
[义] Reacting quickly and positively; answering. 对刺激或请求反应快（responsive design 响应式设计；responsive patient 对 treatment 有反应的病人）。比 receptive 更强调「已有反应、有回应」。
[例] ① The support team was responsive and fixed the bug within hours. 支持团队反应很快，几小时内修好了 bug。 ② The skin became less responsive as the nerve damage worsened. 神经损伤加重，皮肤反应变弱。
[搭] responsive to 对……有反应, responsive design 响应式设计, highly responsive 反应灵敏, responsive audience 反应热烈的观众
[联] receptive 乐于接受的, sensitive 敏感的, quick 迅速的, react 反应, feedback 反馈, patient 病人
[记] responsive vs. receptive：responsive 强调「有反应」；receptive 强调「愿意接收」。tech 语境 responsive design 指「自适应布局」

**cue**
英 /kjuː/ 美 /kjuː/
n. 暗示；信号；提示
v. 给提示；示意开始
[义] A signal for action, especially in performance; something that helps you understand or remember. 表演中的 cues（on cue 准时）；感官/社交上的「线索」（visual cues 视觉线索，social cues 社交信号）。动词 cue sb. 表「示意开始」。
[例] ① She entered on cue when the lights dimmed. 灯光暗下时她准时登场。 ② Babies pick up social cues from their caregivers' faces. 婴儿从照护者的表情读取社交信号。
[搭] on cue 准时, visual/social cues 视觉/社交线索, cue sb. to do 示意某人做, take one's cue from 以……为榜样
[联] signal 信号, hint 暗示, clue 线索, prompt 提示, stage 舞台, facial expression 面部表情
[记] on cue 固定短语。take a cue from sb. 表「学某人的做法」。cue 与 queue（队列）同音异义

**clang**
英 /klæŋ/ 美 /klæŋ/
v. 铛铛响
n. 铛的一声
[义] To make a loud ringing metallic sound. 金属撞击的洪亮 ringing 声（gates clanged 铁门铛铛，clang of swords 剑击铛鸣）。单音节拟声感强。
[例] ① The prison gates clanged shut behind him. 监狱铁门在他身后铛地关上。 ② We heard the clang of pots from the kitchen. 我们听见厨房里锅子铛铛响。
[搭] clang shut 铛地关上, clang of metal 金属撞击声, gates clanged 铁门响
[联] clatter 哐当, bang 砰, metal 金属, gate 门, sword 剑, echo 回声
[记] clang 多指金属单声或反复撞击。clang of the trolley 电车铛声（文学）

**thud**
英 /θʌd/ 美 /θʌd/
v. 砰的一声落下
n. 闷响
[义] To hit something with a low dull sound; such a sound. 重物落地的闷响（book thudded 书砰地落下，thud of footsteps 沉重的脚步声）。比 bang 更低沉、不脆。
[例] ① The bag thudded onto the floor. 包砰的一声落在地上。 ② She heard the thud of footsteps upstairs. 她听见楼上沉重的脚步声。
[搭] thud onto 砰地落在, thud of footsteps 沉重的脚步声, dull thud 沉闷的撞击声, heart thudding 心怦怦跳
[联] bang 砰, crash 撞击, thump 重击, fall 落下, dull 沉闷的, heartbeat 心跳
[记] thud 拟声；heart thudding 描写紧张常用。thud vs. thump：thud 更闷

**thump**
英 /θʌmp/ 美 /θʌmp/
v. 砰砰响；重击
n. 重击声；闷响
[义] To hit something heavily, making a dull sound; the sound or sensation of a heavy beat. 重重击打或心跳（thump on the door 砰砰敲门，heart thumping 心砰砰跳）。比 thud 可更强调「反复、用力」。
[例] ① Someone was thumping on the door late at night. 深夜有人砰砰敲门。 ② My heart was thumping as I waited for the results. 等结果时我心怦怦直跳。
[搭] thump on 砰砰敲, heart thumping 心怦怦跳, thump of bass 低音砰砰, thump down 重重放下
[联] thud 闷响, bang 砰, pulse 脉搏, knock 敲, heart 心, bass 低音
[记] thump 可指敲门、心跳、低音。thumping headache 英式口语「 pounding 头痛」

**ringing**
英 /ˈrɪŋɪŋ/ 美 /ˈrɪŋɪŋ/
adj. 鸣响的；刺耳的
n. 耳鸣；鸣响
[义] Making a clear resonant sound; a high continued sound in the ears after noise. 清亮、回荡的鸣响声（ringing applause 掌声雷动）；或「耳鸣」（a ringing in my ears 耳朵嗡嗡响）。
[例] ① There was ringing applause when she finished the solo. 独奏结束时掌声雷动。 ② After the concert, I had a ringing in my ears for an hour. 音乐会后我耳鸣了一小时。
[搭] ringing in one's ears 耳鸣, ringing applause 掌声雷动, ringing endorsement 响亮背书, clear ringing tone 清脆的鸣响声
[联] echo 回声, buzz 嗡嗡, deafening 震耳, applause 掌声, ears 耳朵, tinnitus 耳鸣
[记] ringing in the ears = tinnitus 的通俗说法。ringing endorsement 喻「响亮背书/强烈推荐」

**unclear**
英 /ʌnˈklɪə/ 美 /ʌnˈklɪr/
adj. 不清楚的；含糊的
[义] Not easy to see, hear, or understand. 看不清楚、听不明白或理解不了（unclear instructions 含糊的说明，unclear photo 模糊的照片）。un- + clear；与 vague 比更偏「信息/感知输入质量差」。
[例] ① The recording was unclear, so we couldn't identify the voice. 录音不清楚，我们听不出是谁的声音。 ② It remains unclear who will lead the team next year. 明年谁带队仍不清楚。
[搭] remain unclear 仍不清楚, unclear instructions 含糊说明, unclear photo/image 模糊图像, sound unclear 听不清楚
[联] vague 模糊的, clear 清楚的, blurry 模糊的, muffled 闷声的, obscure 晦涩的, confusion 混乱
[记] unclear 可修饰 sound, image, message, responsibility。It is unclear whether... 新闻/学术常用

**synesthesia**
英 /ˌsɪnəsˈiːziə/ 美 /ˌsɪnəsˈiːʒə/
n. 联觉
> 美式亦拼 synesthesia /ˌsɪnəsˈiːʒə/
[义] A condition in which one sense triggers another, e.g. seeing colors when hearing music. 一种感官刺激自动引发另一种感官体验——如「听到音乐看见颜色」。属神经/心理通用术语，非日常自诊词。
[例] ① People with synesthesia might associate letters with specific colors. 有联觉的人可能把字母和特定颜色联系起来。 ② The novel describes music in terms usually reserved for synesthesia. 小说用近乎联觉的方式描写音乐。
[源] 希腊语 *syn-*（共同）+ *aisthēsis*（感觉）→ 感官联通。
[搭] synesthesia experience 联觉体验, grapheme-color synesthesia 字母-颜色联觉, synesthesia research 联觉研究
