[联] sensory 感官的, perception 感知, visual 视觉的, auditory 听觉的, color 颜色, music 音乐
[记] 英式常拼 synaesthesia。属少数神经现象，科普与文学常见；与 metaphor 不同——联觉为真实感知体验

**glimmer**
英 /ˈɡlɪmə/ 美 /ˈɡlɪmər/
v. 闪烁；隐约显现
n. 微光；一丝
[义] To shine faintly with a wavering light; a faint sign of something. 微弱、摇曳的光（candles glimmered 烛光摇曳）；喻「一线希望/迹象」（a glimmer of hope 一线希望）。比 gleam 更弱、更不稳；比 glitter 少「许多光点乱闪」。
[例] ① Lights glimmered on the far side of the lake. 湖对岸灯光隐约闪烁。 ② After weeks of bad news, we finally saw a glimmer of hope. 连番坏消息后，我们终于看到一线希望。
[搭] glimmer of hope/light 一线希望/微光, glimmer in the distance 远处闪烁, faint glimmer 微弱闪光
[联] gleam 微光, flicker 闪烁, sparkle 闪耀, hope 希望, candle 蜡烛, dawn 黎明
[记] glimmer 作名词可数（a glimmer）。not a glimmer of 表「丝毫没有（希望/证据）」

**shadow**
英 /ˈʃædəʊ/ 美 /ˈʃædoʊ/
n. 影子；阴影
v. 投下阴影；跟踪
[义] A dark shape on a surface where light is blocked; partial darkness. 光被挡住时在表面形成的暗形（cast a shadow 投下影子，in the shadow of 在……阴影下）；动词亦可指「跟踪观察」（shadow sb. 盯梢）。本章取视觉感知义；政治义 shadow cabinet 见他章。
[例] ① The tree cast a long shadow across the path. 树在路面上投下长长的影子。 ② She stepped out of the shadow into the sunlight. 她从阴影里走到阳光下。
[搭] cast a shadow 投下影子, in the shadow of 在……阴影下, shadow fell across 阴影掠过, live in the shadow of 活在……阴影下
[联] shade 荫凉, silhouette 剪影, sunlight 阳光, dark 黑暗, outline 轮廓, follow 跟踪
[记] shadow 与 shade 近义：shadow 偏「被挡光形成的形」；shade 偏「荫、凉处」。动词 shadow 在 journalism/培训中指「跟访学习」

**screech**
英 /skriːtʃ/ 美 /skriːtʃ/
v. 尖叫；刺耳地响
n. 尖叫声
[义] To make a loud high unpleasant sound; brakes or voices screeching. 又高又刺的叫声或摩擦声（brakes screeched 刹车尖叫，screech with laughter 尖声大笑）。比 scream 更强调「尖、刮、刺耳」。
[例] ① The car screeched to a halt in front of the house. 车在房前吱——地刹停。 ② A screech of feedback burst from the microphone. 麦克风里爆出一声刺耳的啸叫。
[搭] screech to a halt 吱地刹停, screech of brakes 刹车尖叫, screech with laughter 尖声大笑, ear-splitting screech 震耳尖叫
[联] scream 尖叫, shrill 尖厉的, piercing 刺耳的, brake 刹车, microphone 麦克风, tires 轮胎
[记] screech 几乎总含 unpleasant 刺耳色彩。screech owl 为角鸮类鸟名，日常识读即可

**rustle**
英 /ˈrʌsl/ 美 /ˈrʌsl/
v. 沙沙响；窸窣
n. 沙沙声
[义] To make a soft repeated noise, like paper, leaves, or silk moving. 纸、叶、绸等轻而连续的摩擦声（leaves rustled 树叶沙沙，rustle papers 翻纸窸窣）。比 crackle 更柔、更密。
[例] ① I heard the rustle of pages as she turned the book. 她翻书时我听见纸页沙沙响。 ② Wind rustled through the cornfields. 风过玉米地，一片窸窣。
[搭] rustle of leaves/pages 叶/纸沙沙声, rustle through 穿过时沙沙响, soft rustle 轻柔的窸窣
[联] murmur 低语, whisper 低语, leaves 树叶, paper 纸, wind 风, silk 丝绸
[记] rustle 作动词不及物居多。rustle up 为另一义「匆匆弄出（食物）」，与感知义无关

**cacophony**
英 /kəˈkɒfəni/ 美 /kəˈkɑːfəni/
n. 刺耳的嘈杂声
[义] A harsh mixture of loud unpleasant sounds. 多种难听声音混在一起（cacophony of horns 喇叭齐鸣，urban cacophony 城市喧嚣）。与 symphony 相对——cacophony 强调「乱、刺、不和谐」。
[例] ① A cacophony of car horns greeted us at the intersection. 路口一片汽车喇叭的刺耳嘈杂。 ② The cacophony from the construction site made it hard to think. 工地嘈杂刺耳，让人难以思考。
[源] 希腊语 *kakos*（坏）+ *phōnē*（声音）→ 坏声音、不和谐。
[搭] cacophony of horns/voices 喇叭/人声嘈杂, urban cacophony 城市喧嚣, rise above the cacophony 在嘈杂中突出
[联] din 喧嚣, noise 噪音, deafening 震耳, symphony 交响乐, harmony 和谐, clash 冲突
[记] cacophony 较书面；口语常说 a mess of noise。形容词 cacophonous

**din**
英 /dɪn/ 美 /dɪn/
n. 喧嚣；吵闹声
v. 喧闹；反复灌输
[义] A loud unpleasant noise that continues for a time. 持续、闹人的响（the din of the crowd 人群喧嚣，make a din 大吵大闹）。比 noise 更强调「久、乱、烦」；动词 din into 表「反复灌输」。
[例] ① We could barely hear each other over the din of the festival. 节日喧闹声中我们几乎听不清彼此说话。 ② The din from the kitchen woke the baby. 厨房里的吵闹声把宝宝吵醒了。
[搭] din of the crowd/traffic 人群/交通喧嚣, make a din 大吵大闹, over the din 在喧闹声中, din into 反复灌输
[联] noise 噪音, cacophony 刺耳嘈杂, loud 大声, crowd 人群, racket 吵闹（口语）, silence 安静
[记] din 较英式书面/文学；美式口语 racket 更常见表「吵闹」。din into sb.'s ears 表「唠叨灌输」

**fetid**
英 /ˈfetɪd/ 美 /ˈfetɪd/
adj. 恶臭的
[义] Smelling extremely unpleasant and stale. 腐臭、闷臭（fetid swamp 恶臭沼泽，fetid breath 口臭）。比 smelly 更正式、更强调「腐、馊、令人作呕」。
[例] ① A fetid smell rose from the blocked drain. 堵塞的下水道升起一股恶臭。 ② The room had been closed for weeks and smelled fetid. 房间关了好几周，气味腐臭。
[搭] fetid smell/odor 恶臭, fetid air 臭气, fetid swamp 恶臭沼泽
[联] stench 恶臭, reek 发臭, rotten 腐烂的, musty 霉味的, acrid 刺鼻的, smell 气味
[记] fetid 较书面；日常 stinking / smelly 更口语。与 feted（被宴请的）拼写勿混

**monotone**
英 /ˈmɒnətəʊn/ 美 /ˈmɑːnətoʊn/
n. 单调；单调音
adj. 单调的
[义] A sound or voice without variation in pitch; dull sameness. 音高几乎不变的说话或声响（speak in a monotone 单调地念，monotone buzz 单调的嗡嗡声）。听觉上「平、无起伏、易犯困」。
[例] ① He read the report in a monotone that put half the room to sleep. 他用单调的语调念报告，半个房间的人都快睡着了。 ② The monotone of the engine lulled her into a daze. 引擎一成不变的轰鸣让她昏昏欲睡。
[搭] speak in a monotone 单调地说话, dull monotone 沉闷单调, monotone voice 单调嗓音
[联] dull 沉闷的, boring 乏味的, flat 平淡的, drone 嗡鸣, hum 嗡嗡, pitch 音高
[记] monotone 名词/形容词均可。monotonous 形容词更常见；monotone 偏描述「单一音高模式」

**shimmer**
英 /ˈʃɪmə/ 美 /ˈʃɪmər/
v. 闪烁
n. 微光
[义] To shine with a soft, slightly wavering light. 柔和、轻轻晃动的反光（heat shimmer 热浪里地面闪动，shimmering water 波光粼粼）。比 glimmer 略强、面积感更大；比 glitter 少「许多硬光点乱闪」。
[例] ① Heat shimmer rose from the asphalt on the hot afternoon. 炎热午后，沥青路面上方热浪闪动。 ② Her silk dress shimmered under the stage lights. 她的丝质礼服在舞台灯光下闪烁。
[搭] heat shimmer 热霾闪动, shimmer in the light 在光下闪烁, shimmering water 波光粼粼
[联] glimmer 微光, gleam 闪光, flicker 闪烁, sparkle 闪耀, heat 热, silk 丝绸
[记] shimmer 几乎总褒义/中性，描写水面、织物、热霾。形容词 shimmering 更常见，见属性章；本章取动词/名词感知义

**squeal**
英 /skwiːl/ 美 /skwiːl/
v. 尖叫；吱声
n. 尖叫声
[义] To make a long high cry or sound. 长而尖的叫声——可表兴奋（squeal with delight 高兴得尖叫）、疼痛、或刹车/轮胎吱声（brakes squealed 刹车尖叫）。比 screech 略短、可带「喜」色。
[例] ① The children squealed when they saw the puppy. 孩子们看见小狗高兴得尖叫起来。 ② The car squealed to a stop at the red light. 车在红灯前吱——地刹停。
[搭] squeal with delight 高兴尖叫, squeal to a stop 吱地停住, squeal of brakes 刹车尖叫, high-pitched squeal 尖声
[联] screech 刺耳尖叫, shrill 尖厉的, delight 喜悦, brake 刹车, tires 轮胎, pig 猪
[记] squeal 可褒（兴奋）可贬（轮胎、猪）。squeal on sb. 为另一义「告密」，依语境区分

**creak**
英 /kriːk/ 美 /kriːk/
v. 嘎吱响
n. 嘎吱声
[义] To make a long high sound when something moves slowly, especially wood or an old door. 缓慢移动时发出的长而尖的摩擦声——尤指木门、旧地板（door creaked 门吱呀，floorboards creak 地板嘎吱）。比 crack 更「拖、老、慢」。
[例] ① The floorboards creaked as he tiptoed down the hall. 他踮脚走过走廊，地板嘎吱作响。 ② Every step made the rusty gate creak. 每走一步，生锈的铁门就嘎吱一声。
[搭] creak open 吱呀打开, floorboards creak 地板嘎吱, creak of a door 门吱呀声, rusty creak 生锈的嘎吱
[联] groan 呻吟/承重嘎吱, crack 碎裂声, door 门, floorboard 地板, old 旧的, hinge 铰链
[记] creak 拟声感强；恐怖片里 creaking door 极常见。creak vs. groan：creak 偏「摩擦尖声」；groan 更低沉

**tinkle**
英 /ˈtɪŋkl/ 美 /ˈtɪŋkl/
v. 叮当作响
n. 叮当声
[义] To make a light, clear ringing sound, like small bells or glass. 小铃、玻璃杯等轻脆、清亮的叮当（wind chimes tinkled 风铃叮当，tinkle of laughter 银铃般的笑声）。比 jingle 更「细、脆、单音短」；比 chime 更轻。
[例] ① Wind chimes tinkled on the porch in the breeze. 微风里门廊风铃叮当作响。 ② I heard the tinkle of glasses as they toasted. 听见碰杯时玻璃杯叮当轻响。
[搭] tinkle of bells/glass 铃/玻璃叮当, wind chimes tinkle 风铃响, tinkle with laughter 笑声清脆
[联] jingle 叮当, chime 钟鸣, ring 响, bell 铃, glass 玻璃, laughter 笑声
[记] tinkle 常描写风铃、杯盏、轻笑。英式 tinkle 俚语亦指「小便」，正式写作须依语境

**hoarse**
英 /hɔːs/ 美 /hɔːrs/
adj. 嘶哑的
[义] If your voice is hoarse, it sounds rough and unclear, often from a cold or shouting. 嗓音粗哑、不清（hoarse voice 哑嗓子，speak in a hoarse whisper 嘶哑低语）。感冒、喊叫、吸烟后常见；比 husky 更强调「病/损/沙」。
[例] ① After the concert, the singer's voice was hoarse but happy. 演唱会后歌手嗓子哑了，但很开心。 ② He could only manage a hoarse croak after shouting at the game. 看球时喊太多，他只能发出嘶哑的嘎声。
[搭] hoarse voice 哑嗓子, grow hoarse 喊哑, hoarse from shouting 喊哑的, hoarse whisper 嘶哑低语
[联] husky 沙哑的, croak 嘶哑地说, throat 喉咙, cold 感冒, shout 喊叫, whisper 低语
[记] grow hoarse / be hoarse from... 极常用。hoarse 只作形容词；名词 hoarseness

**grating**
英 /ˈɡreɪtɪŋ/ 美 /ˈɡreɪtɪŋ/
adj. 刺耳的；令人烦躁的
n. 格栅（另义）
[义] Unpleasantly harsh or sharp to hear; annoying. 听起来刮耳、刺人、烦（grating voice 刺耳嗓音，grating sound 刮擦声）。本章取听觉/心理感知义；名词 grating 亦指「格栅」，依语境区分。
[例] ① His grating laugh made everyone in the room wince. 他那刺耳的笑声让在场的人都皱起了眉。 ② The grating scrape of metal on metal lasted only a second. 金属刮擦金属的刺耳声只持续了一秒。
[搭] grating voice/laugh 刺耳的嗓音/笑声, grating sound 刮擦声, grating on the nerves 令人心烦
[联] harsh 刺耳的, shrill 尖厉的, piercing 刺耳的, scrape 刮擦, nerves 神经, annoying 烦人的
[记] grating on the nerves / grating to listen to 为常见框架。grate 动词「擦响；惹恼」：The noise grates on me.

**tickle**
英 /ˈtɪkl/ 美 /ˈtɪkl/
v. 挠痒；使发痒
n. 痒感
[义] To touch someone lightly and make them laugh or feel an itchy sensation. 轻触皮肤引起痒笑（tickle sb.'s feet 挠脚心）；亦指「轻触即痒」（a fabric that tickles 扎人的织物）。与 tingle 不同：tickle 需外力轻触、常伴笑；tingle 为自发刺麻。
[例] ① The kitten tickled my arm with its whiskers. 小猫用胡子蹭得我胳膊发痒。 ② This wool sweater tickles my neck. 这件羊毛毛衣扎得我脖子痒。
[搭] tickle sb.'s fancy 合某人心意（习语）, tickle one's feet 挠脚心, make sb. laugh and tickle 挠得发笑
[联] tingle 刺麻, itch 痒, scratch 挠, laugh 笑, whisker 胡子, fabric 织物
[记] tickle sb.'s fancy 习语表「正合某人口味/心意」。ticklish 见下条

**ticklish**
英 /ˈtɪklɪʃ/ 美 /ˈtɪklɪʃ/
adj. 怕痒的；棘手的
[义] Sensitive to being tickled; difficult and needing careful handling. 一挠就痒（ticklish feet 怕痒的脚）；喻「棘手、须小心」（a ticklish situation 敏感局面）。本章取身体感知义为主。
[例] ① I'm very ticklish around my ribs—please don't! 我肋骨特别怕痒——别挠！ ② Negotiating pay can be a ticklish subject at small firms. 在小公司谈薪水可能是敏感话题。
[搭] ticklish spot 怕痒处, highly ticklish 很怕痒, ticklish subject/situation 敏感话题/局面
[联] tickle 挠痒, sensitive 敏感的, ribs 肋骨, feet 脚, delicate 微妙的, awkward 尴尬的
[记] ticklish 身体义与喻义并存。ticklish problem 口语可说，但更常 delicate / tricky situation

**bleary**
英 /ˈblɪəri/ 美 /ˈblɪri/
adj. 模糊的；睡眼惺忪的
[义] Unable to see clearly, especially because you are tired or have been crying. 因疲劳、刚醒或哭过而视线模糊（bleary eyes 睡眼惺忪，bleary vision 朦胧视觉）。常作 bleary-eyed。
[例] ① She answered the door bleary-eyed at six in the morning. 早上六点她睡眼惺忪地来开门。 ② After twelve hours on screens, everything looked bleary. 盯屏十二小时后，看什么都模模糊糊。
[搭] bleary-eyed 睡眼惺忪, bleary vision 模糊视线, bleary from lack of sleep 缺觉发懵
[联] blurry 模糊的, tired 累的, sleep 睡眠, vision 视觉, squint 眯眼, tear 泪
[记] 几乎总与 eye/vision 连用；bleary-eyed 为固定搭配。与 blurry 近义：bleary 偏「人困/累/哭」；blurry 偏「图像/信号不清」

**sizzle**
英 /ˈsɪzl/ 美 /ˈsɪzl/
v. 滋滋响
n. 滋滋声
[义] To make a sound like food cooking in hot fat or water drops on a hot surface. 热油、热铁、热面上的「滋滋」声（sausages sizzling 香肠滋滋，rain sizzling on asphalt 雨点砸在沥青上滋滋）。听觉+联想「热、香、快熟」。
[例] ① We could hear the steak sizzling on the grill from the patio. 在露台就听见烤架上牛排滋滋响。 ② Raindrops sizzled as they hit the hot hood of the car. 雨点落在滚烫的车盖上滋滋作响。
[搭] sizzle on the grill 在烤架上滋滋, sizzle with heat 热得滋滋, sizzle of bacon 培根滋滋声
[联] fry 煎, grill 烤架, heat 热, bacon 培根, crackle 噼啪, aroma 香气
[记] sizzle 描写厨房极频；形容词 sizzling hot 表「滚烫」。sizzle reel 为影视术语「精彩集锦」，另义

**crunch**
英 /krʌntʃ/ 美 /krʌntʃ/
v. 嘎吱作响；咬碎
n. 嘎吱声；紧要关头
[义] To crush something hard with a loud noise; the sound of doing so. 咬碎、踩碎硬物时的嘎吱声（crunch on snow 踩雪嘎吱，crunch numbers 另义「算数」）。听觉常伴触觉——脆、硬、碎。
[例] ① Fresh snow crunched under our boots. 新雪在我们靴底下嘎吱作响。 ② She took a bite and heard a loud crunch. 她咬了一口，听见清脆的嘎吱声。
[搭] crunch on snow/leaves 踩雪/叶嘎吱, loud crunch 清脆嘎吱声, crunch of gravel 碎石嘎吱
[联] crispy 脆的, crackle 噼啪, snow 雪, gravel 碎石, bite 咬, texture 质感
[记] crunch 兼听触：crunchy 形容词见属性章。credit crunch 等为金融喻义，依语境区分

**gurgle**
英 /ˈɡɜːɡl/ 美 /ˈɡɜːrɡl/
v. 汩汩响；咯咯响
n. 汩汩声
[义] To make a hollow bubbling sound, like water flowing or a baby's stomach. 水、液体、婴儿喉中发出的汩汩/咕噜声（stream gurgled 溪水流淌，baby gurgled happily 宝宝咕噜开心）。比 bubble 更「喉/溪、柔、连续」。
[例] ① The stream gurgled over the stones behind the cabin. 小木屋后溪水淌过石头汩汩作响。 ② The baby gurgled and reached for her toy. 宝宝咕噜着伸手够玩具。
[搭] gurgle with laughter 咯咯笑, stream gurgled 溪水汩汩, gurgle in one's throat 喉咙里咕噜
[联] bubble 冒泡, stream 溪流, baby 婴儿, water 水, stomach 胃, laugh 笑
[记] gurgle 可指水声或婴儿声。gurgling stomach 表「肚子咕噜叫（饿）」

**beep**
英 /biːp/ 美 /biːp/
v. 哔哔响
n. 哔声
[义] A short high sound from electronic equipment; to make this sound. 电子设备发出的短促高音（microwave beep 微波炉哔一声，beep the horn 按喇叭哔）。比 ring 更短、更机械。
[例] ① The scanner beeped when she passed the barcode. 扫过条码时扫描仪哔了一声。 ② He beeped twice to say he had arrived. 他按了两下喇叭表示到了。
[搭] beep the horn 按喇叭, microwave beep 微波炉提示音, short beep 短哔声, beep at sb. 对某人按喇叭
[联] buzz 嗡嗡, ring 响, horn 喇叭, scanner 扫描仪, microwave 微波炉, alert 警报
[记] beep 可数；美式 horn beep / BEEP 标识常见。bleep 为同义略正式变体，广播审查亦指「哔掉脏话」

**rancid**
英 /ˈrænsɪd/ 美 /ˈrænsɪd/
adj. 哈喇的；腐臭的
[义] Smelling or tasting unpleasant because fats or oils have gone bad. 油脂氧化变质的臭——「哈喇味」（rancid butter 哈喇黄油，rancid smell 腐油耗味）。比 rotten 更专指「油/脂变败」；比 fetid 更偏厨房/食品。
[例] ① The nuts tasted rancid, so we threw the bag away. 坚果一股哈喇味，我们把整袋扔了。 ② A rancid smell came from the old cooking oil in the pan. 锅里旧油散发出腐臭的耗味。
[搭] rancid butter/oil 哈喇黄油/油, rancid smell 耗油耗味, taste rancid 吃起来哈喇
[联] rotten 腐烂的, fetid 恶臭的, stale 不新鲜的, oil 油, butter 黄油, smell 气味
[记] rancid 几乎只修饰含油脂食物或气味。go rancid / turn rancid 表「变哈喇」

**husky**
英 /ˈhʌski/ 美 /ˈhʌski/
adj. 沙哑的；低沉的
[义] (Of a voice) deep, rough, and low. 嗓音低沉、带沙砾感（a husky voice 沙哑嗓音，speak in a husky whisper 低沉低语）。常偏「磁、厚、略粗」，不一定因病；hoarse 更强调「喊哑/感冒后沙」。
[例] ① The narrator's husky voice suited the late-night radio show. 旁白沙哑的嗓音很适合深夜电台。 ② "I'm fine," he said in a husky whisper. 「我没事。」他低声说，嗓音沙哑。
[搭] husky voice/whisper 沙哑嗓音/低语, deep husky tone 低沉沙哑的声调
[联] hoarse 嘶哑的, deep 低沉的, rough 粗哑的, whisper 低语, throat 喉咙, magnetic 有磁性的
[记] husky 形容嗓音极常见；勿与 Husky（哈士奇犬）混淆。× husky laugh 可说，但更常 deep laugh

**stinging**
英 /ˈstɪŋɪŋ/ 美 /ˈstɪŋɪŋ/
adj. 刺痛的；尖刻的
[义] Causing a sharp burning pain or feeling; harsh in words. 皮肤或眼睛「蜇、刺、辣」（stinging nettles 荨麻，stinging eyes 辣眼）；亦可指言语「刺人」（stinging criticism 尖刻批评）。sting 的现在分词形容词。
[例] ① Sunscreen got in my eyes and the stinging lasted for minutes. 防晒霜进眼，刺痛持续好几分钟。 ② The report delivered a stinging rebuke to the company. 报告对公司予以尖刻谴责。
[搭] stinging pain/eyes 刺痛/辣眼, stinging criticism/rebuke 尖刻批评/斥责, stinging nettles 荨麻
[联] sting 刺痛, sharp 尖锐的, burn 灼痛, prickle 刺痒, criticism 批评, eyes 眼睛
[记] stinging 可修饰 pain, sensation, wind, remark。物理刺蜇与言语尖刻两义并存

**watery**
英 /ˈwɔːtəri/ 美 /ˈwɔːtəri/
adj. 水汪汪的；稀薄的
[义] Full of water; thin and weak in taste or appearance. 含泪「水汪汪」（watery eyes 泪汪汪）；视觉/味觉「淡、稀、无劲」（watery soup 清汤寡水，watery light 灰蒙蒙的光）。
[例] ① Her eyes were red and watery after chopping onions. 切完洋葱，她双眼发红、泪汪汪。 ② The photo looked watery and overexposed in the bright sun. 强光下照片发灰、过曝，层次很「水」。
[搭] watery eyes 泪汪汪, watery soup/gravy 清汤/稀汁, watery light 灰蒙的光, watery taste 寡淡的味道
[联] tearful 含泪的, thin 稀薄的, bland 淡而无味, eyes 眼睛, soup 汤, light 光线
[记] watery 几乎总略含负面「太稀/太淡/泪多」。× watery coffee 极口语

**splash**
英 /splæʃ/ 美 /splæʃ/
v. 溅；哗啦响
n. 溅泼声；溅迹
[义] To hit a surface noisily and send liquid flying; the sound or mark of this. 液体猛击水面或物体（splash into the pool 扑通入池，rain splashing 雨点噼啪）。听觉+视觉「溅开」一体。
[例] ① Kids splashed each other in the shallow end of the pool. 孩子们在浅水区互相泼水。 ② We heard the splash when something fell off the dock. 有东西从码头掉进水里，我们听见扑通一声。
[搭] splash of water 水花, splash into 扑通进入, make a splash 溅起水花；引起轰动（喻）, rain splashing 雨点溅落
[联] plop 扑通, drip 滴落, pool 泳池, water 水, puddle 水坑, sound 声响
[记] make a splash 习语亦表「引起关注」。splash out 英式「大手笔花钱」，与感知义无关

**drip**
英 /drɪp/ 美 /drɪp/
v. 滴下；滴答响
n. 滴；滴答声
[义] To fall in drops; to let liquid fall drop by drop; the sound of this. 液体一滴滴落下（water dripping 滴水，drip from the tap 龙头滴答）。比 splash 更「细、慢、单滴重复」。
[例] ① Water dripped from the ceiling into a bucket overnight. 一夜里天花板的水滴进桶里。 ② The steady drip of the tap kept me awake. 龙头规律的滴答声让我睡不着。
[搭] drip from 从……滴下, steady drip 持续的滴答, drip of water 水滴, drip irrigation 滴灌（专名另义）
[联] drop 滴, leak 漏, tap 龙头, puddle 水坑, sound 声响, wet 湿的
[记] dripping wet 表「湿透了」。drip 作名词可数（a drip of coffee）

**patter**
英 /ˈpætə/ 美 /ˈpætər/
n. 啪嗒声；急促语
v. 啪嗒响
[义] A quick light tapping sound, especially of rain or feet; rapid speech. 雨点、脚步等轻快密集的嗒嗒声（patter of rain 雨声啪嗒，patter of feet 脚步啪嗒）。本章取听觉义；「快嘴脱口秀」为另一常用义。
[例] ① I fell asleep to the patter of rain on the roof. 听着雨打屋顶的啪嗒声，我睡着了。 ② We heard the patter of small feet in the hallway. 我们听见走廊里小脚啪嗒啪嗒跑过。
[搭] patter of rain 雨声啪嗒, patter of feet 脚步啪嗒, gentle patter 轻柔的嗒嗒声
[联] rain 雨, drip 滴落, footsteps 脚步, tap 轻敲, roof 屋顶, sound 声响
[记] patter of rain/feet 为固定搭配。sales patter / comic patter 指「连珠快语」，依语境区分

**croak**
英 /krəʊk/ 美 /kroʊk/
v. 嘶哑地说；呱呱叫
n. 嘶哑声；蛙鸣
[义] To speak with a rough low voice; the sound a frog makes. 嗓子粗哑地说话（croak out a warning 嘶哑喊出警告）；或青蛙「呱呱」（frogs croaking 蛙鸣）。比 hoarse 更「像蛙、像卡痰」。
[例] ① He could only croak his name after shouting at the match. 看球时喊太多，他报名字都只能嘶哑出声。 ② Frogs croaked loudly after the evening rain. 晚雨过后，青蛙呱呱叫成一片。
[搭] croak out 嘶哑说出, voice croaked 嗓音嘶哑, frogs croaking 蛙鸣, hoarse croak 嘶哑的嘎声
[联] hoarse 嘶哑的, husky 沙哑的, frog 青蛙, throat 喉咙, rasp 粗嘎, voice 嗓音
[记] croak 可指人声或蛙声。× croak 作「死」的俚语（旧式），今少见

**appetizing**
英 /ˈæpɪtaɪzɪŋ/ 美 /ˈæpɪtaɪzɪŋ/
adj. 开胃的；诱人的
> 英式亦拼 appetising /əˈpetaɪzɪŋ/
[义] Stimulating appetite; looking or smelling good enough to eat. 看起来或闻起来让人想吃（appetizing aroma 诱人香气，appetizing display 橱窗里勾人食欲的摆盘）。较 tasty 更强调「还没吃，已被勾起食欲」。
[例] ① The appetizing smell of baking bread drifted from the kitchen. 厨房里飘出烤面包的诱人香气。 ② Nothing looked appetizing after a long day of travel. 奔波一天后，什么菜都引不起食欲。
[搭] appetizing smell/aroma 诱人香气, appetizing appearance 看起来好吃, less than appetizing 不太开胃
[联] mouth-watering 流口水的, aroma 香气, tasty 好吃的, hunger 饥饿, display 摆盘, smell 气味
[记] unappetizing 反义更常用（an unappetizing grey paste）。appetizer 名词「开胃菜」

**aromatic**
英 /ˌærəˈmætɪk/ 美 /ˌærəˈmætɪk/
adj. 芳香的
[义] Having a pleasant, distinctive smell. 有好闻、可辨识的香气（aromatic herbs 芳香 herb，aromatic coffee 香咖啡）。比 fragrant 略偏「复杂、料理/植物/化学品」语境；fragrant 更偏「花、淡香」。
[例] ① She added aromatic spices to the stew. 她在炖菜里加了芳香香料。 ② The shop sells aromatic oils and candles. 这家店卖香薰油和蜡烛。
[搭] aromatic herbs/spices 芳香香草/香料, aromatic coffee 香咖啡, aromatic compounds 芳香族化合物（化学另义）
[联] fragrant 芬芳的, scent 气味, aroma 芳香, spice 香料, smell 气味, herb 香草
[记] aromatic 可修饰 food, plant, oil, wine。化学 aromatic 指「芳香族」，依语境区分

**whoosh**
英 /wuːʃ/ 美 /wuːʃ/
n. 呼的一声
v. 呼啸而过
[义] A sudden rushing sound, like air or water moving fast. 空气、水快速移动时的「呼——」声（whoosh of wind 风呼啸，whoosh past 呼啸而过）。拟声感强，口语书面均可。
[例] ① The subway train arrived with a whoosh of air. 地铁进站，带出一阵呼的气流声。 ② A bird whooshed past my ear and vanished into the trees. 一只鸟呼地从我耳边掠过，钻进树丛。
[搭] whoosh of air/wind 气流/风呼啸, whoosh past 呼啸而过, with a whoosh 呼的一声
[联] rush 猛冲, wind 风, swoosh 嗖, speed 速度, subway 地铁, sound 声响
[记] whoosh 可数名词；动词 whoosh 不及物居多。swoosh 近义，品牌 Nike Swoosh 另指标志

**wheeze**
英 /wiːz/ 美 /wiːz/
v. 喘鸣
n. 喘鸣声
[义] To breathe noisily through partly blocked airways; such a sound. 气道不畅时的「呼哧、喘鸣」（wheeze and cough 喘鸣咳嗽，asthma wheeze 哮喘喘鸣）。比 pant 更「窄、哨、病」。
[例] ① He wheezed slightly when he climbed the stairs. 爬楼梯时他微微喘鸣。 ② The doctor listened for wheezes in the child's chest. 医生听孩子胸腔有没有喘鸣音。
[搭] wheeze and cough 喘鸣咳嗽, asthma wheeze 哮喘喘鸣, wheeze with effort 费力喘鸣
[联] pant 喘息, gasp 喘气, asthma 哮喘, breath 呼吸, cough 咳嗽, chest 胸腔
[记] wheeze 常提示呼吸道问题；英式口语 old wheeze 指「老笑话/老把戏」，另义

**putrid**
英 /ˈpjuːtrɪd/ 美 /ˈpjuːtrɪd/
adj. 腐烂恶臭的
[义] Decaying and smelling extremely unpleasant. 腐烂发臭（putrid smell 腐臭，putrid meat 腐肉）。比 rotten 更强调「臭到令人作呕」；比 fetid 更偏「正在腐烂的有机物」。
[例] ① A putrid smell led rescuers to the abandoned fridge. 一股腐臭把救援人员引到废弃冰箱前。 ② The meat had turned putrid in the heat. 肉在高温下已经腐烂发臭。
[搭] putrid smell/odor 腐臭, putrid meat/waste 腐肉/腐坏废物, turn putrid 腐烂发臭
[联] rotten 腐烂的, fetid 恶臭的, stench 恶臭, decay 腐烂, smell 气味, waste 废物
[记] putrid 较书面、强；口语 stinking / rotten 更常见。putrefy 动词「腐败」

**queasy**
英 /ˈkwiːzi/ 美 /ˈkwiːzi/
adj. 恶心的；不安的
[义] Feeling sick to the stomach; slightly nauseated or uneasy. 胃里翻腾、想吐（feel queasy 觉得恶心，queasy stomach 反胃）；亦可指「心里发毛、不踏实」（queasy about the deal 对交易心里打鼓）。
[例] ① I felt queasy after reading in the moving car. 在晃动的车里看书，我觉得恶心。 ② Investors grew queasy as the stock kept falling. 股价持续下跌，投资者心里越来越没底。
[搭] feel queasy 觉得恶心, queasy stomach 反胃, queasy about 对……心里发毛, slightly queasy 有点恶心
[联] nauseous 恶心的, sick 想吐的, stomach 胃, seasick 晕船, uneasy 不安的, motion 晃动
[记] queasy 比 nauseous 更口语、略轻。queasy feeling 可指生理或心理不适

**conspicuous**
英 /kənˈspɪkjuəs/ 美 /kənˈspɪkjuəs/
adj. 显眼的；引人注目的
[义] Easy to see or notice; attracting attention. 视觉或行为上「扎眼、难忽略」（conspicuous consumption 炫耀性消费，conspicuous error 明显的错误）。con-（完全）+ spic（看）→ 一眼能看见。
[例] ① His bright yellow jacket made him conspicuous in the crowd. 亮黄夹克让他在人群里格外显眼。 ② There was a conspicuous lack of evidence in the report. 报告里明显缺少证据。
[源] 拉丁语 *conspicuus*：*con-*（完全）+ *specere*（看）→ 看得见的。
[搭] conspicuous consumption 炫耀性消费, conspicuous absence/lack 明显缺席/缺失, highly conspicuous 非常显眼
[联] noticeable 明显的, obvious 明显的, visible 可见的, hidden 隐藏的, eye-catching 抢眼的, stand out 突出
[记] conspicuous by one's absence 固定短语「因缺席而格外引人注目」。反义 inconspicuous

**blinding**
英 /ˈblaɪndɪŋ/ 美 /ˈblaɪndɪŋ/
adj. 刺眼的；炫目的
[义] Extremely bright, so that you cannot see properly; dazzling. 亮到睁不开眼（blinding snow 雪地反光，blinding headlights 刺眼大灯）。本章取光线感知义；blind 作「失明」见 `01-01`。
[例] ① The blinding sun reflected off the wet road. 刺眼阳光从湿路面上反射过来。 ② She shielded her eyes from the blinding camera flash. 她挡住刺眼相机闪光灯。
[搭] blinding light/sun 刺眼的光/阳光, blinding flash 炫目闪光, blinding snow/glare 雪地反光/眩光
[联] dazzling 炫目的, glare 眩光, bright 明亮的, sun 太阳, flash 闪光, snow 雪
[记] blinding 作副词口语：blindingly obvious 「明摆着」。勿与 blind（失明）词条混为一谈

**groggy**
英 /ˈɡrɒɡi/ 美 /ˈɡrɑːɡi/
adj. 昏沉的；摇摇晃晃的
[义] Weak and unable to think or move well, especially after sleep, illness, or drugs. 睡醒、生病或用药后「头蒙、反应慢、感官发钝」（groggy in the morning 早上昏沉，still groggy from anesthesia 麻醉后仍迷糊）。
[例] ① I felt groggy for an hour after the nap on the plane. 在飞机上小睡醒来，我昏沉了一小时。 ② She answered the phone, still groggy with sleep. 她接起电话，还睡眼惺忪、头脑发木。
[搭] feel groggy 感到昏沉, groggy in the morning 早上发懵, still groggy from 仍因……而迷糊
[联] drowsy 困倦的, sleepy 想睡的, dizzy 头晕的, anesthesia 麻醉, wake 醒来, dull 迟钝的
[记] groggy 几乎总含「刚醒/刚恢复，感官未上线」。grogginess 名词

**numbing**
英 /ˈnʌmɪŋ/ 美 /ˈnʌmɪŋ/
adj. 麻木的；使失去感觉的
[义] Causing loss of feeling; so extreme that you stop reacting. 使身体部位失去知觉（numbing cold 冻到麻木，numbing injection 局麻针）；喻「麻木到无感」（numbing boredom 无聊到麻木）。
[例] ① A numbing wind blew off the lake all night. 整晚湖上刮来刺骨寒风，冻得发麻。 ② The lecture was a numbing repeat of slides we had seen before. 讲座只是重复旧幻灯片，听得人麻木。
[搭] numbing cold/pain 冻麻/痛到麻木, numbing injection 局麻, numbing boredom 无聊到麻木
[联] numb 麻木的, freeze 冻, anesthesia 麻醉, cold 寒冷, pain 痛, dull 迟钝的
[记] numbing 现在分词形容词；numb 动词表「使麻木」。numbing vs. numb：numbing 强调「过程/致因」

**harmonious**
英 /hɑːˈməʊniəs/ 美 /hɑːrˈmoʊniəs/
adj. 和谐的；悦耳的
[义] Pleasant when combined, especially sounds; free from disagreement. 多种声音组合在一起「和谐、顺耳」（harmonious chords 和谐和弦）；亦可指关系融洽。本章取听觉感知义。
[例] ① The choir's voices blended in a harmonious whole. 合唱团的声音融成和谐的整体。 ② Harmonious background music made the café feel calm. 和谐的背景音乐让咖啡馆显得安静。
[搭] harmonious blend 和谐融合, harmonious chords/music 和谐和弦/音乐, harmonious sound 悦耳声响
[联] discordant 不和谐的, melody 旋律, chord 和弦, music 音乐, blend 融合, pleasant 悦耳的
[记] harmonious 可修饰 sound, music, relationship。harmony 名词；harmonize 动词

**discordant**
英 /dɪsˈkɔːdənt/ 美 /dɪsˈkɔːrdənt/
adj. 不和谐的；刺耳的
[义] Not in agreement or harmony; harsh when heard together. 多个音「打架、刺耳」（discordant notes 不协和音）；喻意见不合（discordant voices 众说纷纭）。dis-（不）+ cord（心/弦）。
[例] ① A discordant alarm rang while the orchestra was tuning. 乐团调音时，刺耳的警报响了。 ② Discordant opinions made it hard to reach a decision. 意见纷歧，难以做决定。
[搭] discordant notes/sounds 不协和音/刺耳声, discordant voices 意见不合, discordant clang 刺耳的铛声
[联] harmonious 和谐的, cacophony 嘈杂, harsh 刺耳的, clash 冲突, note 音符, disagreement 分歧
[记] discordant 较书面；日常 harsh / clashing 更口语。discord 名词「不和；不协和」

**mellifluous**
英 /məˈlɪfluəs/ 美 /məˈlɪfluəs/
adj. （嗓音）圆润悦耳的
[义] (Of a voice or words) sweet and smooth to hear. 嗓音或措辞「甜、滑、顺耳」（mellifluous tones 圆润声调，mellifluous speech 悦耳演说）。较书面、褒义；mel-（蜜）+ flu（流）→ 如蜜流淌。
[例] ① The host's mellifluous voice kept listeners tuned in for hours. 主持人圆润悦耳的嗓音让听众听了几小时。 ② She delivered the poem in mellifluous French. 她用悦耳的法语朗诵那首诗。
[源] 拉丁语 *mellifluus*：*mel*（蜜）+ *fluere*（流）→ 如蜜般流淌。
[搭] mellifluous voice/tone 圆润嗓音/声调, mellifluous speech 悦耳言辞
[联] sonorous 洪亮的, smooth 平滑的, honey 蜜, voice 嗓音, pleasant 悦耳的, husky 沙哑的
[记] mellifluous 几乎只褒义，多用于 voice, tone, language。日常 smooth / soothing 更口语

**resonant**
英 /ˈrezənənt/ 美 /ˈrezənənt/
adj. 洪亮的；共鸣的
[义] Deep, clear, and continuing to sound; filled with resonance. 浑厚、有回响、持续（resonant voice 洪亮嗓音，resonant hall 共鸣好的大厅）。与 resonate 动词成对；比 loud 更强调「腔体共鸣、音质厚」。
[例] ① The actor's resonant voice carried to the back row without a mic. 演员洪亮的声音不用麦克风也传到后排。 ② The cello produced a warm, resonant tone in the small chapel. 小礼拜堂里，大提琴发出温暖而共鸣的音色。
[族] resonate 共鸣, resonance 共鸣, resonator 共鸣器
[搭] resonant voice/tone 洪亮嗓音/音色, resonant hall 共鸣良好的大厅, deep resonant sound 浑厚回响
[联] resonate 共鸣, loud 大声的, deep 深沉的, echo 回声, voice 嗓音, hall 大厅
[记] resonant 修饰 voice, sound, chamber 极常见。物理义 resonant frequency「共振频率」依语境区分

**thunderous**
英 /ˈθʌndərəs/ 美 /ˈθʌndərəs/
adj. 雷鸣般的；轰隆的
[义] Extremely loud, like thunder. 响如雷鸣（thunderous applause 掌声如雷，thunderous footsteps 沉重的脚步）。比 loud/deafening 更带「低、滚、震」感。
[例] ① The proposal received thunderous applause from the audience. 提案赢得观众雷鸣般的掌声。 ② Thunderous footsteps echoed in the empty corridor. 空走廊里回荡着沉重的脚步声。
[搭] thunderous applause 掌声如雷, thunderous footsteps 沉重脚步, thunderous roar 轰鸣
[联] deafening 震耳欲聋, rumble 隆隆, applause 掌声, roar 轰鸣, loud 大声的, thunder 雷
[记] thunderous 常修饰 applause, ovation, roar, footsteps。thunderous silence 为反讽「死寂」

**yelp**
英 /jelp/ 美 /jelp/
v. 短促尖叫
n. 短促尖叫声
[义] A short sharp cry, especially of pain or surprise. 因痛或惊而发出的短促尖叫（yelp in pain 痛得叫一声，dog yelped 狗惨叫）。比 scream 更短、更突然。
[例] ① She yelped when the hot coffee splashed her hand. 热咖啡溅到手，她短促地叫了一声。 ② The puppy yelped and hid behind the sofa. 小狗惨叫一声，躲到沙发后面。
[搭] yelp in pain/surprise 痛/惊叫, sharp yelp 短促尖叫, dog yelped 狗惨叫
[联] scream 尖叫, squeal 尖叫, pain 痛, surprise 惊讶, dog 狗, sharp 尖锐的
[记] yelp 极短、单声；scream 可拖长。 Yelp 亦为美国点评 App 名，依语境区分

**whimper**
英 /ˈwɪmpə/ 美 /ˈwɪmpər/
v. 呜咽；啜泣
n. 呜咽声
[义] To make low weak crying sounds; to speak in this way. 低弱、断续的哭或哼（whimper with fear 吓得呜咽，whimper for help 呜咽求助）。比 cry 更轻、更无助；比 moan 更「碎、小、孩/动物」。
[例] ① The lost child whimpered until a passer-by stopped. 迷路的孩子一直呜咽，直到有人停下脚步。 ② "Don't leave," he whimpered. 「别走。」他抽泣着说。
[搭] whimper with fear 吓得呜咽, whimper for help 呜咽求助, soft whimper 轻声呜咽
[联] cry 哭, moan 呻吟, sob 啜泣, fear 恐惧, weak 弱的, child 孩子
[记] whimper 常含「弱、小、可怜」。end not with a bang but a whimper 文学典故「无声收场」

**trickle**
英 /ˈtrɪkl/ 美 /ˈtrɪkl/
v. 滴流；细细流淌
n. 细流；滴答
[义] To flow slowly in a thin stream; to come or go slowly in small numbers. 细水慢流（water trickled 细流涓涓，trickle of sweat 汗珠渗出）；喻「慢慢增加/减少」（trickle in 陆续到来）。
[例] ① Sweat trickled down his back during the hike. 徒步时汗珠顺着后背往下淌。 ② Only a trickle of customers came in before noon. 中午前只有零星客人进来。
[搭] trickle of water/sweat 细流/汗珠, trickle down 往下滴, trickle in 陆续到来, slow trickle 缓缓细流
[联] drip 滴, stream 溪流, flow 流, sweat 汗, leak 漏, sound 声响
[记] trickle-down economics 为另一义「涓滴经济学」。本章取「细流/慢流」感知义

**aftertaste**
英 /ˈɑːftəteɪst/ 美 /ˈæftərteɪst/
n. 余味；事后感受
[义] A taste that remains after eating or drinking; a feeling that remains after an event. 咽下去后留在嘴里的味道（bitter aftertaste 苦涩余味）；喻「事后留下的印象」（an aftertaste of disappointment 失望余韵）。
[例] ① The wine left a pleasant aftertaste of berries. 这酒留下怡人的浆果余味。 ② The scandal left an aftertaste of mistrust in the company. 丑闻在公司里留下不信任的余波。
[搭] bitter/sweet aftertaste 苦/甜余味, leave an aftertaste 留下余味, pleasant aftertaste 怡人余味
[联] taste 味道, flavor 风味, linger 萦绕, bitter 苦, wine 酒, impression 印象
[记] aftertaste 可数/不可数均可。强调「事后仍留在感官/心里的残留」

**stinky**
英 /ˈstɪŋki/ 美 /ˈstɪŋki/
adj. 臭的（口语）
[义] Having a strong unpleasant smell (informal). 很臭——口语、儿语感（stinky socks 臭袜子，stinky cheese 臭奶酪）。比 smelly 更随意、更强；stink 的形容词形式。
[例] ① The stinky bin had not been emptied all week. 垃圾桶一整周没倒，臭气熏天。 ② Kids laughed at his stinky gym bag. 孩子们笑他那臭烘烘的健身包。
[搭] stinky socks/cheese 臭袜子/臭奶酪, stinky smell 臭气, really stinky 特别臭
[联] smelly 有臭味的, stink 发臭, odor 气味, socks 袜子, rotten 腐烂的, foul 恶臭的
[记] stinky 口语；正式写作用 malodorous / foul。stink 动词见本章 stink 条

**nasal**
英 /ˈneɪzl/ 美 /ˈneɪzl/
adj. 鼻音的；鼻的
[义] (Of a voice) produced partly through the nose; relating to the nose. 嗓音「鼻音重」（nasal voice 鼻音）；或「鼻的、经鼻的」（nasal spray 鼻喷，nasal passage 鼻腔）。听觉+嗅觉通道皆涉鼻。
[例] ① He spoke with a nasal accent that marked him as from the region. 他说话带明显鼻音，一听便知来自那地区。 ② A cold can make your voice sound nasal. 感冒会让嗓音听起来鼻音很重。
[搭] nasal voice/accent 鼻音/带鼻音的口音, nasal spray 鼻喷, nasal passage 鼻腔, sound nasal 听起来鼻音重
[联] nose 鼻, voice 嗓音, accent 口音, cold 感冒, smell 嗅觉, throat 喉咙
[记] nasal 修饰 voice 极常见。名词 nasal 亦指「鼻音 consonant」（/n/, /m/）

**fizz**
英 /fɪz/ 美 /fɪz/
v. 嘶嘶冒泡
n. 嘶嘶声；气泡饮
[义] To make a sound like gas escaping through liquid; such a sound. 气泡冒出时的「嘶——」声（champagne fizzing 香槟嘶嘶，fizz of soda 汽水气泡声）。比 crackle 更「气、泡、碳酸」。
[例] ① We heard the fizz when she opened the bottle. 她开瓶时我们听见嘶嘶声。 ② The tablet fizzed as it dissolved in the glass. 药片在杯里溶解时嘶嘶冒泡。
[搭] fizz of soda/champagne 汽水/香槟嘶嘶, fizz with excitement 兴奋得冒泡（喻）, lose its fizz 没气了
[联] bubble 冒泡, hiss 嘶嘶, soda 汽水, champagne 香槟, crackle 噼啪, sound 声响
[记] fizz out 喻「虎头蛇尾」见他章。形容词 fizzy 见属性章；本章取动词/名词「嘶嘶冒泡」

**plop**
英 /plɒp/ 美 /plɑːp/
v. 扑通落下
n. 扑通声
[义] To fall or drop with a short sound like something hitting water; such a sound. 物体落入水中或软表面的短促「扑通」（plop into the bath 扑通入浴，plop down on the sofa 扑通坐沙发）。比 splash 更「单声、短、小」。
[例] ① The frog plopped into the pond and disappeared. 青蛙扑通跳进池塘，不见了。 ② She plopped two ice cubes into the glass. 她往杯里扑通扔进两块冰。
[搭] plop into 扑通进入, plop down 扑通坐下, with a plop 扑通一声
[联] splash 溅泼, drop 落下, pond 池塘, sofa 沙发, sound 声响, water 水
[记] plop 拟声；plop down 口语「重重/随便坐下」。英式 plop 亦指「廉价劣质酒」（旧俚），少见

**mouth-watering**
英 /ˈmaʊθˌwɔːtərɪŋ/ 美 /ˈmaʊθˌwɔːtərɪŋ/
adj. 令人流口水的；诱人的
[义] Smelling or looking so good that you want to eat it. 闻或看就让人馋（mouth-watering aroma 诱人香气，mouth-watering photos 看得人流口水的照片）。比 appetizing 更口语、更强调「馋涎被勾起」。
[例] ① The mouth-watering smell of grilled meat filled the street. 街上弥漫着烤肉令人流口水的香气。 ② Food bloggers post mouth-watering close-ups of every dish. 美食博主给每道菜拍让人垂涎的特写。
[搭] mouth-watering smell/aroma 诱人香气, mouth-watering food/photos 诱人食物/照片, look mouth-watering 看起来馋人
[联] appetizing 开胃的, aroma 香气, tasty 好吃的, hunger 饥饿, delicious 美味的, smell 气味
[记] 常作定语；表语 It looks mouth-watering 口语自然。与 watering 复合：eye-watering 见下条

**eye-watering**
英 /ˈaɪˌwɔːtərɪŋ/ 美 /ˈaɪˌwɔːtərɪŋ/
adj. 辣眼的；贵得吓人的
[义] Making your eyes fill with tears, especially from onions or strong fumes; shockingly high (informal). 刺激得流泪（eye-watering onions 辣眼洋葱，eye-watering chili fumes 呛眼辣椒烟）；口语亦指「贵/高到咋舌」（eye-watering price 天价）。本章取感官义为主。
[例] ① Chopping eye-watering onions made everyone in the kitchen tear up. 切洋葱辣得厨房里人人都流泪。 ② The eye-watering heat from the chili pan made us cough. 炒锅里的辣椒热烟呛眼，把我们呛得咳嗽。
[搭] eye-watering onions/chili 辣眼洋葱/辣椒, eye-watering fumes 呛眼烟雾, eye-watering price 天价（口语）
[联] watery 水汪汪的, tearful 含泪的, sting 刺痛, onion 洋葱, chili 辣椒, fumes 烟雾
[记] eye-watering 几乎总含「强刺激」。价格义英式新闻常见：eye-watering cost

**tangy**
英 /ˈtæŋi/ 美 /ˈtæŋi/
adj. 尖酸开胃的；味道浓郁的
[义] Having a sharp, slightly acid taste or smell. 酸爽、带刺激的开胃味（tangy sauce 酸香酱，tangy lemon 酸劲足的柠檬）。比 sour 更「活泼、有层次、常令人分泌口水」；比 zesty 略偏「酸」。
[例] ① The salad had a tangy dressing of yogurt and lime. 沙拉用了酸奶青柠的酸爽淋汁。 ② A tangy smell of vinegar hung in the pickling room. 腌制间里飘着醋的酸香。
[搭] tangy flavor/sauce 酸爽风味/酱, tangy lemon/vinegar 酸劲柠檬/醋, slightly tangy 略酸开胃
[联] sour 酸的, zesty 有 zest 的, sharp 尖锐的, lemon 柠檬, vinegar 醋, flavor 风味
[记] tangy 多褒义，描写酱、果、发酵食品。名词 tang 指「强烈味道/特色」

**zesty**
英 /ˈzesti/ 美 /ˈzesti/
adj. 有 zest 的；活泼的
[义] Having a strong, pleasant, and slightly spicy flavour; energetic and appealing. 味道鲜明、带劲（zesty citrus 酸香柑橘）；亦可指「活泼、有感染力」（zesty performance 有活力的表演）。本章取味觉/嗅觉义为主。
[例] ① The soup had a zesty kick from fresh ginger and lime. 汤里姜和青柠带来鲜明的一股劲。 ② She added zesty herbs to brighten the bland pasta. 她加了提神的香草，把寡淡的面食提亮。
[搭] zesty flavor 鲜明风味, zesty citrus/herbs 酸香柑橘/香草, zesty kick 一股劲
[联] tangy 酸爽的, spicy 辣的, fresh 新鲜的, herb 香草, citrus 柑橘, lively 有活力的
[记] zest 名词本义「外层橙皮；热情」。zesty 比 spicy 更宽，可含酸、香、微辣

**malodorous**
英 /mælˈəʊdərəs/ 美 /mælˈoʊdərəs/
adj. 恶臭的（正式）
[义] Having an unpleasant smell. 气味难闻——较书面（malodorous waste 恶臭废物，malodorous chemical 气味刺鼻的化学品）。mal-（坏）+ odor（气味）；比 stinky/smelly 更正式。
[例] ① Malodorous smoke from the dump drifted over the neighborhood. 垃圾场飘来的恶臭烟雾笼罩街区。 ② The lab stores malodorous samples in sealed containers. 实验室把气味难闻的样本放在密封容器里。
[搭] malodorous smell/odor 恶臭, malodorous waste/smoke 恶臭废物/烟, highly malodorous 极臭
[联] stinky 臭的, fetid 恶臭的, odor 气味, smell 气味, foul 恶臭的, chemical 化学品
[记] malodorous 书面/技术语；日常 stinking / smelly 更自然。反义 fragrant / aromatic

**noxious**
英 /ˈnɒkʃəs/ 美 /ˈnɑːkʃəs/
adj. 有害的；有毒气的
[义] Harmful to health; poisonous or very unpleasant to breathe. 对健康有害；吸入 unpleasant（noxious fumes 有毒烟雾，noxious weeds 有毒植物）。比 toxic 更常接「气体、烟雾、气味」感知语境。
[例] ① Workers wore masks to avoid noxious fumes from the paint. 工人戴口罩，以免吸入油漆的有毒气体。 ② A noxious smell warned us to leave the basement. 一股刺鼻怪味提醒我们离开地下室。
[搭] noxious fumes/smoke 有毒烟/气, noxious smell 刺鼻有害气味, noxious substance 有害物质
[联] toxic 有毒的, fumes 烟雾, harmful 有害的, smell 气味, poison 毒, chemical 化学品
[记] noxious 可修饰 gas, fumes, odor, plant。noxious vs. toxic：noxious 偏「闻/吸到的危害」

**umami**
英 /uːˈmɑːmi/ 美 /uːˈmɑːmi/
n. 鲜味
adj. 鲜味的
[义] A savoury taste, one of the basic tastes, found in foods like soy sauce, mushrooms, and aged cheese. 第五种基本味觉「鲜」——酱油、蘑菇、陈年奶酪等（umami flavor 鲜味，rich umami 浓郁鲜香）。日语借词，近二十年进入英语通用语汇。
[例] ① Slow-cooked broth draws out a deep umami from the bones. 慢炖高汤从骨头里熬出深厚鲜味。 ② Parmesan adds umami without making the dish salty. 帕玛森增鲜而不让菜过咸。
[源] 日语 *umami*（うまみ，*uma-* 美味 + *mi* 味）→ 鲜味。
[搭] umami flavor/taste 鲜味, rich umami 浓郁鲜香, umami-rich foods 富含鲜味的食物
[联] savory 咸鲜的, taste 味道, soy sauce 酱油, mushroom 蘑菇, salty 咸的, broth 高汤
[记] 与 sweet, sour, bitter, salty 并列第五味。形容词用法 umami notes 在美食写作中常见

**insipid**
英 /ɪnˈsɪpɪd/ 美 /ɪnˈsɪpɪd/
adj. 寡淡的；乏味的
[义] Lacking flavour or interest; dull. 食物「没味、像喝水」（insipid soup 寡淡的汤）；引申「无聊、没特点」（insipid conversation 乏味的交谈）。比 bland 更带贬义、更书面。
[例] ① The tea tasted insipid, as if it had been brewed twice. 茶寡淡无味，像泡了两遍。 ② Critics called the film insipid and forgettable. 批评者称那部电影平淡无奇、看过就忘。
[搭] insipid taste/flavor 寡淡味道, insipid food 没劲的食物, insipid conversation 乏味交谈
[联] bland 淡而无味的, tasteless 无味的, dull 沉闷的, flavor 风味, weak 弱的, boring 无聊的
[记] insipid 可修饰 food, drink, writing, personality。in-（不）+ sap（味道）→ 无滋味

**applause**
英 /əˈplɔːz/ 美 /əˈplɔːz/
n. 掌声；喝彩
[义] The sound of people clapping to show approval; praise shown in this way. 观众拍手发出的声响与喝彩（thunderous applause 掌声雷动，break into applause 爆发出掌声）。不可数居多。
[例] ① The singer bowed to thunderous applause. 歌手在雷鸣般的掌声中鞠躬。 ② There was polite applause when the talk ended. 演讲结束时响起礼节性的掌声。
[搭] thunderous/enthusiastic applause 掌声雷动/热烈掌声, break into applause 掌声响起, round of applause 一轮掌声
[联] clap 拍手, cheer 欢呼, ovation 热烈鼓掌, audience 观众, thunderous 雷鸣般的, praise 赞扬
[记] applause 不可数；a round of applause 可数框架。动词 applaud；形容词 applauded

**clap**
英 /klæp/ 美 /klæp/
v. 拍手；啪地合上
n. 拍手声；霹雳声
[义] To hit your open hands together to make a noise; the sound of this. 双手合击出声（clap your hands 拍手，audience clapped 观众鼓掌）；亦指「啪」的短促声（clap of thunder 一声霹雳）。本章取听觉/鼓掌义。
[例] ① The audience clapped until their hands hurt. 观众鼓掌鼓到手疼。 ② A clap of thunder woke the whole house. 一声霹雳把全家都惊醒了。
[搭] clap your hands 拍手, clap of thunder 霹雳一声, clap loudly 大声鼓掌, clap sb. on the back 拍某人后背（鼓励）
[联] applause 掌声, cheer 欢呼, thunder 雷, hands 手, sound 声响, praise 赞扬
[记] clap 不规则 rarely；clap eyes on 英式「看见」。clap 作名词 thunderclap 常合写

**whistle**
英 /ˈwɪsl/ 美 /ˈwɪsl/
v. 吹口哨；呼啸
n. 口哨声；哨子
[义] To make a high sound by blowing air through pursed lips; such a sound; a device that makes this sound. 用嘴吹出的高亢哨声（whistle a tune 吹调子）；物体快速移动时的「呼啸」（bullet whistled 子弹呼啸）；名词亦指哨子。
[例] ① He whistled while he washed the dishes. 他洗碗时吹着口哨。 ② The referee blew his whistle and stopped play. 裁判吹哨叫停了比赛。
[搭] blow a whistle 吹哨, whistle a tune 吹调子, whistle past 呼啸而过, wolf whistle 挑逗口哨（贬义）
[联] chirp 唧唧, hiss 嘶嘶, tune 曲调, referee 裁判, wind 风, sound 声响
[记] whistle 可数/不可数均可。whistleblower 为另一义「吹哨人/举报人」。wolf whistle 含骚扰义，依语境区分

**click**
英 /klɪk/ 美 /klɪk/
v. 咔嗒响；点击
n. 咔嗒声
[义] To make a short sharp sound; such a sound. 短促清脆的「咔嗒」（door clicked 门咔嗒关上，click of heels 鞋跟咔嗒）。科技语境 click 另义「鼠标点击」，依语境区分。
[例] ① The lock clicked and the door swung open. 锁咔嗒一声，门开了。 ② I heard the click of her heels on the marble floor. 我听见她的鞋跟在大理石地面上咔嗒作响。
[搭] click shut 咔嗒关上, click of heels/keys 鞋跟/钥匙咔嗒, click into place 咔嗒到位
[联] clack 咔咔, snap 啪嗒, lock 锁, heels 鞋跟, keyboard 键盘, sound 声响
[记] click 拟声极频。click with sb. 喻「合得来」为另一义。× click the photo 应为 click on the icon

**grumble**
英 /ˈɡrʌmbl/ 美 /ˈɡrʌmbl/
v. 咕哝；发牢骚；轰隆响
n. 咕哝；轰隆声
[义] To complain quietly; to make a low continuous sound. 低声抱怨（grumble about the weather 抱怨天气）；或 stomach/thunder「咕噜、轰隆」（grumbling thunder 远处隆隆雷声，stomach grumbling 肚子咕咕叫）。听觉+情绪两用。
[例] ① Commuters grumbled about the delayed trains. 通勤者抱怨火车晚点。 ② We heard grumbling thunder in the distance. 我们听见远处隆隆的雷声。
[搭] grumble about 抱怨, stomach grumbling 肚子咕咕叫, grumbling thunder 隆隆雷声, grumble under one's breath 低声牢骚
[联] rumble 隆隆, mutter 低声说, complain 抱怨, thunder 雷, stomach 胃, moan 呻吟
[记] grumble 比 complain 更「碎碎念、低音量」。grumble 作名词可数（a grumble）

**honk**
英 /hɒŋk/ 美 /hɑːŋk/
v. 按喇叭；鹅叫
n. 喇叭声；鹅鸣
[义] To make the loud sound of a car horn; the cry of a goose. 汽车喇叭的「嘀——」或「叭——」（honk the horn 按喇叭，honked impatiently 不耐烦地 honk）；亦指鹅叫。
[例] ① Drivers honked as the truck blocked the intersection. 卡车堵在路口，司机们按喇叭。 ② A flock of geese honked overhead. 一群鹅在头顶嘎嘎叫。
[搭] honk the horn 按喇叭, honk at sb. 对某人按喇叭, loud honk 响亮喇叭声
[联] beep 哔哔, horn 喇叭, traffic 交通, goose 鹅, impatient 不耐烦, sound 声响
[记] honk 美式极口语。goose honk 与 car honk 同词不同义，依语境区分

**sonorous**
英 /səˈnɒrəs/ 美 /səˈnɑːrəs/
adj. 浑厚响亮的
[义] (Of a person's voice or language) deep, clear, and powerful. 嗓音或措辞「深、清、有力量」（sonorous voice 浑厚嗓音，sonorous prose 铿锵文字）。比 resonant 略偏「庄严、播音感」；比 loud 更强调「音质美、厚」。
[例] ① The actor's sonorous voice filled the theatre without a microphone. 演员浑厚嗓音不用麦也充满剧场。 ② The judge read the verdict in sonorous tones. 法官以庄严声调宣读判决。
[搭] sonorous voice/tone 浑厚嗓音/声调, sonorous prose 铿锵文字, deep sonorous sound 深沉洪亮的声音
[联] resonant 共鸣的, mellifluous 圆润的, deep 深沉的, voice 嗓音, loud 大声的, prose 文字
[记] sonorous 几乎总褒义。sonorous vs. resonant：sonorous 更「庄严、播音/演说」；resonant 更「物理共鸣」

**inconspicuous**
英 /ˌɪnkənˈspɪkjuəs/ 美 /ˌɪnkənˈspɪkjuəs/
adj. 不显眼的；不引人注目的
[义] Not clearly visible or attracting attention; easy to miss. 不易被看见或忽略（inconspicuous entrance 不显眼的入口，inconspicuous gray coat 不扎眼的灰外套）。conspicuous 的反义；in- + conspicuous。
[例] ① She chose an inconspicuous seat at the back of the hall. 她选了大厅后排不显眼的座位。 ② The camera was hidden in an inconspicuous smoke detector. 摄像头藏在不起眼的烟雾报警器里。
[搭] inconspicuous place/entrance 不起眼的地方/入口, remain inconspicuous 不引人注目, inconspicuous gray 不扎眼的灰色
[联] conspicuous 显眼的, hidden 隐藏的, subtle 细微的, visible 可见的, unnoticed 未被注意的, blend in 融入
[记] try to remain inconspicuous 固定表达「尽量低调不显眼」。反义 conspicuous 见本章

**glaring**
英 /ˈɡleərɪŋ/ 美 /ˈɡlerɪŋ/
adj. 刺眼的；显眼的
[义] Unpleasantly bright; very obvious and impossible to ignore. 光线刺眼（glaring sun 刺眼阳光，glaring headlights 晃眼大灯）；或错误/差异「扎眼、明显」（a glaring mistake 明显的错误）。glare 的现在分词形容词。
[例] ① The glaring white walls made it hard to look at the screen. 白墙反光刺眼，很难看屏幕。 ② There is a glaring gap in the safety data. 安全数据里有个明显漏洞。
[搭] glaring sun/light 刺眼阳光/光, glaring mistake/error 明显错误, glaring headlights 晃眼大灯
[联] blinding 刺眼的, glare 眩光, bright 明亮的, obvious 明显的, mistake 错误, sun 太阳
[记] glaring 可修饰 light, sun, error, omission。glaring at sb. 为 glare 动词「怒视」，依语境区分

**quiver**
英 /ˈkwɪvə/ 美 /ˈkwɪvər/
v. 颤抖；微颤
n. 颤抖；箭袋（另义）
[义] To shake slightly with quick small movements. 轻微、快速的颤动——声、光、唇、叶皆可（voice quivered 声音发颤，leaves quivering 叶子沙沙轻颤）。比 tremble 更「细、快、局部」；比 shiver 少「冷/怕」色彩。
[例] ① Her lip quivered as she tried not to cry. 她强忍泪水，嘴唇微微颤抖。 ② The candle flame quivered in the draft. 烛火在穿堂风里轻轻晃动。
[搭] quiver with emotion 激动得发颤, lip/voice quivered 唇/声颤抖, quiver in the wind 在风中轻颤
[联] tremble 颤抖, shiver 发抖, shake 摇晃, voice 嗓音, lip 嘴唇, flame 火焰
[记] quiver 可及物/不及物。quiver 名词另义「箭袋」，依语境区分

**lightheaded**
英 /ˌlaɪtˈhedɪd/ 美 /ˌlaɪtˈhedɪd/
adj. 头晕的；轻飘飘的
[义] Feeling as if you might faint; slightly dizzy and not thinking clearly. 头重脚轻、像要晕倒（feel lightheaded 觉得头晕，lightheaded from skipping lunch 没吃午饭发晕）。比 dizzy 更口语、常指「短暂、轻度」。
[例] ① I felt lightheaded when I stood up too quickly. 站得太猛，我觉得一阵头晕。 ② Donating blood left her a bit lightheaded for an hour. 献完血她晕乎乎的大约一小时。
[搭] feel lightheaded 觉得头晕, lightheaded and dizzy 头晕眼花, lightheaded from 因……发晕
[联] dizzy 头晕的, faint 要晕倒的, groggy 昏沉的, stand up 站起, blood 献血, weak 虚弱的
[记] 常作表语 feel lightheaded。lightheadedness 名词。与 light-headed 同义，美式常连写

**vertigo**
英 /ˈvɜːtɪɡəʊ/ 美 /ˈvɜːrtɪɡoʊ/
n. 眩晕；晕动症
[义] The sensation of spinning or losing balance when you are not moving; dizziness. 静止时仍觉天旋地转、失衡（suffer from vertigo 患眩晕，a bout of vertigo 一阵眩晕）。较 clinical/正式；日常 dizzy 更宽。
[例] ① She had to sit down when a sudden bout of vertigo hit. 一阵突发眩晕袭来，她只好坐下。 ② Looking down from the glass floor triggered his vertigo. 从玻璃地板往下看，诱发了他的恐高眩晕。
[搭] suffer from vertigo 患眩晕, bout of vertigo 一阵眩晕, sense of vertigo 眩晕感, trigger vertigo 引发眩晕
[联] dizzy 头晕的, balance 平衡, spin 旋转, height 高度, nausea 恶心, ear 耳（内耳）
[记] vertigo 可数/不可数均可。电影/小说名 Vertigo 亦常见。内耳问题常致 vertigo

**hypersensitive**
英 /ˌhaɪpəˈsensətɪv/ 美 /ˌhaɪpərˈsensətɪv/
adj. 超敏感的；过敏的
[义] Extremely sensitive to physical stimuli, criticism, or allergens. 对刺激、批评或过敏原「反应过强」（hypersensitive skin 超敏感皮肤，hypersensitive to noise 对噪音过敏）。hyper-（过度）+ sensitive。
[例] ① Hypersensitive skin may react to fragrance in soap. 超敏感皮肤可能对皂里香精起反应。 ② He became hypersensitive to any mention of the mistake. 一提那错误他就反应过激。
[搭] hypersensitive skin 超敏感皮肤, hypersensitive to 对……超敏感, hypersensitive reaction 超敏反应
[联] sensitive 敏感的, allergic 过敏的, skin 皮肤, noise 噪音, reaction 反应, immune 免疫
[记] hypersensitive 人际义略贬「玻璃心」；医学/护肤义中性。hypersensitivity 名词

**teary**
英 /ˈtɪəri/ 美 /ˈtɪri/
adj. 含泪的；泪汪汪的
[义] Full of tears or likely to cry; wet with tears. 眼里有泪或快要哭（teary eyes 泪汪汪，teary voice 带哭腔）。比 tearful 更短、更口语；比 watery 更强调「快哭/刚哭」。
[例] ① She gave a teary thank-you speech at the farewell party. 告别会上她含泪致谢。 ② His eyes were teary after watching the final scene. 看完结尾他的眼睛泪汪汪的。
[搭] teary eyes 泪汪汪, teary voice 带哭腔, get teary 快哭了, teary goodbye 含泪告别
[联] tearful 含泪的, watery 水汪汪的, cry 哭, eyes 眼睛, emotional 动情的, voice 嗓音
[记] get teary / grow teary 口语极频。teary-eyed 复合形容词同义

**euphonious**
英 /juːˈfəʊniəs/ 美 /juːˈfoʊniəs/
adj. 悦耳的；和谐的
[义] (Of sound, especially speech) pleasing to hear. 听起来 pleasant、和谐（euphonious name 顺口的名字，euphonious language 悦耳的语言）。eu-（好）+ phon（声）；与 harmonious 近义，euphonious 更书面。
[例] ① The poet chose euphonious words for the opening lines. 诗人为开头选了音韵悦耳的词。 ② Italian place names often sound euphonious to English ears. 意大利地名在英语听者耳里往往很顺耳。
[源] 希腊语 *eu-*（好）+ *phōnē*（声音）→ 好声音。
[搭] euphonious sound/language 悦耳声音/语言, euphonious name 顺口名字
[联] harmonious 和谐的, mellifluous 圆润的, pleasant 悦耳的, melody 旋律, phonetic 语音的, discordant 不和谐的
[记] euphonious 较书面/文学；日常 pleasant-sounding 更口语。反义 discordant / harsh

**swish**
英 /swɪʃ/ 美 /swɪʃ/
v. 嗖嗖响；挥动
n. 嗖嗖声
[义] To move quickly through the air with a soft hissing sound; such a sound. 物体快速划破空气的「嗖——」声（curtain swished 窗帘嗖地滑开，swish of a broom 扫帚划过声）。比 whoosh 更「轻、细、近」。
[例] ① The basketball swished through the net. 篮球唰地穿网而过。 ② I heard the swish of her dress as she walked past. 她走过时我听见裙摆嗖嗖响。
[搭] swish through 嗖地穿过, swish of curtains/dress 窗帘/裙摆嗖声, swish past 嗖地掠过
[联] swoosh 嗖, whoosh 呼, hiss 嘶嘶, dress 连衣裙, curtain 窗帘, sound 声响
[记] basketball swish 美式指「空心入网」。swish 亦可俚语，依语境区分

**squawk**
英 /skwɔːk/ 美 /skwɑːk/
v. 嘎嘎叫；尖声抱怨
n. 嘎嘎声；故障杂音
[义] To make a loud harsh cry like a bird; to complain loudly. 鸟或喇叭的「嘎——」粗厉声（parrot squawked 鹦鹉嘎嘎）；电子设备「滋啦」杂音；口语大声抱怨（squawk about fees 抱怨费用）。
[例] ① The parrot squawked whenever someone entered the room. 有人进房间鹦鹉就嘎嘎叫。 ② The radio squawked with static before the signal cleared. 信号清晰前，电台滋啦作响。
[搭] squawk of a parrot 鹦鹉嘎嘎, squawk with static 滋啦杂音, squawk about 大声抱怨
[联] screech 尖叫, honk 喇叭/鹅叫, parrot 鹦鹉, static 静电杂音, harsh 刺耳的, bird 鸟
[记] squawk box 旧式「对讲喇叭」。squawk 几乎总含「粗、刺耳、闹」

**boom**
英 /buːm/ 美 /buːm/
n. 轰隆声；繁荣（另义）
v. 轰隆响；低沉地说
[义] A deep loud sound; to make this sound. 深沉响亮的「轰——」（boom of thunder 雷声轰鸣，door slammed with a boom 门砰地巨响）。本章取听觉义；经济 boom、麦克风 boom 为另义。
[例] ① We heard the boom of fireworks across the lake. 我们听见湖对岸烟花轰隆作响。 ② The cannon boomed once and fell silent. 大炮轰响一声，归于沉寂。
[搭] boom of thunder/fireworks 雷/烟花轰鸣, with a boom 轰的一声, sonic boom 音爆
[联] thunder 雷, rumble 隆隆, blast 爆响, loud 大声的, echo 回声, deep 深沉的
[记] boom 可数；economic boom 为另一义「繁荣」。mic boom 指「挑杆麦克风」

**mouthfeel**
英 /ˈmaʊtfiːl/ 美 /ˈmaʊtfiːl/
n. 口感
[义] The physical feel of food in the mouth, including texture and consistency. 食物在口中的质地与「触感」——滑、稠、沙、弹等（creamy mouthfeel 顺滑口感，gritty mouthfeel 沙粒感）。美食与品酒常用词。
[例] ① The chocolate has a smooth, creamy mouthfeel that melts quickly. 这巧克力口感顺滑、醇滑，入口即化。 ② Cheap wine often lacks depth in flavor and mouthfeel. 廉价葡萄酒往往在风味和口感上都欠层次。
[搭] creamy/smooth mouthfeel 顺滑口感, gritty mouthfeel 沙粒感, rich mouthfeel 醇厚口感
[联] texture 质地, taste 味道, flavor 风味, creamy 顺滑的, gritty 有沙粒感的, palate 味觉
[记] 美食写作高频；中文常直译「口感」。与 flavor（味）不同——mouthfeel 专指触觉质地

**dissonant**
英 /ˈdɪsənənt/ 美 /ˈdɪsənənt/
adj. 不和谐的；刺耳的
[义] Not harmonious; harsh when combined, especially in music. 音程或声音「不协和、刺耳」（dissonant chords 不协和和弦）；喻观点冲突（dissonant voices 分歧之声）。比 discordant 更偏音乐术语；discordant 更宽。
[例] ① The composer used dissonant chords to create tension. 作曲家以不协和和弦制造张力。 ② Dissonant opinions surfaced during the meeting. 会上出现了相互冲突的意见。
[搭] dissonant chords/notes 不协和和弦/音, dissonant sound 刺耳声音, dissonant voices 分歧意见
[联] discordant 不和谐的, harmonious 和谐的, chord 和弦, harsh 刺耳的, music 音乐, clash 冲突
[记] dissonance 名词「不协和；分歧」。dissonant vs. discordant：前者音乐味更浓

**cacophonous**
英 /kəˈkɒfənəs/ 美 /kəˈkɑːfənəs/
adj. 刺耳嘈杂的
[义] Involving or producing a harsh mixture of sounds. 充满 cacophony 的、刺耳嘈杂的（cacophonous traffic 嘈杂交通，cacophonous orchestra tuning 乐团调音时的乱响）。cacophony 的形容词。
[例] ① The cacophonous street made phone calls almost impossible. 街道嘈杂刺耳，几乎没法打电话。 ② Cacophonous alarms rang from several buildings at once. 几栋楼的警报同时刺耳大作。
[搭] cacophonous noise/street 嘈杂噪音/街道, cacophonous sound 刺耳声音
[联] cacophony 嘈杂, discordant 不和谐的, deafening 震耳, noise 噪音, harsh 刺耳的, din 喧嚣
[记] cacophonous 较书面；口语 a noisy mess 更随意。反义 harmonious / euphonious

**slosh**
英 /slɒʃ/ 美 /slɑːʃ/
v. 哗啦晃荡
n. 晃荡声
[义] To move noisily with liquid splashing inside a container; such a sound. 液体在容器里晃荡的「哗啦」声（water sloshing in the bottle 瓶里水晃荡，slosh through puddles 蹚水哗啦）。比 splash 更「晃、满、容器内」。
[例] ① Water sloshed over the rim as she hurried down the hall. 她快步穿过走廊时，水从杯沿晃出来。 ② We sloshed through ankle-deep puddles after the storm. 暴雨后我们蹚过没踝的积水，哗啦作响。
[搭] slosh around 晃荡, slosh over 晃出, slosh through 哗啦穿过, slosh of water 水晃荡声
[联] splash 溅泼, swish 嗖, liquid 液体, puddle 水坑, bottle 瓶子, sound 声响
[记] slosh 常描写半满容器或蹚水。sloshing 名词/现在分词

**swoosh**
英 /swuːʃ/ 美 /swuːʃ/
n. 嗖的一声
v. 嗖地掠过
[义] A smooth rushing sound, like fast movement through air. 物体快速划过的「嗖——」声（swoosh of a basketball 篮球穿网嗖声，swoosh past 嗖地掠过）。比 swish 略长、更有「气流、品牌 Logo 感」。
[例] ① The arrow made a swoosh as it flew toward the target. 箭飞向靶子时发出嗖的一声。 ② A skateboard swooshed past us on the sidewalk. 滑板从人行道上嗖地掠过我们身边。
[搭] swoosh past 嗖地掠过, swoosh of air 气流嗖声, with a swoosh 嗖的一声
[联] swish 嗖, whoosh 呼, rush 猛冲, arrow 箭, skateboard 滑板, sound 声响
[记] Nike Swoosh 指品牌标志；swoosh 本身为拟声词。与 whoosh 近义，swoosh 略短促

**clack**
英 /klæk/ 美 /klæk/
v. 咔嗒响
n. 咔嗒声
[义] To make a short sharp sound, especially hard objects hitting each other. 硬物相碰的短促「咔嗒」（heels clacked 鞋跟咔嗒，clack of typewriter keys 打字机键咔嗒）。比 click 更「硬、响、双物碰撞」。
[例] ① Her heels clacked on the marble floor. 她的鞋跟在大理石地面上咔嗒作响。 ② The old keyboard clacked loudly in the quiet office. 旧键盘在安静的办公室里咔嗒声很大。
[搭] clack of heels/keys 鞋跟/键咔嗒, clack together 咔嗒相碰, clack down the hall 咔嗒走过走廊
[联] click 咔嗒, clatter 哐当, heels 鞋跟, keyboard 键盘, snap 啪嗒, sound 声响
[记] clack 拟声；clackers 为玩具名。clack vs. click：clack 更硬、更响

**cackle**
英 /ˈkækl/ 美 /ˈkækl/
v. 咯咯怪笑；嘎嘎叫
n. 咯咯声
[义] To make a loud harsh laugh; the cry of a hen. 刺耳、尖利的笑声（cackle with laughter 咯咯怪笑）；或母鸡「咯咯」叫。几乎总含「尖、闹、略 unsettling」。
[例] ① The witch in the story cackled over her cauldron. 故事里的女巫对着大锅咯咯怪笑。 ② Hens cackled in the yard after laying eggs. 鸡下蛋后在院子里咯咯叫。
[搭] cackle with laughter 咯咯怪笑, witch's cackle 女巫式怪笑, hen cackled 母鸡咯咯
[联] giggle 咯咯笑, laugh 笑, hen 母鸡, harsh 刺耳的, witch 女巫, squawk 嘎嘎
[记] cackle 常含负面或漫画色彩。× cackle 形容 friendly laugh 不自然

**giggle**
英 /ˈɡɪɡl/ 美 /ˈɡɪɡl/
v. 咯咯笑
n. 咯咯笑声
[义] To laugh lightly and repeatedly in a silly way. 轻、短、反复的 silly 笑声——常少女、孩子、紧张时（giggle at a joke 听笑话咯咯笑，giggle nervously 紧张地咯咯笑）。比 chuckle 更「尖、碎、傻」。
[例] ① The girls giggled when he tripped on the step. 他在台阶上绊了一下，女孩们咯咯笑。 ② She tried to stop giggling during the serious meeting. 严肃会议上她努力憋住咯咯笑。
[搭] giggle at 对……咯咯笑, giggle nervously 紧张地咯咯笑, burst into giggles 爆出一阵咯咯笑
[联] chuckle 轻笑, laugh 笑, nervous 紧张的, silly 傻的, girl 女孩, joke 笑话
[记] giggle 可数/不可数；the giggles 口语「笑个不停」。giggle fit 笑到停不下来

**chuckle**
英 /ˈtʃʌkl/ 美 /ˈtʃʌkl/
v. 轻笑；低声笑
n. 轻笑声
[义] To laugh quietly, especially in amusement. 低声、克制而觉得好笑的轻笑（chuckle at the remark 对那句话轻笑，chuckle to oneself 暗自发笑）。比 giggle 更「低、暖、成人」；比 laugh 更轻。
[例] ① He chuckled when he read her text message. 读到她的短信，他轻笑一声。 ② "That figures," she chuckled, shaking her head. 「果然如此。」她摇摇头，低声笑了。
[搭] chuckle at 对……轻笑, chuckle to oneself 暗笑, soft chuckle 轻笑
[联] giggle 咯咯笑, laugh 笑, amused 觉得好笑的, mutter 低声说, smile 微笑, remark 话
[记] chuckle 几乎总 friendly。chuckle vs. giggle：chuckle 更低沉、少傻气

**snicker**
英 /ˈsnɪkə/ 美 /ˈsnɪkər/
v. 窃笑；暗笑
n. 窃笑声
[义] To laugh quietly in a disrespectful or mocking way. 带嘲弄、不敬的憋笑（snicker at sb.'s mistake 嘲笑某人出错）。比 giggle 更「坏、藏、讥」；英式亦 snigger。
[例] ① Students snickered when he mispronounced the word. 他把词念错了，学生们窃笑。 ② I heard snickers from the back row. 我听见后排传来暗笑声。
[搭] snicker at 对……窃笑, snicker behind sb.'s back 背后讥笑, stifle a snicker 憋住窃笑
[联] giggle 咯咯笑, mock 嘲笑, laugh 笑, disrespectful 不敬的, mistake 错误, whisper 低语
[记] snicker 常含 mock 义。Snickers 为巧克力品牌名，依语境区分

**guffaw**
英 /ɡəˈfɔː/ 美 /ɡəˈfɑː/
v. 哄堂大笑
n. 洪亮的笑声
[义] To laugh loudly and heartily. 大声、粗豪、停不住的笑（guffaw at a joke 听笑话哄堂大笑）。比 laugh 更强调「响、粗、whole-body」。
[例] ① The whole table guffawed at his imitation of the boss. 他模仿老板，整桌人哄堂大笑。 ② A loud guffaw came from the corner booth. 角落卡座传来一声洪亮的笑。
[搭] guffaw at 对……哄笑, loud guffaw 洪亮笑声, burst into guffaws 哄堂大笑
[联] laugh 笑, chuckle 轻笑, roar with laughter 大笑, joke 笑话, loud 大声的, hearty 畅快的
[记] guffaw 较书面/文学；口语 roar with laughter 更常见。几乎总 loud

**snuffle**
英 /ˈsnʌfl/ 美 /ˈsnʌfl/
v. 抽鼻子；哼着嗅
n. 抽鼻声
[义] To breathe noisily through the nose, especially when crying or having a cold. 鼻子不通或哭后「抽抽、哼」地呼吸（snuffle with a cold 感冒抽鼻子，dog snuffling 狗哼着嗅）。比 sniff 更「湿、堵、连续」。
[例] ① The child snuffled quietly after the argument. 吵完架，孩子低声抽着鼻子。 ② The dog snuffled around the base of the tree. 狗在树根处哼着嗅来嗅去。
[搭] snuffle with a cold 感冒抽鼻子, snuffle around 哼着嗅, quiet snuffle 轻轻抽鼻
[联] sniff 嗅, snivel 抽泣, cold 感冒, nose 鼻, dog 狗, cry 哭
[记] snuffle vs. sniff：snuffle 更「堵、湿、哭/感冒」；sniff 更主动嗅闻

**bang**
英 /bæŋ/ 美 /bæŋ/
n. 砰的一声
v. 砰地响；猛关
[义] A sudden loud noise; to make such a noise. 突然、响亮的「砰——」（door banged 门砰地关上，bang of a gun 枪响）。本章取听觉义；big bang、bang for buck 为另义。
[例] ① The door banged shut behind him in the wind. 风把他身后的门砰地带上。 ② We heard a loud bang from the kitchen. 我们听见厨房传来砰的一声。
[搭] bang shut 砰地关上, loud bang 巨响, bang on the door 砰砰敲门, with a bang 砰的一声
[联] thud 闷响, boom 轰隆, door 门, gun 枪, loud 大声的, crash 撞击
[记] bang 作副词口语：bang on（英式「完全正确」）。go with a bang 英式「大获成功」

**thwack**
英 /θwæk/ 美 /θwæk/
n. 啪的击打声
v. 啪地打
[义] A dull sound made by something hitting a surface hard; to hit with such a sound. 重物拍击的「啪/呼」声（thwack of a bat 球棒啪击，thwack the table 啪拍桌子）。拟声，比 smack 略闷。
[例] ① The book fell with a thwack onto the carpet. 书啪的一声落在地毯上。 ② He thwacked the mosquito on his arm. 他一巴掌啪地打死了胳膊上的蚊子。
[搭] thwack of a bat 球棒啪击, thwack onto 啪地落在, thwack the table 拍桌
[联] smack 啪打, thud 闷响, hit 打, bat 球棒, slap 掌掴, sound 声响
[记] thwack 拟声；漫画/体育描写常见。thwack vs. smack：thwack 更钝、更重

**bubble**
英 /ˈbʌbl/ 美 /ˈbʌbl/
n. 气泡；冒泡声
v. 冒泡；沸腾
[义] A ball of air in liquid; to rise and break with a soft sound. 液体里的气泡；液体「咕嘟、冒泡」的声响与视觉（water bubbling 水沸腾冒泡，bubble of laughter 阵阵笑声——喻）。本章取感官义；经济 bubble 为另义。
[例] ① Bubbles rose to the surface as the soup simmered. 汤小火煨着，气泡升到表面。 ② I love the sound of champagne bubbling in the glass. 我喜欢香槟在杯里嘶嘶冒泡的声音。
[搭] bubble up 冒上来, bubble of laughter 阵阵笑声, bubble wrap 气泡膜, champagne bubbling 香槟冒泡
[联] fizz 嘶嘶, gurgle 汩汩, boil 沸腾, liquid 液体, foam 泡沫, sound 声响
[记] bubble 可数；economic/housing bubble 为喻义「泡沫」。bubble bath 泡泡浴

**full-bodied**
英 /ˌfʊlˈbɒdid/ 美 /ˌfʊlˈbɑːdid/
adj. 醇厚的；浓郁的
[义] Rich and strong in taste or smell. 味道或香气「饱满、有厚度」（full-bodied wine 醇厚葡萄酒，full-bodied coffee 浓郁咖啡）。品酒、咖啡、茶常用；比 rich 更专业。
[例] ① She prefers a full-bodied red with grilled meat. 她配烤肉更爱醇厚红酒。 ② The full-bodied aroma filled the small café. 浓郁香气弥漫小咖啡馆。
[搭] full-bodied wine/coffee/tea 醇厚酒/咖啡/茶, full-bodied flavor 浓郁风味, full-bodied aroma 浓郁香气
[联] rich 浓郁的, aroma 香气, wine 葡萄酒, coffee 咖啡, flavor 风味, taste 味道
[记] full-bodied 几乎只修饰饮品与香气。反义 light-bodied / thin

**palatable**
英 /ˈpælətəbl/ 美 /ˈpælətəbl/
adj. 可口的；可接受的
[义] Pleasant to taste; acceptable to the mind. 味道可接受、不难吃（palatable meal 可口的饭）；喻「说得过去、可接受」（palatable compromise 可接受的妥协）。palate（上腭）+ -able。
[例] ① The hospital food was bland but palatable. 医院餐淡而无味，但还能吃。 ② Neither side found the proposal palatable. 双方都觉得这项提议难以接受。
[搭] palatable food/meal 可口食物/餐, palatable compromise 可接受妥协, barely palatable 勉强能入口
[联] tasty 好吃的, appetizing 开胃的, palate 上腭, acceptable 可接受的, bland 淡的, flavor 风味
[记] palatable 可指食物或方案。× palatable 不等于 delicious——仅「过得去」

**attuned**
英 /əˈtjuːnd/ 美 /əˈtuːnd/
adj. 敏锐的；协调的
[义] Able to understand or sense something; in harmony with. 对细微信号敏感（attuned to others' feelings 能体察他人感受，attuned ear 敏锐的耳朵）。常作 be attuned to；比 sensitive 更强调「已校准、能接收」。
[例] ① Good therapists are attuned to small shifts in tone. 优秀治疗师对语气细微变化很敏锐。 ② Musicians must stay attuned to each other on stage. 乐手在台上须彼此默契听感协调。
[搭] attuned to 对……敏锐/协调, finely attuned 高度敏锐, attuned ear 敏锐听觉
[联] sensitive 敏感的, receptive 乐于接受的, aware 意识到的, tone 语气, feelings 感受, harmony 和谐
[记] attuned to 为固定框架；动词 attune（使协调）。attuned vs. attentive：attuned 偏「感官/情感同频」

**vigilant**
英 /ˈvɪdʒɪlənt/ 美 /ˈvɪdʒɪlənt/
adj. 警惕的；警觉的
[义] Keeping careful watch for danger or difficulty. 保持警觉、留意异常（remain vigilant 保持警惕，vigilant guard 警觉的守卫）。比 watchful 更正式、更强调「防险」。
[例] ① Residents were urged to remain vigilant after the burglaries. 入室盗窃后，居民被呼吁保持警惕。 ② She stayed vigilant for any sign of infection. 她对任何感染迹象保持警觉。
[搭] remain/stay vigilant 保持警惕, vigilant about 对……警觉, vigilant guard 警觉守卫
[联] watchful 留神的, alert 警觉的, careful 小心的, danger 危险, guard 守卫, notice 注意到
[记] vigilance 名词；vigil 守夜。vigilant 常修饰 guard, citizen, parent

**watchful**
英 /ˈwɒtʃfl/ 美 /ˈwɑːtʃfl/
adj. 留神的；警惕的
[义] Paying attention to what is happening in order to notice anything unusual. 留心观察、不松懈（watchful eye 留神的目光，watchful parents 盯得紧的父母）。比 vigilant 略日常；比 attentive 更偏「眼看、守」。
[例] ① Under her watchful eye, the children played safely. 在她留神看着下，孩子们安全地玩。 ② Investors remain watchful for signs of inflation. 投资者仍留神通胀迹象。
[搭] watchful eye 留神目光, remain watchful 保持留神, watchful parents 盯得紧的父母
[联] vigilant 警惕的, attentive 专心的, eye 眼睛, careful 小心的, observe 观察, parent 父母
[记] keep a watchful eye on 固定短语。watchful vs. vigilant：watchful 更日常

**unnoticeable**
英 /ʌnˈnəʊtɪsəbl/ 美 /ʌnˈnoʊtɪsəbl/
adj. 难以察觉的
[义] Not easy to see, hear, or notice. 几乎感觉不到或看不见（unnoticeable change 难以察觉的变化，unnoticeable scar 不明显的疤）。un- + noticeable；与 imperceptible 近义，unnoticeable 更口语。
[例] ① The scar was almost unnoticeable after a year. 一年后疤痕几乎看不出来。 ② There was an unnoticeable lag in the audio stream. 音频流里有几乎听不出的延迟。
[搭] almost unnoticeable 几乎察觉不到, unnoticeable change/difference 难以察觉的变化/差异, unnoticeable to 对……不明显
[联] noticeable 明显的, imperceptible 难以察觉的, subtle 细微的, hidden 隐藏的, change 变化, scar 疤
[记] unnoticeable 可修饰 change, difference, delay, scar。反义 noticeable 见本章

**observant**
英 /əbˈzɜːvənt/ 美 /əbˈzɜːrvənt/
adj. 观察力强的；严守习俗的
[义] Good at noticing things; obeying religious rules (religious sense). 感官与注意力敏锐（observant reader 观察力强的读者，observant child 眼尖的孩子）。本章取感知义；宗教义 observant Muslim 另属文化专名。
[例] ① An observant customer spotted the typo on the menu. 一位眼尖的顾客发现菜单上的拼写错误。 ② She is remarkably observant about changes in people's moods. 她对他人情绪变化观察极细。
[搭] observant reader/customer 观察力强的读者/顾客, remarkably observant 观察力极强, observant of detail 留意细节
[联] perceptive 敏锐的, attentive 专心的, notice 注意到, detail 细节, sharp-eyed 眼尖的, reader 读者
[记] observant vs. observant（宗教）：religious observance 指「守礼」。日常义 almost always 褒义

**drool**
英 /druːl/ 美 /druːl/
v. 流口水
n. 口水
[义] To let saliva fall from the mouth; saliva that does so. 唾液从嘴角流下（baby drooling 宝宝流口水，drool over food 看食物流口水）。口语/儿语感；比 salivate 更 casual。
[例] ① The baby drooled on my shoulder during the nap. 午睡时宝宝口水流我肩上。 ② Don't drool over desserts you can't afford. 别对买不起的甜点垂涎三尺。
[搭] drool over 对……流口水/垂涎, baby drooling 宝宝流口水, drool on 口水流到……上
[联] saliva 唾液, mouth 嘴, baby 婴儿, hunger 饥饿, food 食物, appetizing 开胃的
[记] drool over 喻「垂涎」极口语。salivate 较正式/医学

**choke**
英 /tʃəʊk/ 美 /tʃoʊk/
v. 窒息；呛；哽咽
n. 窒息；扼喉
[义] To be unable to breathe because something blocks the throat; to fail to speak smoothly because of emotion. 气管被堵「喘不上、呛」（choke on food 噎着，smoke made me choke 烟呛人）；或 emotion 导致「哽咽说不出」（choke up 哽咽）。强触觉/呼吸感知。
[例] ① He choked on a piece of bread and needed help. 他被一块面包噎住，需要帮忙。 ② She choked up when she thanked her parents. 她感谢父母时哽咽了。
[搭] choke on 被……噎/呛, choke up 哽咽, choke with smoke 烟呛, almost choke 差点窒息
[联] gag 作呕, cough 咳嗽, breathe 呼吸, smoke 烟, food 食物, emotion 情绪
[记] choke up 表「哽咽」；choke off 表「掐断」。choke 名词：choke on a grape

**gag**
英 /ɡæɡ/ 美 /ɡæɡ/
v. 作呕；塞住嘴
n. 呕吐反射；噱头
[义] To feel nausea or retch; to stop someone speaking by blocking the mouth. 恶心到干呕（gag at the smell 闻到作呕）；或物/手「堵嘴」（gag reflex 咽反射）。本章取生理恶心义；笑话 gag 为另义「噱头」。
[例] ① The smell of the dumpster made me gag. 垃圾桶的气味让我干呕。 ② He gagged when the dentist put the mirror too far back. 牙医把镜子伸太里，他一阵干呕。
[搭] gag at/on 因……作呕, gag reflex 咽反射, make sb. gag 让某人作呕
[联] choke 窒息, nauseous 恶心的, smell 气味, vomit 呕吐, reflex 反射, retch 干呕
[记] gag reflex 医学常用。stand-up gag 指「笑话包袱」，依语境区分

**scalding**
英 /ˈskɔːldɪŋ/ 美 /ˈskɔːldɪŋ/
adj. 滚烫的；灼人的
[义] Hot enough to burn; very hot. 热到烫伤（scalding water 滚烫的水，scalding coffee 烫嘴咖啡）。scald 的现在分词形容词；比 hot 更强调「会烫伤、刺痛」。
[例] ① Be careful—the tea is scalding. 小心，茶烫得很。 ② Scalding steam rose from the broken pipe. 破管子里升起灼人蒸汽。
[搭] scalding water/steam 滚烫水/蒸汽, scalding hot 滚烫, scalding coffee 烫嘴咖啡
[联] burn 烫伤, hot 烫, steam 蒸汽, scald 烫伤, boiling 沸腾, pain 痛
[记] scalding 可修饰 water, steam, coffee, criticism（喻「灼人言辞」）。动词 scald

**clammy**
英 /ˈklæmi/ 美 /ˈklæmi/
adj. 湿冷黏的
[义] Unpleasantly damp and sticky, especially on skin. 皮肤又湿又冷又黏（clammy hands 湿冷黏的手，clammy sweat 又冷又黏的汗）。典型恐惧、生病、闷热后触感。
[例] ① His palms were clammy before the interview. 面试前他手心又湿又冷。 ② The cave walls felt clammy to the touch. 洞穴石壁摸上去湿冷黏滑。
[搭] clammy hands/skin 湿冷黏的手/皮肤, clammy sweat 黏冷汗, feel clammy 摸起来湿冷
[联] sweaty 汗湿的, damp 潮湿的, sticky 黏的, cold 冷的, fear 恐惧, skin 皮肤
[记] clammy 几乎总负面。clammy vs. sweaty：clammy 强调「冷+黏」

**belch**
英 /beltʃ/ 美 /beltʃ/
v. 打嗝；喷出
n. 饱嗝
[义] To send gas noisily from the stomach through the mouth; to force out smoke, flames, etc. 从胃里响亮地返气（belch loudly 大声打嗝）；火山、烟囱等「喷出」（belch smoke 喷烟）。比 burp 略粗、略正式；burp 美式日常更常见。
[例] ① He belched after the heavy meal and apologized. 大餐后他打了个嗝并道了歉。 ② The factory belched black smoke into the sky. 工厂向天空喷出黑烟。
[搭] belch loudly 大声打嗝, belch smoke/fumes 喷烟/废气, volcanic belch 火山喷发
[联] burp 打嗝, stomach 胃, gas 气体, smoke 烟, rude 无礼, hiccup 膈肌嗝
[记] belch 兼指人体与火山/烟囱。礼貌场合用 burp 或 excuse me 更自然

**huff**
英 /hʌf/ 美 /hʌf/
v. 喷气；怒气冲冲
n. 喷气声；一阵气
[义] To breathe out loudly, especially when annoyed or tired; to puff. 因生气或劳累而粗重呼气（huff and puff 呼哧呼哧）；名词指短促喷气或「闹脾气」（in a huff 气呼呼地）。本章取呼吸/喷气感知义。
[例] ① She huffed up the stairs with the heavy suitcase. 她拖着沉重行李箱呼哧呼哧爬上楼梯。 ② He left in a huff after the argument. 吵完架他气呼呼地走了。
[搭] huff and puff 呼哧呼哧, in a huff 气呼呼, huff out 喷气走出
[联] puff 喷, pant 喘气, breath 呼吸, angry 生气, tired 累, sigh 叹气
[记] huff and puff 童话《三只小猪》wolf 经典台词。in a huff 表闹情绪离开

**ahem**
英 /əˈhem/ 美 /əˈhem/
interj. 咳咳（清嗓）
[义] A sound made to attract attention or suggest embarrassment, often by clearing the throat. 清嗓子或假咳以引人注意、暗示尴尬或「我有话要说」（Ahem, I think you forgot… 咳咳，我想你忘了……）。
[例] ① Ahem—could everyone look this way, please? 咳咳——请大家看这边好吗？ ② She said, "Ahem," and waited for him to stop talking. 她「咳咳」一声，等他停嘴。
[搭] ahem to get attention 咳咳引人注意, say ahem 咳一声, ahem politely 礼貌清嗓
[联] cough 咳嗽, throat 喉咙, attention 注意, embarrassed 尴尬, interrupt 打断, clear 清
[记] ahem 写作常带引号或逗号。比 cough 更轻、更「社交信号」

**phew**
英 /fjuː/ 美 /fjuː/
interj. 唷；总算
[义] An exclamation of relief, tiredness, or surprise at something difficult or close. 松一口气（Phew, that was close 唷，好险）、劳累或如释重负（Phew, I'm glad that's over 总算结束了）。
[例] ① Phew—it stopped raining just before the ceremony started. 唷——仪式开始前雨刚好停了。 ② Phew, I thought I'd lost my keys. 吓我一跳，还以为钥匙丢了。
[搭] phew what a relief 唷总算松口气, phew that was close 好险, phew I'm tired 唷好累
[联] sigh 叹气, relief 松口气, tired 累, close 险, glad 高兴, whew 类似感叹
[记] phew / whew 近义，表如释重负。常独立成句，口语极频

**ouch**
英 /aʊtʃ/ 美 /aʊtʃ/
interj. 哎哟
[义] A cry of pain or sympathy when you or someone else is hurt. 自己或他人受伤时的痛呼（Ouch! That hurt 哎哟！好痛）；也可对别人的痛表示同情（Ouch, that looks painful 哎哟，看着好疼）。
[例] ① Ouch—I just stubbed my toe on the table leg. 哎哟——脚趾撞桌腿了。 ② "Ouch," she said when the nurse cleaned the cut. 护士清理伤口时她说了一声「哎哟」。
[搭] cry ouch 喊哎哟, ouch that hurts 哎哟好痛, say ouch 叫一声哎哟
[联] pain 痛, hurt 受伤, yelp 痛叫, wince 龇牙, sting 刺痛, toe 脚趾
[记] ouch 几乎专表即时痛觉反应。比 ow 略常见

**smack**
英 /smæk/ 美 /smæk/
v. 啪地响；咂嘴
n. 啪声；咂嘴声
[义] To make a sharp sound by hitting something; to part the lips noisily. 清脆击打声（smack into the wall 砰地撞上墙）；或嘴唇「吧唧」响（smack your lips 咂嘴）。本章取声响与咂嘴感知；掌掴动作见基本动作章 slap。
[例] ① The branch smacked against the window in the wind. 风把树枝拍得窗户啪啪响。 ② He smacked his lips after tasting the soup. 尝了口汤，他咂了咂嘴。
[搭] smack into/against 砰地撞上, smack your lips 咂嘴, with a smack 啪一声
[联] slap 掌掴, bang 砰, lips 嘴唇, taste 品尝, crash 撞, loud 响
[记] smack of 另有「带有……意味」；smack dab 美式「正好」。咂嘴表享受味道

**snarl**
英 /snɑːl/ 美 /snɑːrl/
v. 咆哮；吼叫
n. 咆哮声
[义] To make an angry, deep, rough sound while showing teeth; to speak angrily. 露齿发出低沉怒吼（dog snarled 狗龇牙低吼）；或人厉声说话（snarl at sb. 冲某人吼）。比 growl 更尖、更怒。
[例] ① The dog snarled when strangers approached the gate. 陌生人靠近大门时，狗龇牙低吼。 ② "Get out of my way," he snarled. 「滚开，」他吼道。
[搭] snarl at 冲……咆哮, snarl with rage 怒吼, traffic snarl 交通堵塞（另义）
[联] growl 低吼, bark 吠, angry 愤怒, dog 狗, teeth 牙, roar 吼
[记] traffic snarl 指「交通缠结/堵塞」，与咆哮声另义。snarl vs. growl：snarl 更露齿、更凶

**shriek**
英 /ʃriːk/ 美 /ʃriːk/
v. 尖叫
n. 尖叫声
[义] To give a loud, high, piercing cry of fear, pain, or excitement. 因恐惧、疼痛或兴奋发出的尖厉高声（shriek with laughter 尖声大笑；shriek in terror 惊恐尖叫）。比 scream 更强调音高与刺耳。
[例] ① She shrieked when the mouse ran across the floor. 老鼠窜过地板，她尖叫起来。 ② Brakes shrieked as the bus skidded to a stop. 公交打滑刹停时，刹车尖啸。
[搭] shriek with laughter/terror 尖笑/惊恐尖叫, shriek in pain 痛得尖叫, ear-piercing shriek 刺耳尖叫
[联] scream 尖叫, piercing 刺耳的, shrill 尖厉, terror 恐惧, brakes 刹车, loud 响
[记] shriek 可修饰 brakes, sirens, wind。行为章有 scream；本章侧重刺耳声感

**sob**
英 /sɒb/ 美 /sɑːb/
v. 抽泣；哽咽
n. 抽泣声
[义] To cry noisily while taking short, sudden breaths. 一边短促吸气一边出声哭（sob into a pillow 伏枕抽泣）。名词 a sob 指一声抽噎。
[例] ① She sobbed quietly in the back row during the film. 看电影时她在后排轻轻抽泣。 ② A sob escaped him before he could finish the sentence. 话没说完，他漏出一声抽噎。
[搭] sob quietly/loudly 轻轻/大声抽泣, sob into 伏在……上哭, stifle a sob 忍住抽泣
[联] weep 哭泣, cry 哭, tear 泪, pillow 枕头, grief 悲伤, whimper 呜咽
[记] sob story 指「卖惨故事」。sob 强调听得见的抽噎，比 cry 更具体

**sniffle**
英 /ˈsnɪfl/ 美 /ˈsnɪfl/
v. 吸鼻子；抽鼻子
n. 吸鼻声
[义] To make repeated sniffing sounds, especially when you have a cold or are crying. 感冒或哭后反复吸鼻子（sniffle all day 整天吸溜鼻子）；the sniffles 口语指「小感冒」。
[例] ① The child sniffled and wiped his nose on his sleeve. 孩子吸了吸鼻子，用袖子擦。 ② I've got the sniffles, so I'll work from home. 有点感冒流鼻涕，我在家办公。
[搭] sniffle quietly 轻轻吸鼻, have the sniffles 有点感冒, stop sniffling 别吸鼻子了
[联] sniff 嗅, nose 鼻子, cold 感冒, tissue 纸巾, runny 流鼻涕, cry 哭
[记] the sniffles 可数复数形表轻症感冒。sniffle vs. sniff：sniffle 反复、带鼻涕

**flushed**
英 /flʌʃt/ 美 /flʌʃt/
adj. 涨红的；兴奋的
[义] Red in the face from heat, effort, embarrassment, or illness. 因热、运动、尴尬或发烧而脸红（flushed cheeks 红扑扑的脸颊；flushed with embarrassment 羞得满脸通红）。flush 的过去分词作形容词。
[例] ① Her cheeks were flushed after the run. 跑完步她双颊通红。 ② He looked flushed and avoided eye contact. 他一脸通红，不敢对视。
[搭] flushed cheeks/face 涨红的脸, flushed with embarrassment/excitement 羞/兴奋得脸红, look flushed 看起来脸红
[联] blush 脸红, red 红, heat 热, embarrassed 尴尬, fever 发烧, cheek 脸颊
[记] flushed with success 喻「因成功而飘飘然」。勿与 flush the toilet 混淆

**ashen**
英 /ˈæʃən/ 美 /ˈæʃən/
adj. 灰白的；惨白的
[义] Very pale, especially with shock, fear, or illness. 因惊吓、恐惧或疾病而面色灰白（ashen face 惨白的脸；turn ashen 脸色煞白）。比 pale 更强调「失去血色、像灰」。
[例] ① His face went ashen when he heard the news. 听到消息，他脸色煞白。 ② She had an ashen complexion after the fever broke. 退烧后她脸色仍惨白。
[搭] ashen face/complexion 惨白的脸/面色, turn/go ashen 变得惨白, ashen with fear 吓得灰白
[联] pale 苍白, gray 灰, shock 震惊, fear 恐惧, sick 病, bloodless 无血色
[记] ashen 源自 ash（灰）。ashen vs. pale：ashen 更戏剧、更「吓坏/病倒」

**sweaty**
英 /ˈsweti/ 美 /ˈsweti/
adj. 汗湿的；闷热的
[义] Covered with sweat or causing you to sweat. 满是汗或让人出汗（sweaty palms 汗湿的手心；sweaty weather 闷热天）。生理章有 sweat 名词/动词；本章取皮肤与环境的汗湿感知。
[例] ① His shirt was sweaty after an hour in the gym. 健身房待了一小时，衬衫汗湿了。 ② I hate sweaty summer nights without air conditioning. 我讨厌没空调的闷热夏夜。
[搭] sweaty palms/hands 汗湿的手, sweaty shirt 汗衫, hot and sweaty 又热又汗
[联] sweat 汗, clammy 湿冷黏, damp 潮湿, gym 健身, heat 热, sticky 黏
[记] sweaty 可形容人、衣服、天气、手心。break into a sweat 短语表「开始出汗」

**burning**
英 /ˈbɜːnɪŋ/ 美 /ˈbɜːrnɪŋ/
adj. 灼热的；火烧般的
[义] Feeling or producing very great heat or pain on the skin or in the body. 皮肤或体内「火烧火燎」（burning sensation 灼烧感；burning eyes 辣眼）。burn 的现在分词形容词；比 hot 更强调刺痛。
[例] ① She felt a burning pain in her sunburnt shoulders. 晒伤的双肩一阵灼痛。 ② The chili left a burning taste on my tongue. 辣椒在舌头上留下火辣感。
[搭] burning sensation/pain 灼烧感/灼痛, burning eyes 辣眼, burning taste 火辣味
[联] sting 刺痛, hot 烫, scald 烫伤, chili 辣椒, sunburn 晒伤, numb 麻木
[记] burning question 喻「亟待回答的问题」，与触觉无关。burning vs. scalding：burning 可喻持续辣/痛

**tepid**
英 /ˈtepɪd/ 美 /ˈtepɪd/
adj. 温吞的；不冷不热
[义] Slightly warm, especially in a disappointing way; lacking enthusiasm (tepid response). 温度「温吞、不够热」（tepid water 温吞水；tepid bath 不够热的澡）。常带轻微不满——该热的不热、该冷的太温。
[例] ① The tea had gone tepid while she was on the phone. 她打电话时茶已经温吞了。 ② Tepid applause followed the short speech. 短演讲后响起稀稀拉拉的掌声。
[搭] tepid water/tea 温吞水/茶, tepid bath/shower 不够热的澡, tepid response 温吞反应
[联] lukewarm 不冷不热, warm 温, cold 冷, bath 澡, disappointment 失望, flat 平淡
[记] tepid 几乎总略负面。tepid vs. lukewarm：tepid 更书面，lukewarm 更口语

**rank**
英 /ræŋk/ 美 /ræŋk/
adj. 恶臭的；蔓生的
[义] Having a strong, unpleasant smell or taste. 气味或味道「恶臭扑鼻」（rank smell 恶臭；rank odour 臭气）。比较章 rank 作「排名」；本章只收嗅觉/味觉形容词义。
[例] ① The rank smell of rotting fish filled the alley. 巷子里弥漫着烂鱼的恶臭。 ② Weeds grew rank after the heavy rains. 大雨后杂草疯长（rank 另义「蔓生」）。
[搭] rank smell/odour/stench 恶臭, smell rank 闻起来臭, rank weeds 蔓生杂草
[联] fetid 恶臭, stench 臭气, rotten 腐烂, reek 发臭, foul 恶臭的, putrid 腐臭
[记] rank 排名义见比较/职业章。本章 rank smell 固定搭配。rank weeds 为植物「疯长」

**pop**
英 /pɒp/ 美 /pɑːp/
v. 啪地响；爆开
n. 啪声；爆裂声
[义] To make a short, sharp sound when something bursts or opens suddenly. 突然破裂或打开时的短促脆响（pop a balloon 扎爆气球；cork popped 软木塞砰地弹出）。本章取声响义；流行音乐 pop music 另条。
[例] ① We heard a pop and the lights went out. 我们听到「啪」一声，灯灭了。 ② The champagne cork popped across the room. 香槟软木塞砰地飞过房间。
[搭] pop a balloon/cork 爆气球/开瓶塞, with a pop 啪一声, pop and fizz 噼啪冒泡
[联] bang 砰, crack 噼啪, balloon 气球, cork 瓶塞, fizz 嘶嘶, burst 爆裂
[记] pop 多义极多（汽水、流行、突然出现）。听语境：a pop = 一声脆响

**lifelike**
英 /ˈlaɪflaɪk/ 美 /ˈlaɪflaɪk/
adj. 栩栩如生的
[义] Looking or seeming exactly like a real person or thing. 看上去几乎跟真的一样（lifelike doll 逼真娃娃；lifelike portrait 栩栩如生的肖像）。强调视觉「像活的/像真的」。
[例] ① The museum's lifelike wax figures startled visitors. 博物馆栩栩如生的蜡像把参观者吓了一跳。 ② The CGI looked surprisingly lifelike on the big screen. 大银幕上特效出奇地逼真。
[搭] lifelike doll/portrait 逼真娃娃/肖像, surprisingly lifelike 出奇逼真, look lifelike 看起来栩栩如生
[联] realistic 逼真的, vivid 生动, portrait 肖像, wax 蜡, doll 娃娃, detail 细节
[记] lifelike 偏「像有生命」；realistic 偏「符合现实」。属性章 vivid 强调色彩/印象

**shadowy**
英 /ˈʃædəʊi/ 美 /ˈʃædoʊi/
adj. 阴影里的；朦胧的
[义] Full of shadows; dark and unclear; only partly known. 光线暗、多阴影（shadowy alley 阴暗小巷）；或轮廓/身份「朦胧」（shadowy figure 模糊人影）。本章取视觉明暗感知。
[例] ① A shadowy figure stood at the end of the corridor. 走廊尽头有个模糊人影。 ② We could only make out shadowy shapes in the fog. 雾里只能辨出朦胧轮廓。
[搭] shadowy figure/alley 模糊人影/阴暗巷, shadowy outline 朦胧轮廓, remain shadowy 仍然模糊
[联] shadow 影, dim 昏暗, dark 暗, vague 模糊, fog 雾, silhouette 剪影
[记] shadowy 可喻「神秘、说不清」（shadowy organization）。勿与 shady（可疑的）混淆

**washed-out**
英 /ˌwɒʃt ˈaʊt/ 美 /ˌwɑːʃt ˈaʊt/
adj. 褪色的；气色差的
[义] Faded in colour; pale and tired-looking. 颜色被洗褪、发灰（washed-out jeans 褪色牛仔裤）；或人气色「发灰、没精神」（look washed-out 气色很差）。复合形容词，常作表语。
[例] ① The photo looked washed-out after years in the sun. 照片日晒多年后颜色发灰。 ② She looked washed-out after three nights without sleep. 三夜没睡，她气色很差。
[搭] washed-out colours/jeans 褪色, look washed-out 气色差, washed-out face 灰扑扑的脸
[联] pale 苍白, faded 褪色, tired 累, grey 灰, sun 日晒, jeans 牛仔裤
[记] washed-out 视觉与气色两用。washed out 另可作动词短语「精疲力尽」

**sickly**
英 /ˈsɪkli/ 美 /ˈsɪkli/
adj. 令人生厌的；病弱的
[义] Unpleasantly sweet, pale, or suggesting illness. 甜得发腻（sickly sweet 甜得发齁）；或颜色惨白、气色病弱（sickly green 病态绿）。比 sweet 更强调「过甜、令人不适」的味觉/视觉感知。
[例] ① The room was painted a sickly shade of yellow. 房间刷成一种令人发腻的黄色。 ② A sickly smell of cheap perfume hung in the air. 廉价香水味腻在空气里，令人不适。
[搭] sickly sweet 甜得发腻, sickly colour/pallor 病态色/苍白, sickly smell 令人作呕的气味
[联] sweet 甜, pale 苍白, nauseating 令人作呕, green 绿, perfume 香水, weak 虚弱
[记] sickly 几乎总负面。sickly sweet 固定搭配极常见

**tearful**
英 /ˈtɪəfəl/ 美 /ˈtɪrfəl/
adj. 含泪的；易哭的
[义] Crying or about to cry; full of tears. 眼眶含泪或易掉泪（tearful eyes 含泪双眼；tearful voice 带哭腔）。比 teary 更偏「整段状态、场合」；teary 多指眼睛本身。
[例] ① She gave a tearful speech at the farewell party. 欢送会上她含泪致辞。 ② The child looked tearful but tried not to sob. 孩子眼看要哭，却努力忍住抽泣。
[搭] tearful eyes/voice 含泪眼/带哭腔, tearful goodbye 含泪告别, look tearful 看起来要哭
[联] teary 泪汪汪, cry 哭, sob 抽泣, weep 哭泣, eyes 眼, voice 声音
[记] tearful vs. teary：tearful 可修饰 speech, goodbye；teary 多修饰 eyes

**realistic**
英 /ˌriːəˈlɪstɪk/ 美 /ˌriːəˈlɪstɪk/
adj. 逼真的；符合实际的
[义] Looking or behaving like real life; accurate and believable. 看上去像真的、可信（realistic painting 写实画）；或预期「符合现实」（realistic expectations 切合实际的预期）。本章侧重视觉/听感逼真；lifelike 偏「像活物」。
[例] ① The sound effects in the game are surprisingly realistic. 游戏音效出奇逼真。 ② Is that a photo or a realistic drawing? 那是照片还是写实画？
[搭] realistic expectations 切合实际, realistic sound/painting 逼真音效/画, look realistic 看起来逼真
[联] lifelike 栩栩如生, vivid 生动, believable 可信, drawing 画, sound 声音, accurate 准确
[记] realistic 也修饰目标、预算等抽象义。realistic vs. lifelike：前者偏「可信、写实」

**sputter**
英 /ˈspʌtə(r)/ 美 /ˈspʌtər/
v. 噼啪喷溅；结巴地说
n. 噼啪声
[义] To make a series of soft explosive sounds, as an engine starting or dying; to speak in bursts. 引擎等「噼里啪啦」断续响（engine sputtered 引擎突突响）；或话「断断续续、语无伦次」（sputter an apology 结结巴巴道歉）。本章取声响感知。
[例] ① The old motorbike sputtered and then died. 旧摩托突突几声，熄火了。 ② Rain sputtered against the tin roof. 雨点噼啪打在铁皮屋顶上。
[搭] sputter to a stop 突突几声停下, engine sputters 引擎突突响, sputter out 渐渐熄灭
[联] splutter 噼啪喷, cough 咳嗽, engine 引擎, spark 火花, rain 雨, die 熄火
[记] sputter vs. splutter：sputter 偏引擎/火花；splutter 偏液体喷溅、说话喷口水

**splutter**
英 /ˈsplʌtə(r)/ 美 /ˈsplʌtər/
v. 喷溅；气急败坏地说
n. 喷溅声
[义] To make wet spitting sounds; to speak angrily or confusedly while almost choking. 液体或口水「噗噗」喷溅（splutter oil 油花四溅）；或人气急败坏、几乎呛到地说话（splutter with rage 气得语无伦次）。
[例] ① Bacon spluttered in the hot pan. 培根在热锅里噼啪喷油。 ② "That's—that's ridiculous!" he spluttered. 「那——那太荒唐了！」他气急败坏地说。
[搭] splutter with rage/indignation 气得/愤然语无伦次, splutter oil 油花四溅, splutter to a halt 噗噗几声停下
[联] sputter 突突响, spit 喷, rage 愤怒, oil 油, pan 锅, choke 呛
[记] splutter 常带 anger, indignation。与 sputter 拼近义近，依主语区分

**ding**
英 /dɪŋ/ 美 /dɪŋ/
v. 叮当作响
n. 叮当声
[义] To make a short ringing sound, like a small bell or microwave. 短促清脆的「叮」声（microwave dinged 微波炉叮一声；elevator ding 电梯到层铃响）。
[例] ① The timer dinged and she pulled the tray from the oven. 定时器叮一声，她把烤盘取出。 ② We heard a ding as the lift reached our floor. 电梯到我们楼层时叮响一声。
[搭] ding like a bell 如铃叮响, microwave/timer dings 微波炉/定时器叮, elevator ding 电梯铃
[联] ping  ping声, tinkle 叮当, bell 铃, microwave 微波炉, chime 鸣钟, ring 响
[记] ding 拟声；美式 elevator ding 极日常。ding dong 门铃双音节

**ping**
英 /pɪŋ/ 美 /pɪŋ/
v. 发 ping 声；尖啸而过
n. ping 声
[义] To make a short high ringing or clicking sound. 短促尖细的「 ping」——电子提示（phone pinged 手机叮一声）、雷达/声纳、或子弹飞过（bullet pinged 子弹啸过）。比 ding 更尖、更电子感。
[例] ① My phone pinged with a new message. 手机叮一声，来了新消息。 ② Something pinged off the metal fence. 有东西铛一声打在铁栅栏上。
[搭] phone/computer pings 手机/电脑提示音, ping off 弹开/击中, sonar ping 声纳脉冲
[联] ding 叮, beep 哔, notification 通知, sonar 声纳, message 消息, click 咔嗒
[记] ping 作名词也指网络延迟测试（latency ping）。听语境区分

**dank**
英 /dæŋk/ 美 /dæŋk/
adj. 阴湿臭的
[义] Unpleasantly damp, cold, and often smelling of mould. 又湿又冷、常带霉味（dank cellar 阴湿地下室；dank smell 潮霉气）。比 damp 更强调「闷、臭、地下室感」。
[例] ① A dank smell rose from the flooded basement. 淹水的地下室升起一股潮霉味。 ② We stepped into the dank corridor behind the stage. 我们走进舞台后阴湿走廊。
[搭] dank smell/air 潮霉气, dank cellar/basement 阴湿地窖, dank and cold 又潮又冷
[联] musty 霉味, mouldy 发霉, damp 潮湿, cellar 地窖, smell 气味, cold 冷
[记] dank meme 为网络俚语，与潮湿义无关。dank vs. musty：dank 更湿冷

**mouldy**
英 /ˈməʊldi/ 美 /ˈmoʊldi/
adj. 发霉的
[义] Covered with mould; tasting or smelling of mould. 长霉或带霉味（mouldy bread 发霉面包；mouldy smell 霉味）。美式拼写 moldy；musty 偏「陈腐潮气」，mouldy 强调「可见霉菌」。
[例] ① She threw away the mouldy cheese at the back of the fridge. 她把冰箱后面发霉的奶酪扔了。 ② The room had a faint mouldy odour after the leak. 漏水后房间有淡淡霉味。
[搭] mouldy bread/cheese 发霉面包/奶酪, mouldy smell 霉味, go mouldy 发霉
[联] musty 霉腐, rotten 腐烂, bread 面包, smell 气味, fridge 冰箱, damp 潮
[记] 美式 mold / moldy。mouldy vs. stale：stale 偏「不新鲜」；mouldy 有菌丝

**scented**
英 /ˈsentɪd/ 美 /ˈsentɪd/
adj. 有香味的
[义] Having a pleasant smell added or naturally present. 带人工或天然香气（scented candle 香薰蜡烛；scented soap 香皂）。与 odorless、scentless 相对；fragrant 偏「天然芬芳」。
[例] ① She lit a scented candle before guests arrived. 客人来前她点了香薰蜡烛。 ② The brochure promised scented roses in every room. 宣传册说每间屋都有香玫瑰。
[搭] scented candle/soap 香薰蜡烛/香皂, heavily scented 浓香, lightly scented 淡香
[联] fragrant 芬芳, odorless 无味的, scentless 无气味的, candle 蜡烛, perfume 香水, smell 气味
[记] scented 常指「人为加香」。无香产品常说 unscented、odorless

**weep**
英 /wiːp/ 美 /wiːp/
v. 哭泣；渗出
n. 哭泣
[义] To cry tears; to produce liquid slowly from a wound or plant. 流泪、哭泣（weep silently 默默流泪）；或伤口、树木「渗液」（eyes weeping 眼睛流泪；wall weeping damp 墙面渗潮）。比 sob 更静、更久。
[例] ① She wept with relief when she heard he was safe. 听说他平安，她喜极而泣。 ② Fresh paint wept down the window frame in the heat. 天热，新漆顺着窗框往下淌。
[搭] weep with joy/relief 喜极/如释重负而泣, weep silently 默默流泪, weep for 为……哭泣
[联] sob 抽泣, cry 哭, tear 泪, teary 泪汪汪, silent 安静, wound 伤口
[记] weep 可较正式文学（weep bitter tears）。weeping willow 垂柳。行为章无 weep 主条

**warbling**
英 /ˈwɔːblɪŋ/ 美 /ˈwɔːrblɪŋ/
adj. 婉转啼鸣的
v. 啭鸣；颤音歌唱
[义] Singing with a continuous change of pitch, especially birds. 音调起伏的婉转鸣叫（warbling birds 啭鸣的鸟）；或人带颤音地唱。动物章有 chirp；warbling 强调「连续、悦耳、起伏」。
[例] ① We woke to the warbling of birds outside the window. 窗外鸟鸣啭转，把我们叫醒。 ② She warbled an old folk song at the party. 聚会上她颤着嗓子唱了支老民歌。
[搭] warbling birds 啭鸣的鸟, warble a tune 颤音唱, sweet warbling 悦耳啭鸣
[联] chirp 啁啾, sing 唱, bird 鸟, melody 旋律, trill 颤音, sweet 悦耳
[记] warble 作动词规则变化。warbling 几乎总褒义（鸟、歌）

**droning**
英 /ˈdrəʊnɪŋ/ 美 /ˈdroʊnɪŋ/
adj. 嗡嗡不断的
v. 嗡嗡响；单调地说
[义] Making a continuous low dull sound; speaking in a boring monotone. 低沉持续的嗡嗡声（droning engine 轰鸣引擎；droning voice 单调嗓音）。科技章 drone 指无人机；本章取声响义。
[例] ① The droning of the air conditioner kept me awake. 空调嗡嗡响，吵得我没睡着。 ② He listened to the lecturer's droning voice and nodded off. 讲师单调的嗓音听得他打起了瞌睡。
[搭] droning sound/voice/engine 嗡嗡声/单调嗓音/轰鸣, drone on 唠叨没完, droning in the background 背景嗡嗡
[联] hum 嗡嗡, monotone 单调, boring 无聊, engine 引擎, buzz 嗡嗡, lecture 讲座
[记] drone on 指「冗长唠叨」。勿与 drone 无人机条混淆

**slimy**
英 /ˈslaɪmi/ 美 /ˈslaɪmi/
adj. 黏滑腻的
[义] Unpleasantly wet, slippery, and sticky to the touch. 又湿又滑、摸着腻（slimy rock 滑腻的石头；slimy fish 黏滑的鱼）。常带轻微厌恶感。
[例] ① The steps were slimy with algae after the rain. 雨后台阶长满青苔，又滑又腻。 ② He picked up the slimy seaweed and dropped it quickly. 他抓起黏滑的海藻，又赶紧扔掉。
[搭] slimy surface/rock 滑腻面/石头, feel slimy 摸起来黏滑, slimy texture 黏滑质地
[联] slippery 滑, sticky 黏, wet 湿, algae 青苔, fish 鱼, touch 触
[记] slimy 可喻人「油滑、谄媚」（slimy politician）。触觉义与比喻义依语境区分

**surreal**
英 /səˈriːəl/ 美 /səˈriːəl/
adj. 超现实的；离奇的
[义] Strange in a dreamlike way; so bizarre that it feels unreal. 像梦一样离奇、不像真的（surreal experience 超现实经历；surreal landscape 怪诞景色）。视觉/整体感知「失真、梦幻」。
[例] ① Walking through the empty airport at midnight felt surreal. 午夜穿过空荡机场，感觉像在做梦。 ② The war footage had a surreal, nightmarish quality. 战争画面有一种噩梦般的超现实感。
[搭] surreal experience/moment 超现实经历/时刻, feel surreal 感觉不真实, surreal landscape 怪诞景色
[联] dreamlike 如梦, bizarre 离奇, unreal 不真实, nightmare 噩梦, strange 奇怪, vivid 生动
[记] surreal 源自 surrealism（超现实主义）。比 weird 更「像梦、像艺术」

**dreamlike**
英 /ˈdriːmlaɪk/ 美 /ˈdriːmlaɪk/
adj. 如梦的
[义] Having the quality of a dream; hazy and unreal. 朦胧、不真、像梦（dreamlike state 如梦状态；dreamlike quality 梦幻质感）。比 surreal 略柔、少「怪诞 shock」。
[例] ① The mist gave the lake a dreamlike beauty at dawn. 黎明薄雾给湖面一种如梦之美。 ② She remembered the accident in a dreamlike blur. 她对事故的记忆像一团梦幻般的模糊。
[搭] dreamlike quality/state 梦幻感/状态, dreamlike beauty 如梦之美, seem dreamlike 显得如梦
[联] surreal 超现实, hazy 朦胧, blur 模糊, mist 雾, beauty 美, sleep 睡
[记] dreamlike vs. surreal：dreamlike 可褒（美、柔）；surreal 常带「怪」

**eerie**
英 /ˈɪəri/ 美 /ˈɪri/
adj. 阴森诡异的
[义] Strange and frightening in a mysterious way. 神秘而让人发毛（eerie silence 阴森寂静；eerie light 诡异的光）。环境/氛围感知，常伴 quiet、empty、night。
[例] ① An eerie green light glowed from the abandoned house. 废弃房屋透出诡异绿光。 ② There was an eerie stillness before the storm. 暴风雨前有一种阴森的死寂。
[搭] eerie silence/light 阴森寂静/诡异光, eerie feeling 毛骨悚然, eerily quiet 静得诡异
[联] creepy 瘆人, spooky 阴森的, uncanny 怪异的, silence 寂静, night 夜, ghost 鬼
[记] eerily 副词「诡异地」。eerie 几乎总负面、带 mystery

**uncanny**
英 /ʌnˈkæni/ 美 /ʌnˈkæni/
adj. 怪异的；异乎寻常的
[义] Strange in an unsettling way, especially when something looks almost but not quite human or familiar. 熟悉又不对劲、令人不安（uncanny resemblance 惊人相似；uncanny valley 恐怖谷）。比 eerie 更强调「像真的又不像」。
[例] ① The robot's uncanny smile made visitors uneasy. 机器人那诡异的笑让参观者不自在。 ② She had an uncanny ability to guess what he was thinking. 她猜他心思准得离奇。
[搭] uncanny resemblance 惊人相似, uncanny valley 恐怖谷, uncanny ability 离奇本事
[联] eerie 阴森, creepy 瘆人, strange 奇怪, resemblance 相似, robot 机器人, unsettling 不安
[记] uncanny valley 指仿人机器「太像人又不够像」的不适感。也可褒（uncanny accuracy）

**goosebumps**
英 /ˈɡuːsbʌmps/ 美 /ˈɡuːsbʌmps/
n. 鸡皮疙瘩
[义] Small raised areas on the skin caused by cold, fear, or strong emotion. 因冷、怕或激动而起的鸡皮疙瘩（get goosebumps 起鸡皮疙瘩）。英式亦 goose pimples；几乎总复数。
[例] ① The horror film gave me goosebumps. 恐怖片看得我起鸡皮疙瘩。 ② A chill wind raised goosebumps on her arms. 一阵冷风冻得她胳膊起疙瘩。
[搭] get/have goosebumps 起鸡皮疙瘩, give sb. goosebumps 让人起鸡皮疙瘩, goosebumps on the skin 皮肤上鸡皮疙瘩
[联] shiver 发抖, cold 冷, fear 恐惧, horror 恐怖, skin 皮肤, chill 寒意
[记] 书名 Goosebumps 为儿童恐怖系列。英式 goose pimples 同义

**jarring**
英 /ˈdʒɑːrɪŋ/ 美 /ˈdʒɑːrɪŋ/
adj. 刺耳的；违和的
[义] Unpleasantly surprising or clashing; harsh to the senses. 感官上「突兀、刺耳、不协调」（jarring noise 刺耳噪音；jarring colour 扎眼配色）。jar 的现在分词形容词。
[例] ① The jarring alarm jolted everyone awake. 刺耳警报把所有人惊醒。 ② His casual tone was jarring after such sad news. 如此噩耗之后，他随便的语气很违和。
[搭] jarring noise/sound 刺耳声, jarring contrast 强烈反差, jarring colour 扎眼颜色
[联] grating 刺耳, harsh 刺耳, clash 冲突, alarm 警报, contrast 对比, loud 响
[记] jarring 可修饰 sound, colour, note, transition。比 harsh 更强调「意外冲突」

**whirring**
英 /ˈwɜːrɪŋ/ 美 /ˈwɜːrɪŋ/
adj. 嗡嗡旋转的
v. 呼呼转；嗡嗡响
[义] Making a continuous low sound like something spinning quickly. 快速旋转或机械运转的「呼呼」声（whirring fan 呼呼转的风扇；whirring blades 旋翼轰鸣）。
[例] ① The whirring of the projector filled the dark room. 暗室里投影仪呼呼作响。 ② Helicopter blades whirred overhead. 直升机桨叶在头顶呼呼转。
[搭] whirring fan/blades 呼呼转的风扇/桨叶, whirring sound 嗡嗡声, whir to life 呼呼启动
[联] hum 嗡嗡, buzz 嗡嗡, fan 风扇, machine 机器, spin 旋转, drone 轰鸣
[记] whir / whirr 动词变体。whirring 偏「旋转机械」

**whizzing**
英 /ˈwɪzɪŋ/ 美 /ˈwɪzɪŋ/
adj. 呼啸而过的
v. 嗖嗖掠过
[义] Moving very fast with a high whistling sound. 高速掠过时的「嗖嗖」声（bullet whizzing 子弹呼啸；car whizzed past 车嗖地开过）。
[例] ① A ball whizzed past his ear and hit the wall. 球嗖地擦耳而过，砸在墙上。 ② Cars were whizzing along the motorway in the rain. 雨中车辆在高速上嗖嗖飞驰。
[搭] whizz past/by 嗖地经过, whizz through the air 在空中呼啸, whizzing sound 嗖嗖声
[联] whoosh 呼, speed 速度, bullet 子弹, past 经过, fast 快, wind 风
[记] whizz 英式；美式常 whiz。whizzing vs. whooshing：whizzing 更尖、更快

**tinny**
英 /ˈtɪni/ 美 /ˈtɪni/
adj. 尖薄刺耳的
[义] Having a thin, metallic, cheap sound quality. 像廉价金属或小喇叭——尖、薄、空（tinny music 音质很差的音乐；tinny voice 尖细的嗓音）。常形容劣质扬声器、电话音质。
