[例] ① The song sounded tinny through the phone speaker. 通过手机扬声器，歌听起来又尖又薄。 ② He hated the tinny sound of the old radio. 他讨厌旧收音机那种廉价金属音。
[搭] tinny sound/music 尖薄音质, tinny voice 尖细嗓音, sound tinny 听起来刺耳单薄
[联] shrill 尖厉, cheap 廉价, speaker 扬声器, radio 收音机, metallic 金属感, thin 薄
[记] tinny 与 tiny（微小）拼写近义异。tinny 几乎总批评音质

**deadened**
英 /ˈdednd/ 美 /ˈdednd/
adj. 麻木的；迟钝的
[义] Made less sensitive or lively; dulled. 感觉变钝、反应变弱（deadened senses 麻木的感官；deadened sound 被压闷的声音）。dead 的过去分词作形容词；比 numb 更偏「被压制、失去锐度」。
[例] ① Painkillers left her with a deadened feeling in her fingers. 止痛药让她手指一阵麻木。 ② The carpet deadened footsteps in the hallway. 地毯让走廊脚步声变得闷沉。
[搭] deadened senses/sound 麻木感官/闷沉声, feel deadened 感觉迟钝, deadened response 反应迟钝
[联] numb 麻木, dull 迟钝, muted 闷声, senses 感官, painkiller 止痛药, muffled 压闷
[记] deaden 为动词「使麻木/压低」。deadened vs. numb：deadened 可修饰 sound

**oversensitive**
英 /ˌəʊvəˈsensətɪv/ 美 /ˌoʊvərˈsensətɪv/
adj. 过于敏感的
[义] Too easily upset or too strongly reactive to stimuli. 对刺激反应过强——可指皮肤（oversensitive skin 敏感肌）、耳朵、或情绪（oversensitive to criticism 对批评过敏）。hypersensitive 近义，略偏医学/生理。
[例] ① The lotion is for oversensitive skin around the eyes. 这款乳液适合眼周敏感肌。 ② He became oversensitive to noise after the head injury. 头部受伤后他对噪音变得异常敏感。
[搭] oversensitive skin 敏感肌, oversensitive to noise/criticism 对噪音/批评过敏, overly sensitive 过于敏感
[联] hypersensitive 过敏性的, sensitive 敏感, skin 皮肤, noise 噪音, reactive 反应强, irritate 刺激
[记] oversensitive 可带轻微批评（太玻璃心）。hypersensitive 更中性/医学

**monotonous**
英 /məˈnɒtənəs/ 美 /məˈnɑːtənəs/
adj. 单调乏味的
[义] Dull and repetitive, without change in pitch or quality. 音调或节奏毫无变化、听得发腻（monotonous voice 单调嗓音；monotonous hum 嗡嗡不止）。monotone 多作名词；monotonous 作形容词更常见。
[例] ① The lecturer's monotonous tone put half the class to sleep. 讲师单调的语调让半个班睡着了。 ② We listened to the monotonous drip of the tap all night. 我们整晚听着水龙头单调的滴答声。
[搭] monotonous voice/tone 单调嗓音/语调, monotonous work 枯燥重复的工作, monotonous sound 单调声响
[联] monotone 单调, boring 无聊, droning 嗡嗡, repetitive 重复, voice 嗓音, dull 乏味
[记] monotonous 可修饰 voice, job, landscape。monotonous vs. monotone：后者多名词

**blurry**
英 /ˈblɜːri/ 美 /ˈblɜːri/
adj. 模糊的
[义] Not clear or sharp to the eye; out of focus. 看不清楚、轮廓发虚（blurry photo 模糊照片；blurry vision 视线模糊）。比较章 blur 作动词/名词；属性章 blurred；本章取视觉感知形容词。
[例] ① Everything looked blurry when I took off my glasses. 一摘眼镜，什么都模模糊糊。 ② The security footage was too blurry to identify faces. 监控画面太糊，认不出脸。
[搭] blurry photo/vision 模糊照片/视力, blurry image 模糊图像, look blurry 看起来发虚
[联] blurred 模糊, fuzzy 朦胧, focus 对焦, vision 视力, photo 照片, unclear 不清楚
[记] blurry 几乎总视觉义。blurry-eyed 指「睡眼惺忪」

**pixelated**
英 /ˈpɪksəleɪtɪd/ 美 /ˈpɪksəleɪtɪd/
adj. 像素化的；马赛克般的
[义] Appearing as small square blocks, especially when an image is enlarged or low-resolution. 图像放大或分辨率低时呈方块马赛克（pixelated video 像素化视频；pixelated face 打码的脸）。数字时代常见视觉感知词。
[例] ① The zoomed-in image looked heavily pixelated. 放大后的图像马赛克很重。 ② His face was pixelated on TV to protect his identity. 电视上他的脸被打马赛克以保护身份。
[搭] pixelated image/video 像素化图像/视频, heavily pixelated 马赛克很重, pixelated face 打码的脸
[联] blurry 模糊, digital 数字, screen 屏幕, zoom 放大, block 方块, video 视频
[记] pixelate 动词；英式亦 pixellated。新闻打码常用 pixelated

**clink**
英 /klɪŋk/ 美 /klɪŋk/
v. 叮当碰撞
n. 叮当声
[义] To make a light ringing sound when glass or metal objects touch. 玻璃或金属轻碰的清脆叮当（glasses clinked 杯盏相碰；clink of coins 硬币叮当）。
[例] ① They clinked glasses and said "Cheers." 他们碰杯说「干杯」。 ② We heard the clink of keys in the lock. 我们听到钥匙在锁里叮当响。
[搭] clink glasses 碰杯, clink of coins/keys 硬币/钥匙叮当, clink together 叮当相碰
[联] clank 哐当, tinkle 叮当, glass 玻璃, coin 硬币, toast 祝酒, ring 响
[记] clink 比 clank 更轻、更脆。clink glasses 固定祝酒动作

**clank**
英 /klæŋk/ 美 /klæŋk/
v. 哐当响
n. 哐当声
[义] To make a loud, heavy metallic sound. 金属重物碰撞的「哐当」声（chains clanked 铁链哗啦；clank of machinery 机器哐当）。比 clink 更沉、更响。
[例] ① The old gate clanked shut behind us. 旧铁门在我们身后哐当关上。 ② We could hear the clank of pots from the kitchen. 厨房里传来锅盆哐当声。
[搭] clank shut 哐当关上, clank of chains/machinery 铁链/机器哐当, clank loudly 大声哐当
[联] clink 叮当, clang 铛, metal 金属, chain 链, machinery 机器, loud 响
[记] clank vs. clink：clank 沉、重、金属感强

**babbling**
英 /ˈbæblɪŋ/ 美 /ˈbæblɪŋ/
adj. 潺潺的；喋喋不休的
v. 潺潺而流；喋喋不休
[义] Making a continuous murmuring sound, like a stream; talking quickly and confusedly. 溪水等「潺潺」（babbling brook 潺潺小溪）；或人「喋喋不休、语无伦次」。本章两义皆取声响感知。
[例] ① A babbling brook ran past the cottage. 一条潺潺小溪从小屋旁流过。 ② She kept babbling about the accident in shock. 受惊后她不停语无伦次地说事故。
[搭] babbling brook/stream 潺潺溪/小河, babbling voice 喋喋不休, babble on 唠叨没完
[联] murmur 低语, brook 小溪, stream 溪流, chatter 唠叨, water 水, confused 混乱
[记] babbling brook 文学固定搭配。babble 婴儿学语亦称 babbling

**trilling**
英 /ˈtrɪlɪŋ/ 美 /ˈtrɪlɪŋ/
adj. 颤音的
v. 发颤音；啭鸣
[义] Sounding with a rapid alternation of pitch. 音调快速颤动（trilling birds 啭鸣的鸟；trilling phone 铃声颤音）。比 warbling 更强调「颤、抖」的音技。
[例] ① Trilling birds filled the garden at dawn. 黎明时园里满是颤音啭鸣的鸟。 ② She trilled her R's in the old recording. 旧录音里她把 R 发成颤音。
[搭] trilling birds 颤音鸣鸟, trill with laughter 颤声笑, trilling sound 颤音
[联] warbling 啭鸣, chirp 啁啾, bird 鸟, pitch 音高, phone 电话, sing 唱
[记] trill 作名词指「颤音记号/鸣叫」。西班牙语 rolled R 常描述为 trilling

**haunting**
英 /ˈhɔːntɪŋ/ 美 /ˈhɔːntɪŋ/
adj. 萦绕心头的；凄美的
[义] Beautiful or sad in a way that stays in your memory; eerie and persistent. 听/看后久久不忘、带点 haunting beauty（凄美）或 ghostly 感（haunting melody 萦绕旋律；haunting eyes 令人难忘的眼神）。
[例] ① The film's haunting soundtrack stayed with me for days. 电影那萦绕心头的配乐跟了我好几天。 ② There was a haunting sadness in her voice. 她声音里有一种挥之不去的哀愁。
[搭] haunting melody/music 萦绕旋律/音乐, haunting beauty 凄美, haunting memory 挥之不去的记忆
[联] eerie 阴森, memorable 难忘, melody 旋律, ghost 鬼, sad 悲伤, beautiful 美
[记] haunting 可褒（凄美）可中性（萦绕）。haunting vs. creepy：haunting 常带美感

**creepy**
英 /ˈkriːpi/ 美 /ˈkriːpi/
adj. 瘆人的
[义] Causing an unpleasant feeling of fear or disgust, often without a clear reason. 莫名发毛、起鸡皮疙瘩（creepy silence 瘆人寂静；creepy crawlies 令人发毛的虫子）。口语极频，比 scary 更「细思极恐、黏腻」。
[例] ① The empty playground at night felt creepy. 夜里空荡的游乐场让人发毛。 ② He told a creepy story about the old hospital. 他讲了个关于旧医院的瘆人故事。
[搭] creepy feeling/silence 瘆人感觉/寂静, creepy crawlies 吓人小虫, feel creepy 觉得瘆人
[联] spooky 阴森, eerie 诡异, scary 可怕, uncanny 怪异, crawlies 小虫, night 夜
[记] creepy 可形容 place, person, vibe。creepy vs. scary：creepy 更黏、更细

**spooky**
英 /ˈspuːki/ 美 /ˈspuːki/
adj. 阴森吓人的
[义] Strange and frightening in a ghostly way. 像闹鬼一样阴森（spooky house 鬼屋；spooky Halloween 阴森的万圣节）。比 creepy 更直白的「鬼气、节日恐怖」。
[例] ① Kids love the spooky decorations in the shop window. 孩子们喜欢橱窗里阴森的万圣节装饰。 ② A spooky wind moaned through the broken windows. 阴风从破窗呜咽灌入。
[搭] spooky house/atmosphere 鬼屋/阴森气氛, spooky story 鬼故事, feel spooky 觉得阴森
[联] creepy 瘆人, ghost 鬼, Halloween 万圣节, eerie 诡异, haunted 闹鬼, wind 风
[记] spooky 口语友好，儿童常用。spooky vs. creepy：spooky 更「鬼片/节日」

**nightmarish**
英 /ˈnaɪtmeərɪʃ/ 美 /ˈnaɪtmerɪʃ/
adj. 噩梦般的
[义] Extremely unpleasant, frightening, or chaotic, like a bad dream. 像噩梦一样可怕、混乱（nightmarish traffic 噩梦交通；nightmarish experience 噩梦经历）。整体感知「失真、逃不掉、压顶」。
[例] ① The journey turned into a nightmarish ordeal in the snowstorm. 行程在暴风雪中变成噩梦般的折磨。 ② She described a nightmarish scene of smoke and screaming. 她描述了一幅烟雾与尖叫的噩梦景象。
[搭] nightmarish experience/scene 噩梦经历/景象, nightmarish traffic 噩梦交通, turn nightmarish 变成噩梦
[联] nightmare 噩梦, surreal 超现实, chaotic 混乱, terrifying 可怕, dream 梦, ordeal 折磨
[记] nightmarish 几乎总负面。可修饰 journey, scene, vision, commute

**dulled**
英 /dʌld/ 美 /dʌld/
adj. 变钝的；减弱了的
[义] Made less sharp, bright, or intense. 锋刃、感官或声音变钝（dulled blade 钝刀；dulled senses 迟钝感官；dulled colours 褪色）。dull 的过去分词作形容词。
[例] ① Years of typing dulled the feeling in her fingertips. 多年打字让她指尖感觉变钝。 ② The rain dulled the colours of the street murals. 雨水让街头壁画颜色变暗变钝。
[搭] dulled senses/blade 迟钝感官/钝刃, dulled colours 变暗的颜色, dulled pain 痛感减轻
[联] dull 钝, deadened 麻木, blunt 钝, senses 感官, colour 颜色, fade 褪色
[记] dulled vs. deadened：dulled 偏「锐度/亮度降」；deadened 偏「几乎无感」

**puckering**
英 /ˈpʌkərɪŋ/ 美 /ˈpʌkərɪŋ/
adj. 涩到缩唇的
v. 缩唇；起皱
[义] Tightening the lips because of a sour or astringent taste. 因酸涩刺激而嘴唇缩拢（puckering lemon 涩人的柠檬；puckering sensation 涩感）。mouth-puckering 同义；tangy/zesty 偏「尖酸醒神」。
[例] ① The unripe persimmon left a puckering taste on my tongue. 涩柿子在舌头上留下缩唇的涩感。 ② She made a puckering face after tasting the vinegar. 尝了醋，她皱脸缩唇。
[搭] puckering taste/sensation 涩感, mouth-puckering 涩到缩唇, puckering lemon 涩柠檬
[联] sour 酸, tangy 尖酸, astringent 涩, lemon 柠檬, lips 唇, taste 味道
[记] pucker 动词「缩拢」。puckering 几乎总味觉/触觉唇感

**off-key**
英 /ˌɒf ˈkiː/ 美 /ˌɔːf ˈkiː/
adj./adv. 跑调的
[义] Not at the correct musical pitch. 唱歌或乐器音高不对（sing off-key 跑调；off-key note 走音）。听觉上「刺耳、不对、别扭」。
[例] ① Someone in the crowd was singing loudly and off-key. 人群里有人大声跑调地唱。 ② The piano sounded slightly off-key in the cold hall. 寒冷大厅里钢琴音略偏。
[搭] sing off-key 跑调唱, off-key note 走音, slightly off-key 略跑调
[联] out-of-tune 走调, pitch 音高, sing 唱, note 音符, wrong 不对, music 音乐
[记] off-key 可喻「不合时宜」（off-key remark）。复合词常作表语

**out-of-tune**
英 /ˌaʊt əv ˈtjuːn/ 美 /ˌaʊt əv ˈtuːn/
adj. 走音的；不协调的
[义] Not in the correct tune; not in harmony. 乐器或合唱「走音、不协调」（out-of-tune guitar 走音吉他）。比 off-key 更常形容乐器需调音。
[例] ① The out-of-tune violin made everyone wince. 走音的小提琴听得人龇牙。 ② His comments were completely out-of-tune with the mood. 他的话与当时气氛完全不合。
[搭] out-of-tune guitar/piano 走音吉他/钢琴, sound out-of-tune 听起来走音, out-of-tune with 与……不协调
[联] off-key 跑调, tune 调子, guitar 吉他, wince 龇牙, harmony 和谐, instrument 乐器
[记] out of tune 亦作短语。可喻观点与气氛不合

**echoing**
英 /ˈekəʊɪŋ/ 美 /ˈekoʊɪŋ/
adj. 回荡的
[义] Repeating and fading in a large empty space. 声音在空荡处回响（echoing footsteps 回荡脚步；echoing hall 回声大厅）。比较章 echo 作名/动；本章取听觉空间感。
[例] ① Our echoing footsteps rang through the empty station. 我们的脚步在空车站里回荡。 ② She spoke in a low echoing voice in the cave. 她在洞里低声说话，声音回荡。
[搭] echoing hall/footsteps 回声大厅/脚步, echoing voice 回荡嗓音, echoing sound 回响
[联] echo 回声, reverberate 回响, hall 大厅, cave 洞, empty 空, sound 声
[记] echoing 强调空间感。echo chamber 喻「信息茧房」，另义

**crackling**
英 /ˈkræklɪŋ/ 美 /ˈkræklɪŋ/
adj. 噼啪作响的
v. 噼啪作响
[义] Making short sharp repeated sounds, like fire or old records. 火焰、静电、旧唱片等「噼里啪啦」（crackling fire 噼啪炉火；crackling radio 杂音收音机）。动词 crackle 已有；crackling 强调持续感。
[例] ① We sat by the crackling fire and drank tea. 我们围坐噼啪炉火喝茶。 ② The old recording had a crackling background noise. 旧录音背景有噼啪杂音。
[搭] crackling fire 噼啪炉火, crackling sound/noise 噼啪声/杂音, crackling radio 沙沙响的收音机
[联] crackle 噼啪, fire 火, static 静电, radio 收音机, pop 啪, burn 燃烧
[记] crackling 英式亦指「脆皮烤肉」。crackling vs. crackle：前者偏持续

**yowling**
英 /ˈjaʊlɪŋ/ 美 /ˈjaʊlɪŋ/
adj. 嚎叫的
v. 长嚎；嚎叫
[义] Making a long loud cry, especially of a cat or person in pain or anger. 猫或人因痛怒发出的长嚎（yowling cat 嚎叫的猫；yowling in pain 痛得嚎）。动物章 howl 偏狼/风；yowl 偏猫。
[例] ① A yowling cat woke us at three in the morning. 凌晨三点，嚎叫的猫把我们吵醒。 ② He was yowling with pain after twisting his knee. 膝盖扭伤后他痛得直嚎。
[搭] yowling cat 嚎叫的猫, yowl in pain 痛嚎, yowling sound 嚎叫声
[联] howl 嚎, meow 喵, cat 猫, pain 痛, cry 叫, loud 响
[记] yowl 名词/动词。yowling vs. howling：yowl 更尖、更猫

**bawling**
英 /ˈbɔːlɪŋ/ 美 /ˈbɔːlɪŋ/
adj. 大声哭喊的
v. 大声哭喊；吼
[义] Crying or shouting loudly and roughly. 大声哭或吼（bawling baby 嚎哭婴儿；bawl at sb. 冲某人吼）。比 sob/weep 更响、更粗、更少克制。
[例] ① The bawling toddler refused to leave the toy shop. 嚎哭的幼儿不肯离开玩具店。 ② Fans were bawling their team's name in the rain. 球迷在雨里吼着队名。
[搭] bawling baby/child 嚎哭婴/童, bawl out 痛骂, bawl with rage 气吼
[联] cry 哭, sob 抽泣, shout 喊, baby 婴儿, loud 响, weep 泣
[记] bawl 亦可指「吼叫」非哭。bawling vs. sobbing：bawling 更公开、更响

**spine-tingling**
英 /ˈspaɪn ˌtɪŋɡlɪŋ/ 美 /ˈspaɪn ˌtɪŋɡlɪŋ/
adj. 令人脊背发麻的
[义] Causing excitement or fear that you feel physically along your spine. 因激动或恐惧而脊背发麻（spine-tingling moment 激动时刻；spine-tingling music 令人起鸡皮疙瘩的音乐）。复合形容词，常褒（thrill）或中性。
[例] ① The choir's final note was spine-tingling. 合唱团最后一个音令人脊背发麻。 ② It was a spine-tingling finish to the match. 比赛结尾令人激动到发麻。
[搭] spine-tingling moment/music 发麻时刻/音乐, spine-tingling finish 激动结局, spine-tingling excitement 激动
[联] goosebumps 鸡皮疙瘩, thrilling 激动, fear 恐惧, music 音乐, spine 脊背, tingle 刺麻
[记] 近义 hair-raising 更偏「怕」。spine-tingling 可褒可中性

**desensitized**
英 /diːˈsensətaɪzd/ 美 /diːˈsensətaɪzd/
adj. 感觉迟钝的
[义] Less sensitive to something because of repeated exposure. 因反复接触而不再敏感（desensitized to violence 对暴力麻木；desensitized skin 脱敏皮肤）。英式拼写 desensitised。
[例] ① Years of news footage had desensitized him to graphic images. 多年新闻画面让他对血腥图像麻木了。 ② The cream left my skin slightly desensitized. 药膏让我的皮肤略感脱敏。
[搭] desensitized to violence/noise 对暴力/噪音麻木, become desensitized 变得迟钝, desensitized skin 脱敏皮肤
[联] insensitive 迟钝, numb 麻木, exposure 暴露, violence 暴力, skin 皮肤, dull 变钝
[记] desensitize 动词。医学脱敏疗法同词根

**unresponsive**
英 /ˌʌnrɪˈspɒnsɪv/ 美 /ˌʌnrɪˈspɑːnsɪv/
adj. 无反应的
[义] Not reacting to stimuli, touch, or communication. 对刺激、触摸或沟通没有反应（unresponsive patient 无反应病人；unresponsive screen 没反应的屏幕）。responsive 的反义；本章取感知/反馈义。
[例] ① The patient remained unresponsive after the fall. 跌倒后病人一直无反应。 ② My phone went unresponsive in the cold. 天冷手机触屏没反应了。
[搭] unresponsive patient/screen 无反应病人/屏幕, remain unresponsive 仍无反应, unresponsive to treatment 治疗无反应
[联] responsive 有反应, numb 麻木, patient 病人, screen 屏幕, touch 触, stimulus 刺激
[记] unresponsive 医学与科技皆常用。勿与 irresponsible 混淆

**allergic**
英 /əˈlɜːdʒɪk/ 美 /əˈlɜːrdʒɪk/
adj. 过敏的
[义] Having a bad physical reaction to a substance. 接触某物后身体起反应——皮肤、呼吸、消化（allergic to nuts 对坚果过敏；allergic reaction 过敏反应）。本章取生理感知/反应义。
[例] ① She broke out in a rash—she's allergic to shellfish. 她起疹子了——她对贝类过敏。 ② I'm allergic to cats, so I can't stay long. 我对猫过敏，待不久。
[搭] allergic to nuts/cats 对坚果/猫过敏, allergic reaction 过敏反应, severely allergic 严重过敏
[联] allergy 过敏, rash 疹, sneeze 喷嚏, reaction 反应, food 食物, skin 皮肤
[记] allergic to 可喻「极其反感」（allergic to change）。生理义与比喻义依语境区分
